local FX = {};
do
	local RS = game:GetService("RunService");
	FX.Scale = 1;
	local body = Font.fromEnum(Enum.Font.SourceSans);
	local heavy = body;
	pcall(function()
		heavy = Font.new(body.Family, Enum.FontWeight.Bold, Enum.FontStyle.Normal);
	end);
	FX.Body, FX.Heavy = body, body;
	local SEQ = ColorSequence.new({ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(205, 205, 205)),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.34, Color3.fromRGB(205, 205, 205)),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.43, Color3.fromRGB(205, 255, 255)),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.57, Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 255)),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.66, Color3.fromRGB(205, 205, 205)),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(205, 205, 205))});
	FX.shimmer = function()
	end;
end
local function loadMain()
	local Players = game:GetService("Players");
	local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService");
	local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService");
	local RunService = game:GetService("RunService");
	local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting");
	local Stats = game:GetService("Stats");
	local Marketplace = game:GetService("MarketplaceService");
	local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService");
	local SoundService = game:GetService("SoundService");
	local player = Players.LocalPlayer;
	local PG = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui");
	local ROOT = "1MagHub";
	local ASSETS = ROOT .. "/Assets";
	local PROFILES = ROOT .. "/Profiles";
	local CONFIG = ROOT .. "/config.json";
	local LOGO_FILE = ASSETS .. "/logo.png";
	local function ensureFolder(path)
		if ((type(isfolder) ~= "function") or (type(makefolder) ~= "function")) then
			return;
		end
		local acc = "";
		for part in path:gmatch("[^/]+") do
			acc = ((acc == "") and part) or (acc .. "/" .. part);
			pcall(function()
				if not isfolder(acc) then
					makefolder(acc);
				end
			end);
		end
	end
	ensureFolder(ROOT);
	ensureFolder(ASSETS);
	ensureFolder(PROFILES);
	local LOGO_ID;
	local LOGO_TINT = false;
	local LOGO_URL = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/mrbeanissohot/Scyth1e-knife/main/file_00000000cc4881f4b97f937fb2cf7e3d.png";
	local LOGO_PATH = ASSETS .. "/1mag_logo_white.png";
	if ((type(getcustomasset) == "function") and (type(isfile) == "function") and (type(writefile) == "function")) then
		local function fetch()
			local data;
			pcall(function()
				data = game:HttpGet(LOGO_URL);
			end);
			if not data then
				pcall(function()
					local req = request or http_request or (syn and syn.request);
					local r = req and req({Url=LOGO_URL,Method="GET"});
					data = r and r.Body;
				end);
			end
			if ((type(data) == "string") and (#data > 500) and (data:sub(2, 4) == "PNG")) then
				return data;
			end
		end
		local have = false;
		pcall(function()
			have = isfile(LOGO_PATH) and (#readfile(LOGO_PATH) > 500);
		end);
		if not have then
			local d = fetch();
			if d then
				have = pcall(writefile, LOGO_PATH, d);
			end
		else
			task.spawn(function()
				local d = fetch();
				if d then
					pcall(writefile, LOGO_PATH, d);
				end
			end);
		end
		if have then
			pcall(function()
				LOGO_ID = getcustomasset(LOGO_PATH);
				LOGO_TINT = true;
			end);
		end
	end
	local HOVER_SFX = "rbxassetid://119354387183704";
	local CLICK_SFX = "rbxassetid://116080744084010";
	local CONFIG_VALUES = {};
	local LOADING = false;
	local ENABLED, arrayRefresh = {}, nil;
	local function flag(k)
		return CONFIG_VALUES[k];
	end
	local function playSfx(id, vol)
		if (LOADING or (CONFIG_VALUES["Settings.General.Interface.Sound FX"] == false)) then
			return;
		end
		local s = Instance.new("Sound");
		s.SoundId = id;
		s.Volume = vol or 0.3;
		s.PlayOnRemove = true;
		s.Parent = SoundService;
		s:Destroy();
	end
	local function hoverSfx()
		playSfx(HOVER_SFX, 0.18);
	end
	local function clickSfx()
		playSfx(CLICK_SFX, 0.32);
	end
	local T = {Accent=Color3.fromRGB(196, 26, 30),AccentHi=Color3.fromRGB(240, 62, 62),Bg=Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 13),Panel=Color3.fromRGB(16, 16, 20),PanelHi=Color3.fromRGB(23, 23, 28),Element=Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 34),ElementHi=Color3.fromRGB(42, 42, 50),Border=Color3.fromRGB(52, 52, 62),Text=Color3.fromRGB(244, 244, 248),Sub=Color3.fromRGB(165, 165, 176),Dim=Color3.fromRGB(100, 100, 112)};
	local F_BOLD, F_HEAVY = FX.Body, FX.Body;
	local function make(class, props, parent)
		local o = Instance.new(class);
		for k, v in pairs(props) do
			if ((k == "TextSize") and (type(v) == "number") and props.FontFace) then
				v = math.floor((v * FX.Scale) + 0.5);
			end
			o[k] = v;
		end
		if parent then
			o.Parent = parent;
		end
		return o;
	end
	local function corner(o, r)
		local sz = o.Size;
		if ((sz.X.Scale == 0) and (sz.Y.Scale == 0) and (sz.X.Offset > 0) and (sz.Y.Offset > 0) and ((r * 2) >= (math.min(sz.X.Offset, sz.Y.Offset) - 1))) then
			return make("UICorner", {CornerRadius=UDim.new(1, 0)}, o);
		end
		return make("UICorner", {CornerRadius=UDim.new(0, 3)}, o);
	end
	local function stroke(o, c, th, tr)
		return make("UIStroke", {Color=c,Thickness=(th or 1),Transparency=(tr or 0),ApplyStrokeMode=Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border}, o);
	end
	local function padding(o, t, r, b, l)
		return make("UIPadding", {PaddingTop=UDim.new(0, t or 0),PaddingRight=UDim.new(0, r or 0),PaddingBottom=UDim.new(0, b or 0),PaddingLeft=UDim.new(0, l or 0)}, o);
	end
	local DUMMY_TW = {Completed={Connect=function(_, f)
		task.defer(f);
		return {Disconnect=function()
		end};
	end,Wait=function()
		task.wait(0.1);
	end},Play=function()
	end,Cancel=function()
	end,Pause=function()
	end};
	local function tween(o, t, props, style, dir)
		if LOADING then
			for k, v in pairs(props) do
				pcall(function()
					o[k] = v;
				end);
			end
			return DUMMY_TW;
		end
		local tw = TweenService:Create(o, TweenInfo.new(t or 0.2, style or Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, dir or Enum.EasingDirection.Out), props);
		tw:Play();
		return tw;
	end
	local function ripple(parent, x, y)
		local sz = parent.AbsoluteSize;
		if ((sz.X < 1) or (sz.Y < 1)) then
			return;
		end
		local r = make("Frame", {BackgroundColor3=T.AccentHi,BackgroundTransparency=0.72,BorderSizePixel=0,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),SizeConstraint=Enum.SizeConstraint.RelativeYY,Position=UDim2.fromScale((x - parent.AbsolutePosition.X) / sz.X, (y - parent.AbsolutePosition.Y) / sz.Y),Size=UDim2.fromScale(0, 0),ZIndex=5}, parent);
		make("UICorner", {CornerRadius=UDim.new(1, 0)}, r);
		local tw = tween(r, 0.55, {Size=UDim2.fromScale(14, 14),BackgroundTransparency=1});
		tw.Completed:Connect(function()
			r:Destroy();
		end);
	end
	local function isPress(i)
		return (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1) or (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch);
	end
	local function isMove(i)
		return (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement) or (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch);
	end
	local conns = {};
	local function track(c)
		table.insert(conns, c);
		return c;
	end
	local accentHooks = {};
	local function onAccent(f)
		table.insert(accentHooks, f);
		pcall(f);
	end
	local function setAccent(c)
		T.Accent = c;
		T.AccentHi = c:Lerp(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.28);
		for _, f in ipairs(accentHooks) do
			pcall(f);
		end
	end
	local function logoImg(props, parent)
		props.Image = LOGO_ID;
		local img = make("ImageLabel", props, parent);
		if LOGO_TINT then
			onAccent(function()
				img.ImageColor3 = T.AccentHi;
			end);
		end
		return img;
	end
	local registry = {};
	local toggleKeys = {};
	local keybindRegistry = {};
	local resetters, markDirty, clearDirty = {}, function()
	end, function()
	end;
	local function encode()
		return HttpService:JSONEncode(CONFIG_VALUES);
	end
	local CFG_READY = false;
	local function saveConfig()
		if (type(writefile) ~= "function") then
			return false;
		end
		if not CFG_READY then
			return true;
		end
		local ok = pcall(function()
			writefile(CONFIG, encode());
		end);
		if ok then
			clearDirty();
		end
		return ok;
	end
	local function applyTable(d)
		for k, v in pairs(d) do
			CONFIG_VALUES[k] = v;
			if registry[k] then
				pcall(registry[k], v);
			end
		end
	end
	local function loadConfig()
		if ((type(isfile) ~= "function") or (type(readfile) ~= "function") or not isfile(CONFIG)) then
			return false;
		end
		local ok, d = pcall(function()
			return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(CONFIG));
		end);
		if (ok and (type(d) == "table")) then
			applyTable(d);
			return true;
		end
		return false;
	end
	local SAVED = {};
	do
		if ((type(isfile) == "function") and (type(readfile) == "function") and isfile(CONFIG)) then
			local ok, d = pcall(function()
				return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(CONFIG));
			end);
			if (ok and (type(d) == "table")) then
				SAVED = d;
			end
		end
		local AUTOLOAD = ROOT .. "/autoload.json";
		if ((type(isfile) == "function") and (type(readfile) == "function") and isfile(AUTOLOAD) and (SAVED["Configs.Config Profiles.Auto Load.Auto Load On Startup"] ~= false)) then
			local ok, a = pcall(function()
				return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(AUTOLOAD));
			end);
			if (ok and (type(a) == "table") and (type(a.profile) == "string")) then
				local path = PROFILES .. "/" .. a.profile .. ".json";
				if isfile(path) then
					local ok2, d = pcall(function()
						return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(path));
					end);
					if (ok2 and (type(d) == "table")) then
						for k, v in pairs(d) do
							SAVED[k] = v;
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
		for k in pairs(SAVED) do
			if ((type(k) == "string") and k:find("^Visual%.ESP%.")) then
				SAVED[k] = nil;
			end
		end
		for k, v in pairs(SAVED) do
			if ((type(k) == "string") and (k:find("^Cosmetics%.") or k:find("^Mobile%.pos%.") or (k == "World.CustomSkies"))) then
				CONFIG_VALUES[k] = v;
				SAVED[k] = nil;
			end
		end
	end
	for _, n in ipairs({"1MagHub","1MagLoading","1MagHUD"}) do
		local old = PG:FindFirstChild(n);
		if old then
			old:Destroy();
		end
	end
	local gui = make("ScreenGui", {Name="1MagHub",ResetOnSpawn=false,IgnoreGuiInset=true,ZIndexBehavior=Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,DisplayOrder=999}, PG);
	FX.shimmer(gui);
	local fpsValue = 60;
	do
		local frames, lastT = 0, tick();
		track(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
			frames += 1
			local now = tick();
			if ((now - lastT) >= 1) then
				fpsValue = frames;
				frames = 0;
				lastT = now;
			end
		end));
	end
	local function getPing()
		local ok, v = pcall(function()
			return Stats.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue();
		end);
		return (ok and math.floor(v)) or 0;
	end
	local gameName = "Unknown";
	pcall(function()
		gameName = Marketplace:GetProductInfo(game.PlaceId).Name;
	end);
	local notifHolder = make("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency=1,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(1, 1),Position=UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -16),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(270, 400),ZIndex=900}, gui);
	make("UIListLayout", {VerticalAlignment=Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom,HorizontalAlignment=Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right,Padding=UDim.new(0, 8),SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder}, notifHolder);
	local function notify(title, text, dur, kind)
		dur = dur or 3.5;
		local col = ((kind == "danger") and Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 80)) or ((kind == "ok") and Color3.fromRGB(80, 225, 120)) or T.AccentHi;
		local n = make("CanvasGroup", {BackgroundColor3=T.PanelHi,Size=UDim2.fromOffset(280, 66),GroupTransparency=1,ZIndex=900}, notifHolder);
		corner(n, 10);
		stroke(n, T.Border, 1, 0.2);
		make("Frame", {BackgroundColor3=col,Size=UDim2.new(0, 4, 1, 0),BorderSizePixel=0}, n);
		make("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Position=UDim2.fromOffset(16, 6),Size=UDim2.new(1, -22, 0, 18),Text=string.upper(title),FontFace=F_HEAVY,TextSize=16,TextColor3=col,TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left}, n);
		make("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Position=UDim2.fromOffset(16, 26),Size=UDim2.new(1, -22, 0, 32),Text=text,FontFace=F_BOLD,TextSize=17,TextColor3=T.Text,TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,TextYAlignment=Enum.TextYAlignment.Top,TextWrapped=true}, n);
		local bar = make("Frame", {BackgroundColor3=col,BackgroundTransparency=0.25,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0, 1),Position=UDim2.fromScale(0, 1),Size=UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 2),BorderSizePixel=0}, n);
		tween(n, 0.3, {GroupTransparency=0});
		tween(bar, dur, {Size=UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 2)}, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear);
		task.delay(dur, function()
			if n.Parent then
				tween(n, 0.25, {GroupTransparency=1}).Completed:Connect(function()
					n:Destroy();
				end);
			end
		end);
	end
	local keyOverlay = make("Frame", {Name="KeybindOverlay",BackgroundColor3=T.Panel,Position=UDim2.new(1, -18, 0, 18),AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(1, 0),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(210, 36),Visible=false,ZIndex=850}, gui);
	corner(keyOverlay, 8);
	stroke(keyOverlay, T.Border, 1, 0.15);
	local keyOverlayTitle = make("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Position=UDim2.fromOffset(10, 4),Size=UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 22),Text="ACTIVE KEYBINDS",FontFace=F_HEAVY,TextSize=15,TextColor3=T.AccentHi,TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,ZIndex=851}, keyOverlay);
	local keyOverlayBody = make("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Position=UDim2.fromOffset(10, 28),Size=UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 0),ZIndex=851}, keyOverlay);
	make("UIListLayout", {Padding=UDim.new(0, 3),SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder}, keyOverlayBody);
	local function refreshKeyOverlay()
		for _, child in ipairs(keyOverlayBody:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("TextLabel") then
				child:Destroy();
			end
		end
		local entries = {};
		local hubKey = tostring(flag("Settings.General.Binds.Toggle Hub") or "RightShift");
		table.insert(entries, {"Toggle Hub",hubKey});
		for k, entry in pairs(keybindRegistry) do
			local v = entry.get and entry.get();
			if (v and (tostring(v) ~= "") and (tostring(v) ~= "None")) then
				table.insert(entries, {entry.name,tostring(v)});
			end
		end
		table.sort(entries, function(a, b)
			return a[1] < b[1];
		end);
		local count = math.min(#entries, 12);
		for i = 1, count do
			local e = entries[i];
			make("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Size=UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18),Text=(e[1] .. "  ·  " .. e[2]),FontFace=F_BOLD,TextSize=14,TextColor3=T.Text,TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,TextTruncate=Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,LayoutOrder=i,ZIndex=852}, keyOverlayBody);
		end
		keyOverlayBody.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, count * 21);
		keyOverlay.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(210, 34 + (count * 21));
	end
	task.spawn(function()
		while gui.Parent do
			if keyOverlay.Visible then
				refreshKeyOverlay();
			end
			task.wait(1);
		end
	end);
	track(UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(i, gp)
		if (not gp and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard) and (i.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.RightAlt)) then
			keyOverlay.Visible = not keyOverlay.Visible;
			if keyOverlay.Visible then
				refreshKeyOverlay();
			end
		end
	end));
	local WIN_W, WIN_H = 780, 470;
	local TOP_H, SIDE_W, CARD_H, SQ = 42, 152, 48, 40;
	local SUB_H, DESC_H = 30, 18;
	local holder = make("Frame", {Name="Holder",BackgroundTransparency=1,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),Position=UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(WIN_W, WIN_H)}, gui);
	local holderScale = make("UIScale", {Scale=1}, holder);
	local UI_STATE = ROOT .. "/window_state.json";
	local function loadWindowState()
		if ((type(isfile) ~= "function") or (type(readfile) ~= "function")) then
			return;
		end
		local ok, d = pcall(function()
			if isfile(UI_STATE) then
				return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(UI_STATE));
			end
		end);
		if (ok and (type(d) == "table")) then
			local w = tonumber(d.w);
			local h = tonumber(d.h);
			if (w and h) then
				holder.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.clamp(w, 620, 1100), math.clamp(h, 380, 800));
			end
			if (tonumber(d.xs) and tonumber(d.ys) and tonumber(d.xo) and tonumber(d.yo)) then
				holder.Position = UDim2.new(d.xs, d.xo, d.ys, d.yo);
			end
		end
	end
	local function saveWindowState()
		if (type(writefile) ~= "function") then
			return;
		end
		local p, z = holder.Position, holder.Size;
		pcall(function()
			writefile(UI_STATE, HttpService:JSONEncode({w=z.X.Offset,h=z.Y.Offset,xs=p.X.Scale,ys=p.Y.Scale,xo=p.X.Offset,yo=p.Y.Offset}));
		end);
	end
	loadWindowState();
	local glow = make("Frame", {BackgroundColor3=T.Accent,BackgroundTransparency=1,BorderSizePixel=0,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),Position=UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(WIN_W + 14, WIN_H + 14),Visible=false}, holder);
	corner(glow, 20);
	onAccent(function()
		glow.BackgroundColor3 = T.Accent;
	end);
	local shadow = make("ImageLabel", {BackgroundTransparency=1,ImageColor3=Color3.new(0, 0, 0),ImageTransparency=1,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),Position=UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(WIN_W + 60, WIN_H + 60),Image="rbxasset://textures/ui/Glow.png",Visible=false}, holder);
	local win = make("CanvasGroup", {Name="Window",BackgroundColor3=T.Bg,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),Position=UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),Size=UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),GroupTransparency=1,Visible=false}, holder);
	make("UICorner", {CornerRadius=UDim.new(0, 4)}, win);
	local winStroke = stroke(win, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 1, 0.15);
	local winGrad = make("UIGradient", {}, winStroke);
	onAccent(function()
		winGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, T.Accent),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.35, T.Border),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.65, T.Border),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, T.AccentHi)});
		shadow.ImageColor3 = T.Accent;
	end);
	local winScale = make("UIScale", {Scale=0.88}, win);
	FX.Flame = {on=true,n=((UIS.TouchEnabled and 30) or 64)};
	FX.dim = make("Frame", {Name="Dim",BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(4, 4, 8),BackgroundTransparency=1,BorderSizePixel=0,Size=UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),ZIndex=0,Active=false}, gui);
	do
		local F = FX.Flame;
		local GLOW = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Glow.png";
		local overlay = make("Frame", {Name="Flame",BackgroundTransparency=1,Size=UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),ZIndex=0,Active=false}, gui);
		local halo = make("ImageLabel", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Image=GLOW,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),ImageTransparency=1,ZIndex=0,Visible=false}, overlay);
		local pool, active = {}, {};
		local function newSprite()
			return make("ImageLabel", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Image=GLOW,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),ImageTransparency=1,Visible=false,ZIndex=1}, overlay);
		end
		local rnd, WHITE = math.random, Color3.new(1, 1, 1);
		local ppx, ppy = nil, nil;
		local hvx, hvy = 0, 0;
		local last, acc, spawnAcc = os.clock(), 0, 0;
		local function spawnOne(px, py, pw, ph, sc)
			local d = rnd() * 2 * (pw + ph);
			local up = (60 + (rnd() * 90)) * sc;
			local x, y, vx, vy;
			if (d < pw) then
				x, y, vx, vy = px + d, py + 3, (rnd() - 0.5) * 40 * sc, -up * 1.15;
			elseif (d < (pw + ph)) then
				x, y, vx, vy = (px + pw) - 3, py + (d - pw), (18 + (rnd() * 40)) * sc, -up * 0.9;
			elseif (d < ((2 * pw) + ph)) then
				x, y, vx, vy = px + ((d - pw) - ph), (py + ph) - 3, (rnd() - 0.5) * 60 * sc, -up * 0.45;
			else
				x, y, vx, vy = px + 3, py + ((d - (2 * pw)) - ph), -(18 + (rnd() * 40)) * sc, -up * 0.9;
			end
			local k = rnd();
			local kind = ((k < 0.08) and "e") or ((k < 0.4) and "c") or "f";
			local life, size;
			if (kind == "e") then
				life, size = 0.9 + (rnd() * 0.7), (4 + (rnd() * 4)) * sc;
			elseif (kind == "c") then
				life, size = 0.5 + (rnd() * 0.4), (10 + (rnd() * 10)) * sc;
			else
				life, size = 0.8 + (rnd() * 0.7), (22 + (rnd() * 22)) * sc;
			end
			active[#active + 1] = {s=(table.remove(pool) or newSprite()),x=x,y=y,vx=vx,vy=vy,age=0,life=life,size=size,ph=(rnd() * 6.28),kind=kind,rot=0};
		end
		track(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
			local now = os.clock();
			acc += (now - last)
			last = now;
			if (acc < ((UIS.TouchEnabled and (1 / 30)) or (1 / 60))) then
				return;
			end
			local dt = math.min(acc, 0.06);
			acc = 0;
			local vis = F.on and win.Visible;
			local alpha = (vis and (1 - win.GroupTransparency)) or 0;
			if (not vis and (#active == 0)) then
				halo.Visible = false;
				ppx = nil;
				hvx, hvy = 0, 0;
				return;
			end
			local ap, asz, off = holder.AbsolutePosition, holder.AbsoluteSize, overlay.AbsolutePosition;
			local wdx, wdy = 0, 0;
			if ppx then
				wdx, wdy = math.clamp(ap.X - ppx, -60, 60), math.clamp(ap.Y - ppy, -60, 60);
				local k = math.min(1, dt * 9);
				hvx += ((math.clamp(wdx / dt, -2500, 2500) - hvx) * k)
				hvy += ((math.clamp(wdy / dt, -2500, 2500) - hvy) * k)
			else
				hvx, hvy = 0, 0;
			end
			ppx, ppy = ap.X, ap.Y;
			local speed = math.sqrt((hvx * hvx) + (hvy * hvy));
			local sc = holderScale.Scale;
			local aB, aA, bg = T.AccentHi, T.Accent, T.Bg;
			local hot = aB:Lerp(WHITE, 0.6);
			local fl = 0.5 + (0.25 * math.sin(now * 9.3)) + (0.15 * math.sin((now * 3.1) + 1)) + (0.1 * math.sin(now * 17));
			halo.Visible = alpha > 0.01;
			halo.Position = UDim2.fromOffset((ap.X + (asz.X / 2)) - off.X, (ap.Y + (asz.Y / 2)) - off.Y);
			halo.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(asz.X * 1.35, asz.Y * 1.45);
			halo.ImageColor3 = aA;
			halo.ImageTransparency = 1 - math.clamp((0.16 + (0.1 * fl) + math.min(speed / 4000, 0.1)) * alpha, 0, 1);
			if (vis and (alpha > 0.2)) then
				local want = math.clamp(F.n, 0, 140);
				local boost = 1 + (math.min(speed / 1200, 1) * 0.9);
				spawnAcc += ((dt * want * boost) / 0.95)
				local g = 0;
				while (spawnAcc >= 1) and (#active < (want * 1.7)) and (g < 10) do
					spawnAcc -= 1
					g += 1
					spawnOne(ap.X, ap.Y, asz.X, asz.Y, sc);
				end
				if (spawnAcc > 4) then
					spawnAcc = 0;
				end
			else
				spawnAcc = 0;
			end
			local i = 1;
			while i <= #active do
				local p = active[i];
				p.age += dt
				local t = p.age / p.life;
				if (t >= 1) then
					p.s.Visible = false;
					if (#pool < 160) then
						pool[#pool + 1] = p.s;
					else
						p.s:Destroy();
					end
					active[i] = active[#active];
					active[#active] = nil;
				else
					p.vy -= ((((p.kind == "e") and 30) or 55) * sc * dt)
					p.vx += (math.sin((now * 6) + p.ph + (p.y * 0.02)) * 60 * sc * dt)
					p.vx *= (1 - math.min(1, 1.6 * dt))
					p.vy *= (1 - math.min(1, 0.45 * dt))
					local follow = 0.55 * (1 - t);
					p.x += ((p.vx * dt) + (wdx * follow))
					p.y += ((p.vy * dt) + (wdy * follow))
					local target = math.clamp(math.deg(math.atan2(p.vx - (hvx * 0.5), -(p.vy - (hvy * 0.25)))), -40, 40);
					p.rot += ((target - p.rot) * math.min(1, dt * 10))
					local col, a, w, h, mul;
					if (p.kind == "f") then
						if (t < 0.2) then
							col = hot:Lerp(aB, t / 0.2);
						elseif (t < 0.6) then
							col = aB:Lerp(aA, (t - 0.2) / 0.4);
						else
							col = aA:Lerp(bg, math.min(1, ((t - 0.6) / 0.4) * 0.85));
						end
						w = p.size * (1 - (0.75 * t));
						h = w * 1.9;
						mul = 0.55;
					elseif (p.kind == "c") then
						col = hot:Lerp(aB, t);
						w = p.size * (1 - (0.8 * t));
						h = w * 1.5;
						mul = 0.8;
					else
						col = WHITE:Lerp(aB, 0.3 + (0.7 * t));
						w = p.size * (1 - (0.5 * t));
						h = w;
						mul = 0.95;
					end
					a = math.min(1, t / 0.12) * ((1 - t) ^ 1.4);
					local s = p.s;
					s.Visible = true;
					s.ImageColor3 = col;
					s.ImageTransparency = 1 - math.clamp(a * mul * alpha, 0, 1);
					s.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, h);
					s.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(p.x - off.X, p.y - off.Y);
					s.Rotation = p.rot;
					i += 1
				end
			end
		end));
	end
	for _, o in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
		if (o.Name == "1MagBlur") then
			o:Destroy();
		end
	end
	local blurFx = make("BlurEffect", {Name="1MagBlur",Size=0}, Lighting);
