	local mains, activeMain, activeSub = {}, nil, nil;
	local function badgeRefresh()
		for _, m in ipairs(mains) do
			if (m.badge and (m.name ~= "Settings") and (m.name ~= "Configs")) then
				local c, pre = 0, m.name .. ".";
				for k, v in pairs(CONFIG_VALUES) do
					if ((v == true) and (type(k) == "string") and (string.sub(k, 1, #pre) == pre)) then
						c += 1
					end
				end
				m.badge.Text = tostring(c);
				m.badge.Visible = c > 0;
			end
		end
	end
	local function filterPage(page)
		if not page then
			return;
		end
		local q = string.lower(searchBox.Text or "");
		local collapsed = {};
		for _, c in ipairs(page:GetChildren()) do
			if (c:GetAttribute("IsHeader") and c:GetAttribute("Col")) then
				collapsed[c:GetAttribute("SecId")] = true;
			end
		end
		for _, c in ipairs(page:GetChildren()) do
			if c:IsA("GuiObject") then
				local sid = c:GetAttribute("SecId");
				if c:GetAttribute("Hid") then
					c.Visible = false;
				elseif c:GetAttribute("IsHeader") then
					c.Visible = q == "";
				elseif c:GetAttribute("Sec") then
					c.Visible = (q == "") and not collapsed[sid];
				else
					local sx = c:GetAttribute("S");
					if sx then
						if (q == "") then
							c.Visible = not collapsed[sid];
						else
							c.Visible = string.find(sx, q, 1, true) ~= nil;
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
	do
		local raw = filterPage;
		function filterPage(page)
			raw(page);
			local q = string.lower(searchBox.Text or "");
			local any = false;
			if (page and (q ~= "")) then
				for _, c in ipairs(page:GetChildren()) do
					if (c:IsA("GuiObject") and c.Visible and c:GetAttribute("S")) then
						any = true;
						break;
					end
				end
			end
			emptyLbl.Visible = (page ~= nil) and (q ~= "") and not any;
			if emptyLbl.Visible then
				emptyLbl.Text = 'Nothing matches \"' .. searchBox.Text .. '\"';
			end
		end
	end
	searchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
		filterPage(activeSub and activeSub.page);
	end);
	local function selectSub(m, s)
		if (activeSub == s) then
			return;
		end
		if activeSub then
			activeSub.page.Visible = false;
			tween(activeSub.btn, 0.18, {TextColor3=T.Sub});
			tween(activeSub.line, 0.2, {Size=UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 2)});
			if activeSub.st then
				tween(activeSub.st, 0.2, {Transparency=1});
			end
			activeSub.spin:Cancel();
		end
		activeSub = s;
		m.lastSub = s;
		s.page.Visible = true;
		s.page.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0);
		filterPage(s.page);
		descLabel.Text = s.desc or "";
		crumb.Text = string.upper(m.name) .. "   >   " .. string.upper(s.name);
		tween(s.btn, 0.18, {TextColor3=T.Text});
		tween(s.line, 0.28, {Size=UDim2.new(1, -14, 0, 2)}, Enum.EasingStyle.Back);
		if s.st then
			tween(s.st, 0.25, {Transparency=0.1});
		end
		s.spin:Play();
		s.page.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 0);
		tween(s.page, 0.25, {Position=UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)});
	end
	local function selectMain(m)
		if (activeMain == m) then
			return;
		end
		if activeMain then
			activeMain.bar.scroll.Visible = false;
			tween(activeMain.item, 0.18, {BackgroundTransparency=1});
			tween(activeMain.itemLbl, 0.18, {TextColor3=T.Sub});
			tween(activeMain.itemBar, 0.18, {BackgroundTransparency=1,Size=UDim2.fromOffset(3, 6)});
			tween(activeMain.itemSt, 0.2, {Transparency=1});
			activeMain.itemSpin:Cancel();
			if activeSub then
				activeSub.page.Visible = false;
				activeSub = nil;
			end
		end
		activeMain = m;
		m.bar.scroll.Visible = true;
		tween(m.item, 0.18, {BackgroundTransparency=0.8});
		tween(m.itemLbl, 0.18, {TextColor3=T.Text});
		tween(m.itemBar, 0.24, {BackgroundTransparency=0,Size=UDim2.fromOffset(3, 14)}, Enum.EasingStyle.Back);
		tween(m.itemSt, 0.25, {Transparency=0.1});
		m.itemSpin:Play();
		if m.subs[1] then
			selectSub(m, m.lastSub or m.subs[1]);
		end
	end
	FX.orb = function(parent, z)
		local WHITE = Color3.new(1, 1, 1);
		local o = {};
		o.glow = make("ImageLabel", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Image="rbxasset://textures/ui/Glow.png",ImageColor3=T.Accent,ImageTransparency=1,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),Position=UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(46, 46),ZIndex=z}, parent);
		o.rim = make("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency=1,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),Position=UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(24, 24),ZIndex=(z + 1)}, parent);
		make("UICorner", {CornerRadius=UDim.new(1, 0)}, o.rim);
		o.rimSt = stroke(o.rim, Color3.fromRGB(104, 104, 116), 1, 0.6);
		o.orbit = make("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency=1,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),Position=UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(24, 24),ZIndex=(z + 2)}, parent);
		make("UICorner", {CornerRadius=UDim.new(1, 0)}, o.orbit);
		o.orbitSt = stroke(o.orbit, T.AccentHi, 2, 0.1);
		o.orbitGrad = make("UIGradient", {Name="NoTheme",Transparency=NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.25, 0.15),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.45, 1),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.55, 1),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.75, 0.15),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0)})}, o.orbitSt);
		o.body = make("Frame", {BackgroundColor3=T.Accent,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),Position=UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14),BorderSizePixel=0,ZIndex=(z + 3)}, parent);
		make("UICorner", {CornerRadius=UDim.new(1, 0)}, o.body);
		o.bodySt = stroke(o.body, T.AccentHi, 1.5, 0);
		o.shine = make("Frame", {BackgroundColor3=WHITE,BackgroundTransparency=0.45,BorderSizePixel=0,Position=UDim2.fromOffset(3, 2),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(5, 3),ZIndex=(z + 4)}, o.body);
		make("UICorner", {CornerRadius=UDim.new(1, 0)}, o.shine);
		o.core = make("Frame", {BackgroundColor3=T.AccentHi:Lerp(WHITE, 0.55),AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),Position=UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(6, 6),BorderSizePixel=0,ZIndex=(z + 4)}, parent);
		make("UICorner", {CornerRadius=UDim.new(1, 0)}, o.core);
		return o;
	end;
	FX.headLbl = function(head, txt)
		local v = make("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Position=UDim2.fromOffset(8, 0),Size=UDim2.new(1, -26, 1, 0),Text=txt,FontFace=F_HEAVY,TextSize=17,TextColor3=T.Text,TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,TextTruncate=Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,ZIndex=6}, head);
		make("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency=1,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(1, 0.5),Position=UDim2.new(1, -8, 0.5, 0),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(12, 16),Text="v",FontFace=F_HEAVY,TextSize=15,TextColor3=T.Dim,ZIndex=6}, head);
		return v;
	end;
	FX.outline = function(obj)
		local st = stroke(obj, T.AccentHi, 1.5, 1);
		onAccent(function()
			st.Color = T.AccentHi;
		end);
		local spin = {Play=function()
		end,Cancel=function()
		end};
		return st, spin;
	end;
	local function addMain(name, desc)
		local m = {name=name,desc=desc,subs={}};
		local item = make("TextButton", {BackgroundColor3=T.Accent,BackgroundTransparency=1,Size=UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28),Text="",AutoButtonColor=false,LayoutOrder=(sideOrder + 1),ZIndex=3}, ddList);
		sideOrder += 1
		corner(item, 9);
		local itemBar = make("Frame", {BackgroundColor3=T.Accent,BackgroundTransparency=1,Size=UDim2.fromOffset(3, 6),AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0, 0.5),Position=UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, 0),BorderSizePixel=0,ZIndex=4}, item);
		corner(itemBar, 2);
		local itemLbl = make("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Position=UDim2.fromOffset(18, 0),Size=UDim2.new(1, -22, 1, 0),Text=name,FontFace=F_BOLD,TextSize=20,TextColor3=T.Sub,TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,ZIndex=4}, item);
		m.item, m.itemBar, m.itemLbl = item, itemBar, itemLbl;
		m.itemSt, m.itemSpin = FX.outline(item);
		local badge = make("TextLabel", {BackgroundColor3=T.Accent,BackgroundTransparency=0.3,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(1, 0.5),Position=UDim2.new(1, -8, 0.5, 0),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(22, 16),Text="",FontFace=F_HEAVY,TextSize=14,TextColor3=Color3.new(1, 1, 1),Visible=false,ZIndex=4}, item);
		corner(badge, 8);
		m.badge = badge;
		onAccent(function()
			badge.BackgroundColor3 = T.Accent;
		end);
		onAccent(function()
			item.BackgroundColor3 = T.Accent;
			itemBar.BackgroundColor3 = T.Accent;
		end);
		local scroll = make("ScrollingFrame", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Position=UDim2.fromOffset(10, 4),Size=UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, SUB_H - 2),Visible=false,BorderSizePixel=0,ScrollBarThickness=0,ScrollingDirection=Enum.ScrollingDirection.X,CanvasSize=UDim2.new(),AutomaticCanvasSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.X,ZIndex=3}, subBarHolder);
		make("UIListLayout", {FillDirection=Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,Padding=UDim.new(0, 4),SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,VerticalAlignment=Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center}, scroll);
		m.bar = {scroll=scroll};
		item.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
			if (activeMain ~= m) then
				hoverSfx();
				tween(item, 0.15, {BackgroundTransparency=0.9});
				tween(itemLbl, 0.15, {TextColor3=T.Text});
			end
		end);
		item.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
			if (activeMain ~= m) then
				tween(item, 0.15, {BackgroundTransparency=1});
				tween(itemLbl, 0.15, {TextColor3=T.Sub});
			end
		end);
		item.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			if (activeMain ~= m) then
				clickSfx();
				selectMain(m);
			end
		end);
		table.insert(mains, m);
		ddFullH += (28 + 3)
		ddList.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, ddFullH);
		return m;
	end
	local function addSub(m, name, desc)
		local s = {name=name,desc=desc,main=m};
		local btn = make("TextButton", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Size=UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0),AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.X,Text=name,FontFace=F_BOLD,TextSize=19,TextColor3=T.Sub,AutoButtonColor=false,LayoutOrder=(#m.subs + 1),ZIndex=4}, m.bar.scroll);
		padding(btn, 0, 10, 0, 10);
		local line = make("Frame", {BackgroundColor3=T.Accent,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5, 1),Position=UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0),Size=UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 2),BorderSizePixel=0,ZIndex=5}, btn);
		corner(line, 2);
		onAccent(function()
			line.BackgroundColor3 = T.Accent;
		end);
		s.btn, s.line = btn, line;
		make("UICorner", {CornerRadius=UDim.new(0, 6)}, btn);
		s.st, s.spin = nil, {Play=function()
		end,Cancel=function()
		end};
		local page = make("ScrollingFrame", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Size=UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),Visible=false,BorderSizePixel=0,ScrollBarThickness=3,ScrollBarImageColor3=T.Accent,CanvasSize=UDim2.new(),AutomaticCanvasSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,ZIndex=2}, pagesHolder);
		padding(page, 6, 12, 12, 12);
		make("UIListLayout", {Padding=UDim.new(0, 4),SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder}, page);
		onAccent(function()
			page.ScrollBarImageColor3 = T.Accent;
		end);
		s.page = page;
		table.insert(m.subs, s);
		btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
			if (activeSub ~= s) then
				hoverSfx();
				tween(btn, 0.15, {TextColor3=T.Text});
			end
		end);
		btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
			if (activeSub ~= s) then
				tween(btn, 0.15, {TextColor3=T.Sub});
			end
		end);
		btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			if (activeSub ~= s) then
				clickSfx();
				selectSub(m, s);
			end
		end);
		local API, n, curSec, curSecId = {}, 0, "Main", 0;
		local prefix = m.name .. "." .. name .. ".";
		local function keyOf(text)
			return prefix .. curSec .. "." .. text;
		end
		local function rowBase(h, text)
			n += 1
			local f = make("Frame", {BackgroundColor3=T.Element,Size=UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, h),BorderSizePixel=0,LayoutOrder=n,ZIndex=3}, page);
			corner(f, 9);
			stroke(f, T.Border, 1, 0.55);
			local eh = math.max(8, math.min(h, 36) - 12);
			local edge = make("Frame", {Name="Edge",BackgroundColor3=T.Accent,BackgroundTransparency=1,BorderSizePixel=0,Position=UDim2.fromOffset(0, (math.min(h, 36) - eh) / 2),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(2, eh),ZIndex=5}, f);
			onAccent(function()
				if not edge:GetAttribute("Fixed") then
					edge.BackgroundColor3 = T.Accent;
				end
			end);
			if text then
				f:SetAttribute("S", string.lower(text));
			end
			f:SetAttribute("SecId", curSecId);
			return f;
		end
		local function rowLabel(parent, text, w)
			return make("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Position=UDim2.fromOffset(12, 0),Size=UDim2.new(1, w or -24, 1, 0),Text=text,FontFace=F_BOLD,TextSize=18,TextColor3=T.Text,TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,TextTruncate=Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,ZIndex=4}, parent);
		end
		local function hoverRow(f, hit)
			local edge = f:FindFirstChild("Edge");
			hit.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
				hoverSfx();
				tween(f, 0.15, {BackgroundColor3=T.ElementHi});
				if (edge and not f:GetAttribute("On")) then
					tween(edge, 0.15, {BackgroundTransparency=0.5});
				end
			end);
			hit.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
				tween(f, 0.15, {BackgroundColor3=T.Element});
				if (edge and not f:GetAttribute("On")) then
					tween(edge, 0.15, {BackgroundTransparency=1});
				end
			end);
		end
		API.Section = function(text)
			curSec = text;
			n += 1
			curSecId = n;
			local f = make("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Size=UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36),LayoutOrder=n,ZIndex=3}, page);
			f:SetAttribute("Sec", true);
			f:SetAttribute("IsHeader", true);
			f:SetAttribute("SecId", n);
			f:SetAttribute("Col", false);
			local rule = make("Frame", {BackgroundColor3=T.Accent,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0, 1),Position=UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -2),Size=UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1),BorderSizePixel=0,ZIndex=4}, f);
			make("UIGradient", {Transparency=NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.1),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.55, 0.7),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})}, rule);
			local dia = make("Frame", {BackgroundColor3=T.Accent,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),Position=UDim2.new(0, 7, 0, 18),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8),BorderSizePixel=0,ZIndex=4}, f);
			make("UICorner", {CornerRadius=UDim.new(1, 0)}, dia);
			onAccent(function()
				rule.BackgroundColor3 = T.Accent;
				dia.BackgroundColor3 = T.Accent;
			end);
			local lbl = make("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Position=UDim2.fromOffset(20, 6),Size=UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 24),Text=string.upper(text),FontFace=F_HEAVY,TextSize=18,TextColor3=T.Text,TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,ZIndex=4}, f);
			local chev = make("TextLabel", {Name="Chev",BackgroundTransparency=1,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(1, 0.5),Position=UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 18),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18),Text="v",FontFace=F_HEAVY,TextSize=17,TextColor3=T.Dim,ZIndex=4}, f);
			local hit = make("TextButton", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Size=UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),Text="",ZIndex=6}, f);
			hit.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
				tween(chev, 0.12, {TextColor3=T.AccentHi});
				tween(lbl, 0.12, {TextColor3=T.AccentHi});
			end);
			hit.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
				tween(chev, 0.12, {TextColor3=T.Dim});
				tween(lbl, 0.12, {TextColor3=T.Text});
			end);
			hit.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				clickSfx();
				local c = not f:GetAttribute("Col");
				f:SetAttribute("Col", c);
				tween(chev, 0.22, {Rotation=((c and -90) or 0)}, Enum.EasingStyle.Back);
				filterPage(page);
			end);
			hit.MouseButton2Click:Connect(function()
				clickSfx();
				local anyOpen = false;
				for _, c in ipairs(page:GetChildren()) do
					if (c:GetAttribute("IsHeader") and not c:GetAttribute("Col")) then
						anyOpen = true;
						break;
					end
				end
				for _, c in ipairs(page:GetChildren()) do
					if c:GetAttribute("IsHeader") then
						c:SetAttribute("Col", anyOpen);
						local ch = c:FindFirstChild("Chev");
						if ch then
							tween(ch, 0.22, {Rotation=((anyOpen and -90) or 0)}, Enum.EasingStyle.Back);
						end
					end
				end
				filterPage(page);
			end);
		end;
		API.Hint = function(text)
			n += 1
			local f = make("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Size=UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22),LayoutOrder=n,ZIndex=3}, page);
			f:SetAttribute("Sec", true);
			f:SetAttribute("SecId", curSecId);
			make("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Position=UDim2.fromOffset(14, 0),Size=UDim2.new(1, -14, 1, 0),Text=text,FontFace=F_BOLD,TextSize=15,TextColor3=T.Sub,TextTransparency=0.25,TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,TextTruncate=Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,ZIndex=4}, f);
			return f;
		end;
		API.Label = function(text)
			local f = rowBase(28, text);
			local l = rowLabel(f, text);
			l.TextColor3 = T.Sub;
			l.TextSize = math.floor((17 * FX.Scale) + 0.5);
			return {Set=function(_, t)
				l.Text = t;
			end};
		end;
		API.Toggle = function(text, default, cb, hide)
			local key = keyOf(text);
			local def = (default and true) or false;
			if (CONFIG_VALUES[key] ~= nil) then
				default = CONFIG_VALUES[key];
			end
			local f = rowBase(32, text);
			f.ClipsDescendants = true;
			local lbl = rowLabel(f, text, -58);
			local WHITE = Color3.new(1, 1, 1);
			local OFF, OFFLINE = Color3.fromRGB(38, 38, 48), Color3.fromRGB(104, 104, 116);
			local wash = make("Frame", {BackgroundColor3=T.Accent,BackgroundTransparency=1,BorderSizePixel=0,Size=UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),ZIndex=3}, f);
			make("UIGradient", {Name="NoTheme",Transparency=NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.45, 0.97),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.8)})}, wash);
			local gem = make("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency=1,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),Position=UDim2.new(1, -24, 0.5, 0),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(30, 30),ZIndex=4}, f);
			local gScale = make("UIScale", {Scale=1}, gem);
			local orbScale = make("UIScale", {Scale=0.72}, gem);
			local o = FX.orb(gem, 4);
			o.body.BackgroundColor3 = OFF;
			o.bodySt.Color = OFFLINE;
			o.core.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0);
			o.orbitSt.Transparency = 1;
			o.shine.BackgroundTransparency = 0.8;
			local spin = TweenService:Create(o.orbitGrad, TweenInfo.new(2.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.Out, -1), {Rotation=360});
			local breathe = TweenService:Create(o.glow, TweenInfo.new(1.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, true), {ImageTransparency=0.86});
			local edge = f:FindFirstChild("Edge");
			local state = (default and true) or false;
			local hovering = false;
			local function paint(on)
				tween(o.body, 0.2, {BackgroundColor3=((on and T.Accent) or OFF)});
				tween(o.bodySt, 0.2, {Color=((on and T.AccentHi) or OFFLINE)});
				tween(o.core, (on and 0.3) or 0.18, {Size=((on and UDim2.fromOffset(6, 6)) or UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0))}, (on and Enum.EasingStyle.Back) or Enum.EasingStyle.Quint);
				tween(o.shine, 0.2, {BackgroundTransparency=((on and 0.45) or 0.8)});
				tween(o.orbitSt, 0.25, {Transparency=((on and 0.1) or (hovering and 0.6) or 1)});
				tween(wash, 0.25, {BackgroundTransparency=((on and 0) or 1)});
				tween(lbl, 0.18, {TextColor3=((on and T.Text) or T.Sub)});
				f:SetAttribute("On", on);
				if edge then
					tween(edge, 0.2, {BackgroundTransparency=((on and 0) or 1)});
				end
				breathe:Cancel();
				if on then
					tween(o.glow, 0.25, {ImageTransparency=0.68});
					spin:Play();
					task.delay(0.3, function()
						if state then
							breathe:Play();
						end
					end);
				else
					spin:Cancel();
					tween(o.glow, 0.2, {ImageTransparency=1});
				end
			end
			local function burst()
				for i = 0, 1 do
					task.delay(i * 0.09, function()
						local ring = make("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency=1,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),Position=UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14),ZIndex=8}, gem);
						make("UICorner", {CornerRadius=UDim.new(1, 0)}, ring);
						local rs = stroke(ring, T.AccentHi, 2 - (i * 0.5), 0.1);
						tween(ring, 0.55, {Size=UDim2.fromOffset(38 + (i * 14), 38 + (i * 14))});
						tween(rs, 0.55, {Transparency=1}).Completed:Connect(function()
							ring:Destroy();
						end);
					end);
				end
				for i = 1, 10 do
					local ang = ((i / 10) * math.pi * 2) + (math.random() * 0.4);
					local d = 14 + (math.random() * 10);
					local sz = math.random(2, 4);
					local sp = make("Frame", {BackgroundColor3=T.AccentHi:Lerp(WHITE, math.random() * 0.6),BorderSizePixel=0,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),Position=UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(sz, sz),ZIndex=9}, gem);
					make("UICorner", {CornerRadius=UDim.new(1, 0)}, sp);
					tween(sp, 0.5 + (math.random() * 0.2), {Position=UDim2.new(0.5, math.cos(ang) * d * 1.5, 0.5, math.sin(ang) * d * 0.8),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),BackgroundTransparency=1}, Enum.EasingStyle.Cubic);
					task.delay(0.8, function()
						sp:Destroy();
					end);
				end
			end
			local function set(v, silent)
				state = (v and true) or false;
				CONFIG_VALUES[key] = state;
				if (not hide and not (key:find("^Settings%.") or key:find("^Configs%."))) then
					ENABLED[key] = (state and text) or nil;
					if (arrayRefresh and not LOADING) then
						arrayRefresh();
					end
				end
				paint(state);
				if (state and not silent and not LOADING) then
					burst();
				end
				if (badgeRefresh and not LOADING) then
					badgeRefresh();
				end
				if (cb and not silent) then
					task.spawn(cb, state);
				end
			end
			local hit = make("TextButton", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Size=UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),Text="",ZIndex=6}, f);
			hoverRow(f, hit);
			hit.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
				hovering = true;
				tween(gScale, 0.2, {Scale=1.14}, Enum.EasingStyle.Back);
				if not state then
					tween(o.orbitSt, 0.2, {Transparency=0.6});
				end
			end);
			hit.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
				hovering = false;
				tween(gScale, 0.2, {Scale=1});
				if not state then
					tween(o.orbitSt, 0.2, {Transparency=1});
				end
			end);
			hit.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				clickSfx();
				markDirty();
				local m = UIS:GetMouseLocation();
				ripple(f, m.X, m.Y - 36);
				tween(gScale, 0.07, {Scale=0.7}).Completed:Connect(function()
					tween(gScale, 0.35, {Scale=((hovering and 1.14) or 1)}, Enum.EasingStyle.Back);
				end);
				set(not state);
			end);
			hit.MouseButton2Click:Connect(function()
				clickSfx();
				markDirty();
				set(def);
			end);
			set(state, true);
			onAccent(function()
				if state then
					o.body.BackgroundColor3 = T.Accent;
					o.bodySt.Color = T.AccentHi;
				end
				o.core.BackgroundColor3 = T.AccentHi:Lerp(WHITE, 0.55);
				o.orbitSt.Color = T.AccentHi;
				o.glow.ImageColor3 = T.Accent;
				wash.BackgroundColor3 = T.Accent;
			end);
			registry[key] = function(v)
				set(v);
			end;
			resetters[key] = function()
				set(def);
			end;
			toggleKeys[key] = true;
			if (cb and state) then
				task.spawn(cb, state);
			end
			return {Set=function(_, v)
				set(v);
			end};
		end;
		API.Slider = function(text, min, max, default, cb, step)
			local key = keyOf(text);
			step = step or 1;
			local def = default;
			if (CONFIG_VALUES[key] ~= nil) then
				default = CONFIG_VALUES[key];
			end
			local f = rowBase(48, text);
			local lb = rowLabel(f, text, -100);
			lb.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 6);
			lb.Size = UDim2.new(1, -100, 0, 20);
			local val = make("TextBox", {BackgroundColor3=T.PanelHi,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(1, 0),Position=UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 5),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(64, 22),FontFace=F_HEAVY,TextSize=17,TextColor3=T.AccentHi,Text="",ClearTextOnFocus=false,TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,ZIndex=5}, f);
			corner(val, 4);
			stroke(val, T.Border, 1, 0.5);
			local bar = make("Frame", {Position=UDim2.new(0, 12, 1, -16),Size=UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 6),BackgroundColor3=T.Border,BorderSizePixel=0,ZIndex=4}, f);
			corner(bar, 3);
			local fill = make("Frame", {Size=UDim2.fromScale(0, 1),BackgroundColor3=Color3.new(1, 1, 1),BorderSizePixel=0,ZIndex=5}, bar);
			corner(fill, 3);
			local fillGrad = make("UIGradient", {}, fill);
			fill.ClipsDescendants = true;
			local shine = make("Frame", {BackgroundColor3=Color3.new(1, 1, 1),BorderSizePixel=0,Visible=false,Size=UDim2.new(0, 36, 1, 0),Position=UDim2.new(0, -36, 0, 0),ZIndex=6}, fill);
			make("UIGradient", {Name="NoTheme",Transparency=NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0.4),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})}, shine);
			local function sweepFill()
				shine.Position = UDim2.new(0, -36, 0, 0);
				shine.Visible = true;
				tween(shine, 0.55, {Position=UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)}, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad).Completed:Connect(function()
					shine.Visible = false;
				end);
			end
			local knob = make("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency=1,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),Position=UDim2.fromScale(0, 0.5),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18),ZIndex=6}, bar);
			local orbScale = make("UIScale", {Scale=0.72}, knob);
			local kScale = make("UIScale", {Scale=1}, knob);
			local ko = FX.orb(knob, 6);
			ko.rim.Visible = false;
			ko.orbitSt.Transparency = 0.2;
			ko.glow.ImageTransparency = 0.72;
			ko.core.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(5, 5);
			local kspin = TweenService:Create(ko.orbitGrad, TweenInfo.new(2, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.Out, -1), {Rotation=360});
			onAccent(function()
				fillGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new(T.Accent, T.AccentHi);
				val.TextColor3 = T.AccentHi;
				ko.body.BackgroundColor3 = T.Accent;
				ko.bodySt.Color = T.AccentHi;
				ko.orbitSt.Color = T.AccentHi;
				ko.glow.ImageColor3 = T.Accent;
				ko.core.BackgroundColor3 = T.AccentHi:Lerp(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.55);
			end);
			local function setValue(v, silent)
				v = math.clamp(math.floor((v / step) + 0.5) * step, min, max);
				v = tonumber(string.format("%.4f", v));
				local a = (v - min) / math.max(1e-9, max - min);
				val.Text = string.format("%g", v);
				fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(a, 1);
				knob.Position = UDim2.fromScale(a, 0.5);
				CONFIG_VALUES[key] = v;
				if (cb and not silent) then
					task.spawn(cb, v);
				end
			end
			setValue(default, true);
			if cb then
				task.spawn(cb, CONFIG_VALUES[key]);
			end
			val.FocusLost:Connect(function()
				local typed = tonumber(val.Text);
				if typed then
					setValue(typed);
					markDirty();
				else
					setValue(CONFIG_VALUES[key], true);
				end
			end);
			local dragging, hovering, lastTrail = false, false, 0;
			local function grow()
				tween(kScale, 0.18, {Scale=((dragging and 1.3) or (hovering and 1.15) or 1)}, Enum.EasingStyle.Back);
				tween(ko.glow, 0.2, {ImageTransparency=((dragging and 0.38) or (hovering and 0.6) or 0.72)});
				if (dragging or hovering) then
					kspin:Play();
				else
					kspin:Cancel();
				end
			end
			local function trail()
				local now = os.clock();
				if (LOADING or ((now - lastTrail) < 0.03)) then
					return;
				end
				lastTrail = now;
				local x = knob.Position.X.Scale;
				local s = math.random(4, 8);
				local dot = make("Frame", {BackgroundColor3=T.AccentHi:Lerp(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), math.random() * 0.5),BackgroundTransparency=0.3,BorderSizePixel=0,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),Position=UDim2.new(x, 0, 0.5, 0),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(s, s),ZIndex=5}, bar);
				make("UICorner", {CornerRadius=UDim.new(1, 0)}, dot);
				tween(dot, 0.5, {Position=UDim2.new(x, math.random(-8, 8), 0.5, math.random(-12, -4)),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),BackgroundTransparency=1}).Completed:Connect(function()
					dot:Destroy();
				end);
			end
			local function update(i)
				local rel = (i.Position.X - bar.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(1, bar.AbsoluteSize.X);
				setValue(min + ((max - min) * math.clamp(rel, 0, 1)));
				markDirty();
				trail();
			end
			f.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
				if isPress(i) then
					local p, ap, as = i.Position, val.AbsolutePosition, val.AbsoluteSize;
					if ((p.X >= ap.X) and (p.X <= (ap.X + as.X)) and (p.Y >= ap.Y) and (p.Y <= (ap.Y + as.Y))) then
						return;
					end
					dragging = true;
					update(i);
					grow();
					sweepFill();
					i.Changed:Connect(function()
						if (i.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End) then
							dragging = false;
							grow();
						end
					end);
				end
			end);
			f.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
				hovering = true;
				grow();
			end);
			f.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
				hovering = false;
				grow();
			end);
			track(UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
				if (dragging and isMove(i)) then
					update(i);
				end
			end));
			hoverRow(f, f);
			registry[key] = function(v)
				setValue(v);
			end;
			resetters[key] = function()
				setValue(def);
			end;
			f.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
				if (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2) then
					clickSfx();
					markDirty();
					setValue(def);
				end
			end);
			if cb then
				task.spawn(cb, CONFIG_VALUES[key]);
			end
			return {Set=function(_, v)
				setValue(v);
			end,Frame=f};
		end;
		API.Button = function(text, cb)
			local f = rowBase(32, text);
			f.ClipsDescendants = true;
			local b = make("TextButton", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Size=UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),Text="",AutoButtonColor=false,ZIndex=6}, f);
			make("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Position=UDim2.fromOffset(14, 0),Size=UDim2.new(1, -44, 1, 0),Text=text,FontFace=F_HEAVY,TextSize=18,TextColor3=T.Text,TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,TextTruncate=Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,ZIndex=4}, f);
			local arrow = make("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency=1,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(1, 0.5),Position=UDim2.new(1, -12, 0.5, 0),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18),Text=">",FontFace=F_HEAVY,TextSize=18,TextColor3=T.Dim,ZIndex=4}, f);
			hoverRow(f, b);
			b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
				tween(arrow, 0.18, {Position=UDim2.new(1, -7, 0.5, 0),TextColor3=T.AccentHi});
			end);
			b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
				tween(arrow, 0.18, {Position=UDim2.new(1, -12, 0.5, 0),TextColor3=T.Dim});
			end);
			b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				clickSfx();
				local m = UIS:GetMouseLocation();
				ripple(f, m.X, m.Y - 36);
				tween(f, 0.08, {BackgroundColor3=T.Accent});
				task.delay(0.12, function()
					tween(f, 0.2, {BackgroundColor3=T.Element});
				end);
				if cb then
					task.spawn(cb);
				end
			end);
		end;
		API.Danger = function(text, cb)
			local f = rowBase(36, text);
			local BASE, HOT, ARMED = Color3.fromRGB(40, 14, 16), Color3.fromRGB(62, 20, 24), Color3.fromRGB(110, 26, 32);
			local RED = Color3.fromRGB(235, 70, 76);
			f.BackgroundColor3 = BASE;
			f.ClipsDescendants = true;
			local fs = f:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke");
			if fs then
				fs.Color = Color3.fromRGB(200, 50, 56);
				fs.Transparency = 0.4;
			end
			local edge = f:FindFirstChild("Edge");
			if edge then
				edge:SetAttribute("Fixed", true);
				edge.BackgroundColor3 = RED;
				edge.BackgroundTransparency = 0;
			end
			local warn = make("TextLabel", {BackgroundColor3=RED,Position=UDim2.fromOffset(14, 10),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(20, 20),Text="!",FontFace=F_HEAVY,TextSize=17,TextColor3=Color3.new(1, 1, 1),BorderSizePixel=0,ZIndex=7}, f);
			corner(warn, 10);
			local b = make("TextButton", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Size=UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),Text=text,FontFace=F_HEAVY,TextSize=18,TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255, 130, 130),TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,AutoButtonColor=false,ZIndex=6}, f);
			padding(b, 0, 0, 0, 46);
			local bar = make("Frame", {BackgroundColor3=RED,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0, 1),Position=UDim2.fromScale(0, 1),Size=UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 2),BorderSizePixel=0,ZIndex=7}, f);
			local armed, token, bt = false, 0, nil;
			local function disarm()
				armed = false;
				b.Text = text;
				if bt then
					bt:Cancel();
					bt = nil;
				end
				bar.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 2);
				tween(f, 0.2, {BackgroundColor3=BASE});
			end
			b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
				hoverSfx();
				if not armed then
					tween(f, 0.15, {BackgroundColor3=HOT});
				end
			end);
			b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
				if not armed then
					tween(f, 0.15, {BackgroundColor3=BASE});
				end
			end);
			b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				local m = UIS:GetMouseLocation();
				ripple(f, m.X, m.Y - 36);
				if not armed then
					clickSfx();
					armed = true;
					token += 1
					local mine = token;
					b.Text = "CLICK AGAIN TO CONFIRM";
					tween(f, 0.15, {BackgroundColor3=ARMED});
					bar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 2);
					bt = tween(bar, 3, {Size=UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 2)}, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear);
					task.delay(3, function()
						if (armed and (token == mine)) then
							disarm();
						end
					end);
				else
					token += 1
					disarm();
					if cb then
						task.spawn(cb);
					end
				end
			end);
		end;
		API.IconButton = function(text, cb)
			local f = rowBase(30, text);
			rowLabel(f, text, -60);
			local b = make("TextButton", {BackgroundColor3=T.PanelHi,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(1, 0.5),Position=UDim2.new(1, -8, 0.5, 0),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(30, 24),Text="",AutoButtonColor=false,ZIndex=5}, f);
			corner(b, 7);
			local bs = stroke(b, T.Border, 1, 0.3);
			local icon = make("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency=1,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),Position=UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14),ZIndex=6}, b);
			local ring = make("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Size=UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),ZIndex=6}, icon);
			make("UICorner", {CornerRadius=UDim.new(1, 0)}, ring);
			local rs = stroke(ring, T.AccentHi, 2, 0);
			make("UIGradient", {Rotation=0,Transparency=NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.62, 0),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.63, 1),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.75, 1),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.76, 0),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0)})}, rs);
			local head = make("Frame", {BackgroundColor3=T.AccentHi,BorderSizePixel=0,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),Position=UDim2.new(1, -1, 0, 2),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(5, 5),ZIndex=7}, icon);
			make("UICorner", {CornerRadius=UDim.new(1, 0)}, head);
			onAccent(function()
				rs.Color = T.AccentHi;
				head.BackgroundColor3 = T.AccentHi;
			end);
			b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
				hoverSfx();
				tween(b, 0.15, {BackgroundColor3=T.ElementHi});
				tween(f, 0.15, {BackgroundColor3=T.ElementHi});
			end);
			b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
				tween(b, 0.15, {BackgroundColor3=T.PanelHi});
				tween(f, 0.15, {BackgroundColor3=T.Element});
			end);
			b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				clickSfx();
				tween(icon, 0.45, {Rotation=(icon.Rotation + 360)}, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint);
				if cb then
					task.spawn(cb);
				end
			end);
		end;
		API.TextBox = function(text, placeholder, cb)
			local key = keyOf(text);
			local f = rowBase(30, text);
			rowLabel(f, text, -170);
			local tb = make("TextBox", {BackgroundColor3=T.PanelHi,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(1, 0.5),Position=UDim2.new(1, -8, 0.5, 0),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(150, 24),Text=(CONFIG_VALUES[key] or ""),PlaceholderText=(placeholder or "type..."),PlaceholderColor3=T.Dim,FontFace=F_BOLD,TextSize=17,TextColor3=T.Text,ClearTextOnFocus=false,ZIndex=5}, f);
			corner(tb, 6);
			stroke(tb, T.Border, 1, 0.4);
			tb.FocusLost:Connect(function()
				CONFIG_VALUES[key] = tb.Text;
				if cb then
					task.spawn(cb, tb.Text);
				end
			end);
			registry[key] = function(v)
				tb.Text = tostring(v);
			end;
			resetters[key] = function()
				tb.Text = "";
				CONFIG_VALUES[key] = "";
			end;
			return {Get=function()
				return tb.Text;
			end,Set=function(_, v)
				tb.Text = tostring(v);
			end};
		end;
		API.Keybind = function(text, default)
			local key = keyOf(text);
			if CONFIG_VALUES[key] then
				default = CONFIG_VALUES[key];
			end
			CONFIG_VALUES[key] = default;
			local f = rowBase(30, text);
			rowLabel(f, text, -120);
			local kb = make("TextButton", {BackgroundColor3=T.PanelHi,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(1, 0.5),Position=UDim2.new(1, -8, 0.5, 0),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(96, 24),Text=(default or "None"),FontFace=F_HEAVY,TextSize=17,TextColor3=T.AccentHi,AutoButtonColor=false,ZIndex=5}, f);
			corner(kb, 6);
			stroke(kb, T.Border, 1, 0.4);
			onAccent(function()
				kb.TextColor3 = T.AccentHi;
			end);
			kb.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
				hoverSfx();
				tween(kb, 0.15, {BackgroundColor3=T.ElementHi});
			end);
			kb.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
				tween(kb, 0.15, {BackgroundColor3=T.PanelHi});
			end);
			local listening;
			kb.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				clickSfx();
				if listening then
					return;
				end
				kb.Text = "...";
				listening = UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
					if (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard) then
						kb.Text = i.KeyCode.Name;
						CONFIG_VALUES[key] = i.KeyCode.Name;
						keybindRegistry[key] = {name=text,get=function()
							return CONFIG_VALUES[key];
						end};
						markDirty();
						notify("Keybind", text .. " set to " .. i.KeyCode.Name, 2, "ok");
						listening:Disconnect();
						listening = nil;
					end
				end);
			end);
			registry[key] = function(v)
				kb.Text = tostring(v);
				keybindRegistry[key] = {name=text,get=function()
					return CONFIG_VALUES[key];
				end};
			end;
			keybindRegistry[key] = {name=text,get=function()
				return CONFIG_VALUES[key];
			end};
		end;
		local function expandable(text, headW)
			local HEAD = 30;
			local f = rowBase(HEAD, text);
			f.ClipsDescendants = true;
			rowLabel(f, text, -(headW + 20));
			local state = {open=false,body=0};
			state.apply = function()
				tween(f, 0.2, {Size=UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, (state.open and (HEAD + 6 + state.body)) or HEAD)});
			end;
			state.toggle = function(v)
				state.open = v;
				state.apply();
			end;
			return f, HEAD, state;
		end
		API.Dropdown = function(text, options, default, cb)
			local key = keyOf(text);
			local def = default or options[1];
			if CONFIG_VALUES[key] then
				default = CONFIG_VALUES[key];
			end
			local current = default or options[1];
			CONFIG_VALUES[key] = current;
			local f, HEAD, st = expandable(text, 120);
			local head = make("TextButton", {BackgroundColor3=T.PanelHi,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(1, 0),Position=UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 5),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(120, 24),Text="",FontFace=F_HEAVY,TextSize=17,TextColor3=T.Text,AutoButtonColor=false,ZIndex=5}, f);
			corner(head, 6);
			stroke(head, T.Border, 1, 0.4);
			local valLbl = FX.headLbl(head, tostring(current));
			head.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
				hoverSfx();
				tween(head, 0.15, {BackgroundColor3=T.ElementHi});
			end);
			head.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
				tween(head, 0.15, {BackgroundColor3=T.PanelHi});
			end);
			local list = make("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Position=UDim2.fromOffset(8, HEAD + 2),Size=UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 0),ZIndex=4}, f);
			make("UIListLayout", {Padding=UDim.new(0, 2),SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder}, list);
			local function setCurrent(v, silent)
				current = v;
				CONFIG_VALUES[key] = v;
				valLbl.Text = tostring(v);
				if (cb and not silent) then
					task.spawn(cb, v);
				end
			end
			local function rebuild(opts)
				for _, c in ipairs(list:GetChildren()) do
					if c:IsA("TextButton") then
						c:Destroy();
					end
				end
				for i, opt in ipairs(opts) do
					local o = make("TextButton", {BackgroundColor3=T.PanelHi,Size=UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26),Text=tostring(opt),FontFace=F_BOLD,TextSize=18,TextColor3=T.Text,AutoButtonColor=false,LayoutOrder=i,ZIndex=5}, list);
					corner(o, 6);
					o.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
						hoverSfx();
						tween(o, 0.12, {BackgroundColor3=T.ElementHi});
					end);
					o.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
						tween(o, 0.12, {BackgroundColor3=T.PanelHi});
					end);
					o.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
						clickSfx();
						markDirty();
						setCurrent(opt);
						st.toggle(false);
					end);
				end
				st.body = #opts * 28;
				list.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, st.body);
				if st.open then
					st.apply();
				end
			end
			rebuild(options);
			head.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				clickSfx();
				st.toggle(not st.open);
			end);
			registry[key] = function(v)
				setCurrent(v);
			end;
			resetters[key] = function()
				setCurrent(def);
			end;
			if cb then
				task.spawn(cb, current);
			end
			return {Set=function(_, v)
				setCurrent(v);
			end,SetOptions=function(_, o)
				rebuild(o);
			end,Get=function()
				return current;
			end,Frame=f,Open=function(v)
				st.toggle(v ~= false);
			end};
		end;
		API.MultiDropdown = function(text, options, defaults)
			local key = keyOf(text);
			local sel = {};
			local src = CONFIG_VALUES[key] or defaults or {};
			for _, v in ipairs(src) do
				sel[v] = true;
			end
			local f, HEAD, st = expandable(text, 120);
			local head = make("TextButton", {BackgroundColor3=T.PanelHi,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(1, 0),Position=UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 5),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(120, 24),Text="",FontFace=F_HEAVY,TextSize=17,TextColor3=T.Text,AutoButtonColor=false,ZIndex=5}, f);
			corner(head, 6);
			stroke(head, T.Border, 1, 0.4);
			local valLbl = FX.headLbl(head, "");
			local list = make("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Position=UDim2.fromOffset(8, HEAD + 2),Size=UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, #options * 28),ZIndex=4}, f);
			make("UIListLayout", {Padding=UDim.new(0, 2),SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder}, list);
			st.body = #options * 28;
			local btns = {};
			local function refresh()
				local arr, c = {}, 0;
				for _, o in ipairs(options) do
					if sel[o] then
						c += 1
						table.insert(arr, o);
					end
					if btns[o] then
						tween(btns[o], 0.12, {BackgroundColor3=((sel[o] and T.Accent) or T.PanelHi)});
					end
				end
				valLbl.Text = c .. " selected";
				CONFIG_VALUES[key] = arr;
			end
			for i, opt in ipairs(options) do
				local o = make("TextButton", {BackgroundColor3=T.PanelHi,Size=UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26),Text=tostring(opt),FontFace=F_BOLD,TextSize=18,TextColor3=T.Text,AutoButtonColor=false,LayoutOrder=i,ZIndex=5}, list);
				corner(o, 6);
				btns[opt] = o;
				o.MouseEnter:Connect(hoverSfx);
				o.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
					clickSfx();
					sel[opt] = not sel[opt];
					refresh();
				end);
			end
			refresh();
			head.MouseEnter:Connect(hoverSfx);
			head.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				clickSfx();
				st.toggle(not st.open);
			end);
			registry[key] = function(v)
				sel = {};
				for _, x in ipairs(v) do
					sel[x] = true;
				end
				refresh();
			end;
		end;
		local PALETTE = {"#C41A1E","#F03E3E","#F08C00","#FAB005","#40C057","#12B886","#228BE6","#7048E8","#E64980","#FFFFFF"};
		API.ColorWheel = function(text, defaultHex, cb)
			local key = keyOf(text);
			local hex = CONFIG_VALUES[key] or defaultHex or "#FFFFFF";
			local okc, c0 = pcall(Color3.fromHex, hex);
			if not okc then
				hex = defaultHex or "#FFFFFF";
				c0 = Color3.fromHex(hex);
			end
			local h, s, v = c0:ToHSV();
			local f, HEAD, st = expandable(text, 44);
			local sw = make("TextButton", {BackgroundColor3=c0,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),Position=UDim2.new(1, -22, 0, HEAD / 2),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18),Text="",AutoButtonColor=false,ZIndex=5}, f);
			corner(sw, 9);
			stroke(sw, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 1.5, 0.35);
			local WD = 152;
			local BH = WD + 8;
			local body = make("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Position=UDim2.fromOffset(8, HEAD + 2),Size=UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, BH),ZIndex=4}, f);
			st.body = BH + 4;
			local ui, built = {}, false;
			local function refresh(silent)
				local col = Color3.fromHSV(h, s, v);
				hex = "#" .. col:ToHex();
				CONFIG_VALUES[key] = hex;
				sw.BackgroundColor3 = col;
				if built then
					local a = h * math.pi * 2;
					ui.knob.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5 + (math.sin(a) * s * 0.5), 0.5 - (math.cos(a) * s * 0.5));
					ui.knobFill.BackgroundColor3 = col;
					ui.shade.BackgroundTransparency = v;
					ui.vGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromHSV(h, s, 1), Color3.new(0, 0, 0));
					ui.vKnob.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 1 - v);
					ui.prev.BackgroundColor3 = col;
					if not ui.hexBox:IsFocused() then
						ui.hexBox.Text = hex;
					end
					ui.info.Text = string.format("Hue %d   Sat %d%%   Val %d%%", math.floor((h * 360) + 0.5), math.floor((s * 100) + 0.5), math.floor((v * 100) + 0.5));
				end
				if (cb and not silent) then
					task.spawn(cb, hex);
				end
			end
			local function build()
				if built then
					return;
				end
				built = true;
				local box = make("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Position=UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(WD, WD),ZIndex=5}, body);
				local disc = make("CanvasGroup", {BackgroundColor3=Color3.new(1, 1, 1),BorderSizePixel=0,Size=UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),ZIndex=5}, box);
				make("UICorner", {CornerRadius=UDim.new(1, 0)}, disc);
				local R, N = WD / 2, 72;
				local w = math.ceil((2 * math.pi * R) / N) + 3;
				for i = 0, N - 1 do
					local hh = i / N;
					local sp = make("Frame", {BackgroundColor3=Color3.new(1, 1, 1),BorderSizePixel=0,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),Position=UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(w, WD + 4),Rotation=(hh * 360),ZIndex=5}, disc);
					make("UIGradient", {Name="NoTheme",Rotation=90,Color=ColorSequence.new({ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromHSV(hh, 1, 1)),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.new(1, 1, 1)),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.new(1, 1, 1))}),Transparency=NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.501, 1),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})}, sp);
				end
				ui.shade = make("Frame", {BackgroundColor3=Color3.new(0, 0, 0),BackgroundTransparency=v,BorderSizePixel=0,Size=UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),ZIndex=6}, disc);
				local rim = make("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Size=UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),ZIndex=7}, box);
				make("UICorner", {CornerRadius=UDim.new(1, 0)}, rim);
				stroke(rim, T.Border, 2, 0.1);
				ui.knob = make("Frame", {BackgroundColor3=Color3.new(1, 1, 1),BorderSizePixel=0,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18),ZIndex=9}, box);
				make("UICorner", {CornerRadius=UDim.new(1, 0)}, ui.knob);
				stroke(ui.knob, Color3.new(0, 0, 0), 1, 0.4);
				ui.knobFill = make("Frame", {BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromHSV(h, s, v),BorderSizePixel=0,Position=UDim2.fromOffset(3, 3),Size=UDim2.new(1, -6, 1, -6),ZIndex=10}, ui.knob);
				make("UICorner", {CornerRadius=UDim.new(1, 0)}, ui.knobFill);
				local vb = make("Frame", {BackgroundColor3=Color3.new(1, 1, 1),BorderSizePixel=0,Position=UDim2.fromOffset(4 + WD + 14, 4),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(20, WD),ZIndex=5}, body);
				corner(vb, 6);
				stroke(vb, T.Border, 1, 0.3);
				ui.vGrad = make("UIGradient", {Name="NoTheme",Rotation=90}, vb);
				ui.vKnob = make("Frame", {AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),Size=UDim2.new(1, 8, 0, 6),BackgroundTransparency=1,ZIndex=8}, vb);
				stroke(ui.vKnob, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 2, 0);
				local X0 = 4 + WD + 14 + 20 + 18;
				ui.hexBox = make("TextBox", {BackgroundColor3=T.PanelHi,Position=UDim2.fromOffset(X0, 4),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(112, 28),Text=hex,PlaceholderText="#RRGGBB",PlaceholderColor3=T.Dim,FontFace=F_HEAVY,TextSize=19,TextColor3=T.AccentHi,ClearTextOnFocus=false,ZIndex=5}, body);
				corner(ui.hexBox, 6);
				stroke(ui.hexBox, T.Border, 1, 0.4);
				ui.prev = make("Frame", {BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromHSV(h, s, v),BorderSizePixel=0,Position=UDim2.fromOffset(X0 + 120, 4),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(60, 28),ZIndex=5}, body);
				corner(ui.prev, 6);
				stroke(ui.prev, T.Border, 1, 0.2);
				ui.info = make("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Position=UDim2.fromOffset(X0, 40),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(280, 20),Text="",FontFace=F_BOLD,TextSize=16,TextColor3=T.Sub,TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,ZIndex=5}, body);
				local rl = make("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Position=UDim2.fromOffset(X0 + 4, 74),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(300, 28),ZIndex=5}, body);
				make("UIListLayout", {FillDirection=Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,Padding=UDim.new(0, 9),VerticalAlignment=Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center}, rl);
				for _, ph in ipairs(PALETTE) do
					local c = make("TextButton", {BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromHex(ph),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(17, 17),Text="",AutoButtonColor=false,ZIndex=6}, rl);
					corner(c, 9);
					c.MouseEnter:Connect(hoverSfx);
					c.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
						clickSfx();
						markDirty();
						h, s, v = Color3.fromHex(ph):ToHSV();
						refresh(false);
					end);
				end
				local mode;
				local function update(i)
					if (mode == "wheel") then
						local c = box.AbsolutePosition + (box.AbsoluteSize / 2);
						local dx, dy = i.Position.X - c.X, i.Position.Y - c.Y;
						s = math.clamp(math.sqrt((dx * dx) + (dy * dy)) / math.max(1, box.AbsoluteSize.X / 2), 0, 1);
						h = (math.atan2(dx, -dy) / (2 * math.pi)) % 1;
					elseif (mode == "val") then
						v = 1 - math.clamp((i.Position.Y - vb.AbsolutePosition.Y) / math.max(1, vb.AbsoluteSize.Y), 0, 1);
					end
					refresh(false);
					markDirty();
				end
				local function begin(which)
					return function(i)
						if not isPress(i) then
							return;
						end
						mode = which;
						update(i);
						i.Changed:Connect(function()
							if (i.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End) then
								mode = nil;
							end
						end);
					end;
				end
				box.InputBegan:Connect(begin("wheel"));
				vb.InputBegan:Connect(begin("val"));
				track(UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
					if (mode and isMove(i)) then
						update(i);
					end
				end));
				ui.hexBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
					local t = (ui.hexBox.Text:gsub("%s", ""));
					if (t:sub(1, 1) ~= "#") then
						t = "#" .. t;
					end
					local ok, c = pcall(Color3.fromHex, t);
					if ok then
						h, s, v = c:ToHSV();
						markDirty();
					end
					refresh(false);
				end);
				refresh(true);
			end
			sw.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				clickSfx();
				local o = not st.open;
				if o then
					build();
				end
				st.toggle(o);
			end);
			registry[key] = function(x)
				local ok, c = pcall(Color3.fromHex, x);
				if ok then
					h, s, v = c:ToHSV();
					refresh(false);
				end
			end;
			resetters[key] = function()
				h, s, v = Color3.fromHex(defaultHex or "#FFFFFF"):ToHSV();
				refresh(false);
			end;
			refresh(true);
			if cb then
				task.spawn(cb, hex);
			end
			return {Set=function(_, x)
				registry[key](x);
			end,Get=function()
				return hex;
			end};
		end;
		API.Color = function(text, defaultHex, cb)
			return API.ColorWheel(text, defaultHex, cb);
		end;
		API.Page = page;
		return API;
	end
	local function build(api, list)
		for _, e in ipairs(list) do
			local k = e[1];
			if (k == "sec") then
				api.Section(e[2]);
			elseif (k == "t") then
				api.Toggle(e[2], e[3] or false, e[4]);
			elseif (k == "s") then
				api.Slider(e[2], e[3], e[4], e[5], e[7], e[6]);
			elseif (k == "d") then
				api.Dropdown(e[2], e[3], e[4], e[5]);
			elseif (k == "m") then
				api.MultiDropdown(e[2], e[3], e[4]);
			elseif (k == "k") then
				api.Keybind(e[2], e[3]);
			elseif (k == "b") then
				api.Button(e[2], e[3]);
			elseif (k == "i") then
				api.IconButton(e[2], e[3]);
			elseif (k == "dg") then
				api.Danger(e[2], e[3]);
			elseif (k == "c") then
				api.Color(e[2], e[3]);
			elseif (k == "cw") then
				api.ColorWheel(e[2], e[3], e[4]);
			elseif (k == "x") then
				api.TextBox(e[2], e[3], e[4]);
			elseif (k == "l") then
				api.Label(e[2]);
			end
		end
	end
	local Cosmetic = (function()
		local API = {Ready=false,Blacklist={}};
		local Players = game:GetService("Players");
		local RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage");
		local lp = Players.LocalPlayer;
		local BL = {};
		API.Blacklist.Add = function(name)
			if (type(name) == "string") then
				BL[name] = true;
			end
		end;
		API.Blacklist.Remove = function(name)
			BL[name] = nil;
		end;
		API.Blacklist.Clear = function()
			BL = {};
		end;
		API.Blacklist.Has = function(name)
			return BL[name] == true;
		end;
		API.Blacklist.List = function()
			local out = {};
			for k in pairs(BL) do
				out[#out + 1] = k;
			end
			table.sort(out);
			return out;
		end;
		local okInit, initErr = pcall(function()
			local ctrl = lp:WaitForChild("PlayerScripts"):WaitForChild("Controllers", 3);
			local mods = RS:WaitForChild("Modules", 3);
			local enumLib = require(mods:WaitForChild("EnumLibrary", 3));
			if enumLib then
				pcall(function()
					enumLib:WaitForEnumBuilder();
				end);
			end
			API._enumLib = enumLib;
			API._cosLib = require(mods:WaitForChild("CosmeticLibrary", 3));
			API._datCtrl = require(ctrl:WaitForChild("PlayerDataController", 3));
			local il = mods:FindFirstChild("ItemLibrary");
			if il then
				API._items = (select(2, pcall(require, il)));
			end
		end);
		if (not okInit or not API._cosLib or not API._datCtrl) then
			warn("[Cosmetic] could not load game modules: " .. tostring(initErr));
			return API;
		end
		local enumLib, cosLib, datCtrl = API._enumLib, API._cosLib, API._datCtrl;
		local Cosmetics = cosLib.Cosmetics;
		local itemsTbl = ((type(API._items) == "table") and API._items.Items) or nil;
		local function norm(w)
			if (type(w) == "table") then
				w = w.Name;
			end
			if (type(w) ~= "string") then
				return nil;
			end
			return (w:lower():gsub("%s+", ""));
		end
		local function isPlaceholder(n)
			return (type(n) == "string") and (n:match("^MISSING_") ~= nil);
		end
		local function allowed(name)
			if ((type(name) ~= "string") or BL[name]) then
				return false;
			end
			local c = Cosmetics[name];
			if (type(c) ~= "table") then
				return false;
			end
			if (c.ItemName and BL[c.ItemName]) then
				return false;
			end
			return not isPlaceholder(name);
		end
		API.IsAllowed = allowed;
		local allItemNames;
		local function itemMap()
			if allItemNames then
				return allItemNames;
			end
			allItemNames = {};
			if itemsTbl then
				for n in pairs(itemsTbl) do
					if (type(n) == "string") then
						allItemNames[n] = true;
					end
				end
			end
			return allItemNames;
		end
		local function mkSkin(name)
			local base = Cosmetics[name];
			if not base then
				return nil;
			end
			local d = {};
			for k, v in pairs(base) do
				d[k] = v;
			end
			d.Name = name;
			d.Type = d.Type or "Skin";
			d.Seed = d.Seed or math.random(1, 1000000);
			if enumLib then
				local ok, id = pcall(enumLib.ToEnum, enumLib, name);
				if (ok and id) then
					d.Enum = id;
					d.ObjectID = d.ObjectID or id;
				end
			end
			return d;
		end
		local viewRoot;
		local function viewModelsRoot()
			if (viewRoot and viewRoot.Parent) then
				return viewRoot;
			end
			local ps = lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts");
			local a = ps and ps:FindFirstChild("Assets");
			viewRoot = a and a:FindFirstChild("ViewModels");
			return viewRoot;
		end
		local weaponCache;
		API.Weapons = function()
			if weaponCache then
				return weaponCache;
			end
			local seen, out = {}, {};
			for name, c in pairs(Cosmetics) do
				local w = (type(c) == "table") and c.ItemName;
				if ((type(c) == "table") and (c.Type == "Skin") and (type(w) == "string") and (w ~= "") and not isPlaceholder(w) and not isPlaceholder(name) and not seen[w]) then
					seen[w] = true;
					out[#out + 1] = w;
				end
			end
			table.sort(out);
			weaponCache = out;
			return out;
		end;
		API.SkinsFor = function(weapon)
			local out = {};
			for name, c in pairs(Cosmetics) do
				if ((type(c) == "table") and (c.Type == "Skin") and (c.ItemName == weapon) and allowed(name)) then
					out[#out + 1] = name;
				end
			end
			table.sort(out);
			return out;
		end;
		API.PreviewSource = function(skinName)
			local c = Cosmetics[skinName];
			local root = viewModelsRoot();
			if not (c and root) then
				return nil;
			end
			if ((type(c.Folder) == "string") and (c.Folder ~= "")) then
				local f = root:FindFirstChild(c.Folder);
				local m = f and f:FindFirstChild(skinName, true);
				if m then
					return m;
				end
			end
			return root:FindFirstChild(skinName, true);
		end;
		API.IconId = function(skinName)
			local c = Cosmetics[skinName];
			if (type(c) ~= "table") then
				return nil;
			end
			for _, k in ipairs({"Icon","IconId","Image","ImageId","Thumbnail","PreviewImage"}) do
				local v = c[k];
				if (type(v) == "number") then
					return "rbxassetid://" .. v;
				end
				if ((type(v) == "string") and (v ~= "")) then
					return v;
				end
			end
			return nil;
		end;
		local function folderNames(folder)
			if not folder then
				return nil;
			end
			local t = {};
			for _, c in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
				t[c.Name] = true;
			end
			return t;
		end
		local namesCache = {};
		API.Names = function(kind)
			if namesCache[kind] then
				return namesCache[kind];
			end
			local avail;
			if (kind == "Charm") then
				local ps = lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts");
				local a = ps and ps:FindFirstChild("Assets");
				avail = folderNames(a and a:FindFirstChild("Charms"));
			elseif (kind == "Finisher") then
				local m = RS:FindFirstChild("Modules");
				avail = folderNames(m and m:FindFirstChild("Finishers"));
			end
			local out = {};
			for name, c in pairs(Cosmetics) do
				if ((type(c) == "table") and (c.Type == kind) and not isPlaceholder(name) and ((avail == nil) or avail[name])) then
					out[#out + 1] = name;
				end
			end
			table.sort(out);
			namesCache[kind] = out;
			return out;
		end;
		API.NamesAllowed = function(kind)
			local out = {};
			for _, n in ipairs(API.Names(kind)) do
				if allowed(n) then
					out[#out + 1] = n;
				end
			end
			return out;
		end;
		API.CharmModel = function(name)
			local ps = lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts");
			local a = ps and ps:FindFirstChild("Assets");
			local f = a and a:FindFirstChild("Charms");
			return f and f:FindFirstChild(name);
		end;
		API.WeaponModel = function(weapon)
			local root = viewModelsRoot();
			local w = root and root:FindFirstChild("Weapons");
			return w and w:FindFirstChild(weapon);
		end;
		local wrapCtrl;
		API.WrapController = function()
			if wrapCtrl then
				return wrapCtrl;
			end
			pcall(function()
				wrapCtrl = require(lp.PlayerScripts.Controllers.WrapController);
			end);
			return wrapCtrl;
		end;
		local equipped = {};
		local active = true;
		local origData = {};
		local function entry(weapon, create)
			local k = norm(weapon);
			if not k then
				return nil;
			end
			local e = equipped[k];
			if (not e and create) then
				e = {weapon=weapon};
				equipped[k] = e;
			end
			return e;
		end
		API.Get = function(weapon, kind)
			local e = entry(weapon);
			if not e then
				return nil;
			end
			if (kind == "Skin") then
				return e.skin and e.skin.name;
			end
			if (kind == "Wrap") then
				return e.wrap;
			end
			if (kind == "Charm") then
				return e.charm;
			end
			if (kind == "Finisher") then
				return e.finisher;
			end
		end;
		API.Equipped = function(weapon)
			return API.Get(weapon, "Skin");
		end;
		API.Inverted = function(weapon)
			local e = entry(weapon);
			return e and (e.inverted == true);
		end;
		local function encodeKey(key)
			local toEnum = ((type(enumLib) == "table") and rawget(enumLib, "_to_enum")) or nil;
			local v = ((type(toEnum) == "table") and rawget(toEnum, key)) or nil;
			return ((v ~= nil) and v) or key;
		end
		local function applySel(data, e, key)
			if not (data and e) then
				return;
			end
			if e.skin then
				data[key("Skin")] = e.skin.data;
				data[key("Name")] = e.skin.name;
			end
			if e.wrap then
				data[key("Wrap")] = {Name=e.wrap,Inverted=((e.inverted and true) or nil)};
			end
			if e.charm then
				data[key("Charm")] = {Name=e.charm};
			end
		end
		local fighterCtrl;
		local function localFighter()
			if not fighterCtrl then
				pcall(function()
					fighterCtrl = require(lp.PlayerScripts.Controllers.FighterController);
				end);
			end
			if not fighterCtrl then
				return nil;
			end
			local f = fighterCtrl.LocalFighter;
			if (not f and (type(fighterCtrl.GetFighter) == "function")) then
				local ok, r = pcall(fighterCtrl.GetFighter, fighterCtrl, lp);
				f = (ok and r) or nil;
			end
			return f;
		end
		local function runId2(cb)
			local prev = ((type(getthreadidentity) == "function") and getthreadidentity()) or nil;
			if (type(setthreadidentity) == "function") then
				pcall(setthreadidentity, 2);
			end
			local ok, a, b = pcall(cb);
			if ((prev ~= nil) and (type(setthreadidentity) == "function")) then
				pcall(setthreadidentity, prev);
			end
			return ok, a, b;
		end
		local function findItem(weapon)
			local f = localFighter();
			if (type(f) ~= "table") then
				return nil, nil;
			end
			local held = rawget(f, "EquippedItem");
			if ((type(held) == "table") and (norm(rawget(held, "Name")) == norm(weapon))) then
				return held, f;
			end
			for _, field in ipairs({"Loadout","Slots","Items","EquippedItems"}) do
				local items = rawget(f, field);
				if (type(items) == "table") then
					for _, it in pairs(items) do
						if ((type(it) == "table") and (norm(rawget(it, "Name")) == norm(weapon))) then
							return it, f;
						end
					end
				end
			end
			return nil, f;
		end
		local reloading = {};
		API.Reload = function(weapon)
			local k = norm(weapon);
			if (not k or reloading[k]) then
				return false;
			end
			local item, fighter = findItem(weapon);
			local vm = item and rawget(item, "ViewModel");
			if (type(vm) ~= "table") then
				return false;
			end
			local vmData = rawget(vm, "Data");
			if ((type(vmData) ~= "table") and not origData[k]) then
				return false;
			end
			reloading[k] = true;
			local done = false;
			pcall(function()
				local base = (origData[k] and table.clone(origData[k])) or table.clone(vmData);
				origData[k] = origData[k] or table.clone(base);
				local e = (active and entry(weapon)) or nil;
				applySel(base, e, encodeKey);
				local cvmClass;
				pcall(function()
					cvmClass = require(lp.PlayerScripts.Modules.ClientReplicatedClasses.ClientFighter.ClientItem.ClientViewModel);
				end);
				if ((type(cvmClass) ~= "table") or (type(cvmClass.Destroy) ~= "function")) then
					return;
				end
				local create = item._CreateViewModel;
				if (type(create) ~= "function") then
					return;
				end
				local info = {[encodeKey("Data")]=base};
				local armsData;
				if ((type(fighter) == "table") and (type(fighter.GetArmsData) == "function")) then
					armsData = table.pack(fighter:GetArmsData());
				end
				runId2(function()
					cvmClass.Destroy(vm);
				end);
				local ok, newVm = runId2(function()
					return create(item, info);
				end);
				if (not ok or (type(newVm) ~= "table")) then
					return;
				end
				rawset(item, "ViewModel", newVm);
				runId2(function()
					if (armsData and (type(newVm.SetArmsData) == "function")) then
						newVm:SetArmsData(unpack(armsData, 1, armsData.n));
					end
					if (type(newVm.Equip) == "function") then
						newVm:Equip(true);
					end
				end);
				local spring = rawget(newVm, "_equip_spring");
				if (type(spring) == "table") then
					rawset(spring, "_position0", 0);
					rawset(spring, "_velocity0", 0);
				end
				done = true;
			end);
			reloading[k] = nil;
			return done;
		end;
		local pendingRefresh, refreshQueued = {}, false;
		local function refresh(weapon)
			if weapon then
				pendingRefresh[weapon] = true;
			end
			if refreshQueued then
				return;
			end
			refreshQueued = true;
			task.defer(function()
				refreshQueued = false;
				local list = pendingRefresh;
				pendingRefresh = {};
				pcall(function()
					datCtrl.CurrentData:Replicate("WeaponInventory");
				end);
				for w in pairs(list) do
					pcall(API.Reload, w);
				end
			end);
		end
		API.Set = function(weapon, kind, name, inverted)
			local e = entry(weapon, true);
			if not e then
				return false;
			end
			if ((name ~= nil) and not allowed(name)) then
				return false;
			end
			if (inverted ~= nil) then
				e.inverted = (inverted and true) or false;
			end
			if (kind == "Skin") then
				e.skin = (name and {name=name,data=mkSkin(name)}) or nil;
			elseif (kind == "Wrap") then
				e.wrap = name;
			elseif (kind == "Charm") then
				e.charm = name;
			elseif (kind == "Finisher") then
				e.finisher = name;
			else
				return false;
			end
			refresh(weapon);
			return true;
		end;
		API.Equip = function(weapon, skinName)
			return API.Set(weapon, "Skin", skinName);
		end;
		API.SetInverted = function(weapon, v)
			local e = entry(weapon, true);
			if not e then
				return;
			end
			e.inverted = (v and true) or false;
			refresh(weapon);
		end;
		for _, key in ipairs({"OwnsCosmetic","OwnsCosmeticNormally","OwnsCosmeticUniversally","OwnsCosmeticForWeapon"}) do
			local orig = cosLib[key];
			if (type(orig) == "function") then
				cosLib[key] = function(self, inv, nm, ...)
					if (active and allowed(nm)) then
						return true;
					end
					return orig(self, inv, nm, ...);
				end;
			end
		end
		local origGet = datCtrl.Get;
		local invSrc, invProxy;
		local invMeta = {__index=function(_, k)
			if not allowed(k) then
				return nil;
			end
			local t = Cosmetics[k].Type;
			if ((t == "Wrap") or (t == "Charm") or (t == "Finisher")) then
				return itemMap();
			end
			return true;
		end};
		datCtrl.Get = function(self, key, ...)
			local val = origGet(self, key, ...);
			if (active and (key == "CosmeticInventory")) then
				if (invProxy and (invSrc == val)) then
					return invProxy;
				end
				local prx = {};
				if (type(val) == "table") then
					for k, v in pairs(val) do
						prx[k] = v;
					end
				end
				invSrc, invProxy = val, setmetatable(prx, invMeta);
				return invProxy;
			end
			return val;
		end;
		local origGetWep = datCtrl.GetWeaponData;
		local wepCache = {};
		datCtrl.GetWeaponData = function(self, wn, ...)
			local d = origGetWep(self, wn, ...);
			if not d then
				return nil;
			end
			local e = active and entry(wn);
			if (e and (e.skin or e.wrap or e.charm or e.finisher)) then
				local sig = table.concat({((e.skin and e.skin.name) or ""),(e.wrap or ""),tostring(e.inverted),(e.charm or ""),(e.finisher or "")}, "|");
				local c = wepCache[wn];
				if (c and (c.src == d) and (c.sig == sig)) then
					return c.out;
				end
				local m = {};
				for k, v in pairs(d) do
					m[k] = v;
				end
				m.Name = wn;
				if e.skin then
					m.Skin = e.skin.data;
				end
				if e.wrap then
					m.Wrap = {Name=e.wrap,Inverted=((e.inverted and true) or nil)};
				end
				if e.charm then
					m.Charm = {Name=e.charm};
				end
				if e.finisher then
					m.Finisher = {Name=e.finisher};
				end
				wepCache[wn] = {src=d,sig=sig,out=m};
				return m;
			end
			return d;
		end;
		local buildingWep;
		local cliItem;
		pcall(function()
			cliItem = require(lp.PlayerScripts.Modules.ClientReplicatedClasses.ClientFighter.ClientItem);
		end);
		if (cliItem and cliItem._CreateViewModel) then
			local origCVM = cliItem._CreateViewModel;
			cliItem._CreateViewModel = function(self, vmRef, ...)
				local owner = self.ClientFighter and self.ClientFighter.Player;
				buildingWep = ((owner == lp) and self.Name) or nil;
				if ((owner == lp) and (type(vmRef) == "table")) then
					local k = norm(self.Name);
					local dk = self:ToEnum("Data");
					local data = vmRef[dk] or vmRef.Data;
					if ((type(data) == "table") and k and not origData[k]) then
						origData[k] = table.clone(data);
					end
					local e = active and entry(self.Name);
					if (e and data) then
						if vmRef[dk] then
							applySel(vmRef[dk], e, function(n)
								return self:ToEnum(n);
							end);
						else
							applySel(vmRef.Data, e, function(n)
								return n;
							end);
						end
					end
				end
				local r = origCVM(self, vmRef, ...);
				buildingWep = nil;
				return r;
			end;
		end
		pcall(function()
			local vmMod = lp.PlayerScripts.Modules.ClientReplicatedClasses.ClientFighter.ClientItem:FindFirstChild("ClientViewModel");
			if not vmMod then
				return;
			end
			local CVM = require(vmMod);
			local RC = require(RS.Modules.ReplicatedClass);
			local origNew = CVM.new;
			CVM.new = function(repData, cliItm, ...)
				local owner = cliItm.ClientFighter and cliItm.ClientFighter.Player;
				local wn = buildingWep or cliItm.Name;
				local e = active and (owner == lp) and entry(wn);
				if e then
					local dk = RC:ToEnum("Data");
					repData[dk] = repData[dk] or {};
					applySel(repData[dk], e, function(n)
						return RC:ToEnum(n);
					end);
				end
				return origNew(repData, cliItm, ...);
			end;
		end);
		pcall(function()
			local ce = require(lp.PlayerScripts.Modules.ClientReplicatedClasses.ClientEntity);
			if ((type(ce) ~= "table") or (type(ce._PlayFinisher) ~= "function")) then
				return;
			end
			local origPF = ce._PlayFinisher;
			ce._PlayFinisher = function(self, finisherName, ...)
				if active then
					local f = localFighter();
					local held = f and rawget(f, "EquippedItem");
					local e = (type(held) == "table") and entry(rawget(held, "Name"));
					if (e and e.finisher) then
						return origPF(self, e.finisher, ...);
					end
				end
				return origPF(self, finisherName, ...);
			end;
		end);
		API.Unload = function()
			active = false;
			local weapons = {};
			for _, e in pairs(equipped) do
				weapons[#weapons + 1] = e.weapon;
			end
			equipped = {};
			pcall(function()
				datCtrl.CurrentData:Replicate("WeaponInventory");
			end);
			for _, w in ipairs(weapons) do
				pcall(API.Reload, w);
			end
		end;
		API.Ready = true;
		return API;
	end)();
	local HUD = {on=true,wm=true,arr=true,rainbow=false,side="Right",wmPos="Top Left",fps=true,ping=true,name=true};
	do
		local hudGui = make("ScreenGui", {Name="1MagHUD",ResetOnSpawn=false,IgnoreGuiInset=true,ZIndexBehavior=Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,DisplayOrder=2147483647}, PG);
		gui.Destroying:Connect(function()
			hudGui:Destroy();
		end);
		FX.shimmer(hudGui);
		local BG = Color3.fromRGB(12, 12, 15);
		local wm = make("Frame", {BackgroundColor3=BG,BackgroundTransparency=0.12,BorderSizePixel=0,AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.X,Size=UDim2.fromOffset(0, 30),ZIndex=5}, hudGui);
		corner(wm, 6);
		local wmStroke = stroke(wm, T.Accent, 1, 0.55);
		local wmLine = make("Frame", {BackgroundColor3=T.Accent,BorderSizePixel=0,Size=UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 2),Position=UDim2.fromOffset(6, 0),ZIndex=7}, wm);
		make("UIGradient", {Color=ColorSequence.new({ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, T.Accent),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, T.AccentHi),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, T.Accent)})}, wmLine);
		padding(wm, 0, 12, 0, 12);
		local wmText = make("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency=1,AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.X,Size=UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0),Text="",RichText=true,FontFace=F_HEAVY,TextSize=19,TextColor3=T.Text,ZIndex=6}, wm);
		local function hex(c)
			return string.format("#%02X%02X%02X", c.R * 255, c.G * 255, c.B * 255);
		end
		local function wmRefresh()
			local parts = {string.format('<font color="%s">1Mag</font>.ez', hex(T.AccentHi))};
			if HUD.name then
				parts[#parts + 1] = player.DisplayName;
			end
			if HUD.fps then
				parts[#parts + 1] = fpsValue .. " fps";
			end
			if HUD.ping then
				parts[#parts + 1] = getPing() .. " ms";
			end
			wmText.Text = table.concat(parts, '  <font color="#646470">|</font>  ');
		end
		local function wmPlace()
			wm.Visible = HUD.on and HUD.wm;
			local p = HUD.wmPos;
			if (p == "Top Left") then
				wm.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0);
				wm.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 12);
			elseif (p == "Top Right") then
				wm.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0);
				wm.Position = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 12);
			elseif (p == "Bottom Left") then
				wm.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1);
				wm.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 1, -12);
			else
				wm.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1);
				wm.Position = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, -12);
			end
		end
		local list = make("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency=1,AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.XY,Size=UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),ZIndex=5}, hudGui);
		local layout = make("UIListLayout", {SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,Padding=UDim.new(0, 1),HorizontalAlignment=Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right}, list);
		local entries = {};
		local function arrPlace()
			list.Visible = HUD.on and HUD.arr;
			local right = HUD.side == "Right";
			layout.HorizontalAlignment = (right and Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right) or Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left;
			list.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new((right and 1) or 0, 0);
			local yy = 12;
			if ((not right and (HUD.wmPos == "Top Left")) or (right and (HUD.wmPos == "Top Right"))) then
				yy = 50;
			end
			list.Position = (right and UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, yy)) or UDim2.fromOffset(12, yy);
			for _, e in pairs(entries) do
				e.bar.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new((right and 1) or 0, 0.5);
				e.bar.Position = UDim2.new((right and 1) or 0, (right and 7) or -7, 0.5, 0);
				if e.grad then
					e.grad.Rotation = (right and 180) or 0;
				end
				e.label.TextXAlignment = (right and Enum.TextXAlignment.Right) or Enum.TextXAlignment.Left;
			end
		end
		local function addEntry(key, text)
			local right = HUD.side == "Right";
			local row = make("Frame", {BackgroundColor3=BG,BackgroundTransparency=1,BorderSizePixel=0,AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.X,Size=UDim2.fromOffset(0, 16),LayoutOrder=-#text,ZIndex=5}, list);
			corner(row, 3);
			padding(row, 0, 7, 0, 7);
			local wash = make("Frame", {BackgroundColor3=T.Accent,BackgroundTransparency=0.6,BorderSizePixel=0,Size=UDim2.new(1, 14, 1, 0),Position=UDim2.fromOffset(-7, 0),ZIndex=5}, row);
			corner(wash, 3);
			local grad = make("UIGradient", {Rotation=((right and 180) or 0),Transparency=NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.35),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.6, 0.85),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})}, wash);
			local label = make("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency=1,AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.X,Size=UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0),Text=text,FontFace=F_HEAVY,TextSize=13,TextColor3=T.Text,TextTransparency=1,TextXAlignment=((right and Enum.TextXAlignment.Right) or Enum.TextXAlignment.Left),ZIndex=7}, row);
			local ls = stroke(label, Color3.new(0, 0, 0), 1, 0.55);
			local bar = make("Frame", {BackgroundColor3=T.Accent,BorderSizePixel=0,Size=UDim2.new(0, 2, 1, -4),AnchorPoint=Vector2.new((right and 1) or 0, 0.5),Position=UDim2.new((right and 1) or 0, (right and 7) or -7, 0.5, 0),ZIndex=8,BackgroundTransparency=1}, row);
			corner(bar, 1);
			tween(row, 0.25, {BackgroundTransparency=0.45});
			tween(label, 0.25, {TextTransparency=0});
			tween(bar, 0.25, {BackgroundTransparency=0});
			entries[key] = {row=row,label=label,bar=bar,wash=wash,grad=grad};
		end
		function arrayRefresh()
			for key, text in pairs(ENABLED) do
				if not entries[key] then
					addEntry(key, text);
				end
			end
			for key, e in pairs(entries) do
				if not ENABLED[key] then
					entries[key] = nil;
					tween(e.row, 0.2, {BackgroundTransparency=1});
					tween(e.label, 0.2, {TextTransparency=1});
					tween(e.bar, 0.2, {BackgroundTransparency=1});
					if e.wash then
						tween(e.wash, 0.2, {BackgroundTransparency=1});
					end
					task.delay(0.25, function()
						e.row:Destroy();
					end);
				end
			end
		end
		local t0 = tick();
		local hAcc = 0;
		local hc = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
			hAcc += dt
			if (hAcc < (1 / 20)) then
				return;
			end
			hAcc = 0;
			local now = tick() - t0;
			local i = 0;
			for _, e in pairs(entries) do
				i += 1
				local col = (HUD.rainbow and Color3.fromHSV(((now * 0.25) + (i * 0.07)) % 1, 0.75, 1)) or T.AccentHi;
				e.bar.BackgroundColor3 = (HUD.rainbow and col) or T.Accent;
				if e.wash then
					e.wash.BackgroundColor3 = (HUD.rainbow and col) or T.Accent;
				end
			end
			if HUD.rainbow then
				wmLine.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromHSV((now * 0.25) % 1, 0.75, 1);
			else
				wmLine.BackgroundColor3 = T.Accent;
			end
		end);
		task.spawn(function()
			while hudGui.Parent do
				wmRefresh();
				task.wait(0.5);
			end
		end);
		gui.Destroying:Connect(function()
			hc:Disconnect();
		end);
		onAccent(function()
			wmStroke.Color = T.Accent;
		end);
		HUD.apply = function()
			wmPlace();
			arrPlace();
			wmRefresh();
			arrayRefresh();
		end;
		HUD.apply();
	end
	addGroup("Start");
	local mHome = addMain("Home", "Welcome to 1Mag.ez");
	addGroup("Features");
	local mCombat = addMain("Combat", "Combat features.");
	local mVis = addMain("Visual", "Visual features.");
	local mChar = addMain("Character", "Character features.");
	local mWorld = addMain("World", "Lighting, skyboxes and effects.");
	addGroup("Tools");
	local mTex = addMain("Texture Injector", "Replace game textures and sounds with your own.");
	local mCosm = addMain("Cosmetics", "Unlock and equip cosmetics.");
	addGroup("System");
	local mCfg = addMain("Configs", "Save, load, and delete config profiles.");
	local mSet = addMain("Settings", "Interface and theme.");
	do
		local ov = addSub(mHome, "Overview", "Your session at a glance.");
		local f = make("Frame", {BackgroundColor3=T.Element,Size=UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 104),BorderSizePixel=0,LayoutOrder=0,ZIndex=3}, ov.Page);
		corner(f, 12);
		local fs = stroke(f, T.Accent, 1, 0.6);
		onAccent(function()
			fs.Color = T.Accent;
		end);
		local grad = make("UIGradient", {Rotation=20,Transparency=NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.82),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})}, f);
		f.BackgroundColor3 = T.Accent;
		onAccent(function()
			f.BackgroundColor3 = T.Accent;
		end);
		make("Frame", {BackgroundColor3=T.Element,BackgroundTransparency=0.35,Size=UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),BorderSizePixel=0,ZIndex=3}, f);
		local fcorner = f:FindFirstChildOfClass("Frame");
		if fcorner then
			corner(fcorner, 12);
		end

			logoImg({BackgroundTransparency=1,Position=UDim2.fromOffset(14, 12),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(80, 80),ScaleType=Enum.ScaleType.Fit,ZIndex=5}, f);
		else
			make("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Position=UDim2.fromOffset(14, 12),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(80, 80),Text="1MAG",FontFace=F_HEAVY,TextSize=30,TextColor3=T.AccentHi,ZIndex=5}, f);
		end
		make("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Position=UDim2.fromOffset(108, 12),Size=UDim2.new(1, -118, 0, 34),Text="1Mag.ez",FontFace=F_HEAVY,TextSize=34,TextColor3=T.Text,TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,ZIndex=5}, f);
		make("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Position=UDim2.fromOffset(108, 48),Size=UDim2.new(1, -118, 0, 20),Text=("Welcome back, " .. player.DisplayName),FontFace=F_BOLD,TextSize=20,TextColor3=T.Sub,TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,TextTruncate=Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,ZIndex=5}, f);
		make("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Position=UDim2.fromOffset(108, 70),Size=UDim2.new(1, -118, 0, 20),Text="FREEMIUM BUILD  -  v1.2",FontFace=F_HEAVY,TextSize=17,TextColor3=T.AccentHi,TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,ZIndex=5}, f);
		ov.Section("Session");
