	FX.MB = {show=true,locked=false,size=64};
	do
		local MB = FX.MB;
		local all = {};
		local layer = make("Frame", {Name="MobileButtons",BackgroundTransparency=1,Size=UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),ZIndex=400,Active=false}, gui);
		local function loc(i)
			if (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
				return Vector2.new(i.Position.X, i.Position.Y);
			end
			return UIS:GetMouseLocation();
		end
		MB.attach = function(frame, o)
			o.dx, o.dy = o.px, o.py;
			local saved = CONFIG_VALUES["Mobile.pos." .. o.id];
			if (type(saved) == "string") then
				local a, b = saved:match("^([%d%.%-]+),([%d%.%-]+)$");
				if a then
					o.px, o.py = tonumber(a), tonumber(b);
				end
			end
			o.frame = frame;
			frame.Position = UDim2.fromScale(o.px, o.py);
			frame.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
				if ((i.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch) and (i.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1)) then
					return;
				end
				local start, sx, sy, vp = loc(i), o.px, o.py, gui.AbsoluteSize;
				local dragging = false;
				o.wasDragged = false;
				if o.onDown then
					o.onDown();
				end
				task.spawn(function()
					while (i.UserInputState ~= Enum.UserInputState.End) and frame.Parent do
						local d = loc(i) - start;
						if (not dragging and not MB.locked and (d.Magnitude > 10)) then
							dragging = true;
							o.wasDragged = true;
							if o.onCancel then
								o.onCancel();
							end
						end
						if dragging then
							o.px = math.clamp(sx + (d.X / math.max(vp.X, 1)), 0.03, 0.97);
							o.py = math.clamp(sy + (d.Y / math.max(vp.Y, 1)), 0.04, 0.96);
							frame.Position = UDim2.fromScale(o.px, o.py);
						end
						RunService.RenderStepped:Wait();
					end
					if dragging then
						CONFIG_VALUES["Mobile.pos." .. o.id] = string.format("%.4f,%.4f", o.px, o.py);
						pcall(markDirty);
					elseif o.onUp then
						o.onUp();
					end
				end);
			end);
			all[#all + 1] = o;
		end;
		MB.new = function(id, label, px, py)
			local o = {id=id,px=px,py=py,down=false,on=false,kind="hold",want=function()
				return false;
			end};
			local b = make("TextButton", {Name=("MB_" .. id),AutoButtonColor=false,Text=label,FontFace=F_HEAVY,TextSize=16,TextColor3=T.Text,BackgroundColor3=T.Panel,BackgroundTransparency=0.25,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(MB.size, MB.size),Visible=false,ZIndex=1}, layer);
			make("UICorner", {CornerRadius=UDim.new(1, 0)}, b);
			local st = stroke(b, T.Accent, 2, 0.1);
			onAccent(function()
				st.Color = T.Accent;
			end);
			o.btn = b;
			o.refresh = function()
				local act = o.down or ((o.kind == "toggle") and o.on);
				b.BackgroundColor3 = (act and T.Accent) or T.Panel;
				b.BackgroundTransparency = (act and 0.1) or 0.25;
			end;
			o.onDown = function()
				if (o.kind == "hold") then
					o.down = true;
					o.refresh();
				end
			end;
			o.onCancel = function()
				o.down = false;
				o.refresh();
			end;
			o.onUp = function()
				if (o.kind == "toggle") then
					o.on = not o.on;
				elseif ((o.kind == "tap") and o.onTap) then
					pcall(o.onTap);
				end
				o.down = false;
				o.refresh();
			end;
			MB.attach(b, o);
			return o;
		end;
		MB.reset = function()
			for _, o in ipairs(all) do
				CONFIG_VALUES["Mobile.pos." .. o.id] = nil;
				o.px, o.py = o.dx, o.dy;
				if o.frame then
					o.frame.Position = UDim2.fromScale(o.px, o.py);
				end
			end
			pcall(markDirty);
		end;
		local acc = 0;
		track(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
			acc += dt
			if (acc < 0.1) then
				return;
			end
			acc = 0;
			for _, o in ipairs(all) do
				if o.btn then
					local v = (MB.show and o.want() and true) or false;
					if (o.btn.Visible ~= v) then
						o.btn.Visible = v;
						if not v then
							o.down = false;
							o.refresh();
						end
					end
					if (o.btn.Size.X.Offset ~= MB.size) then
						o.btn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(MB.size, MB.size);
					end
				end
			end
		end));
	end
	do
		local A = {on=false,rmb=true,smooth=0,mode="Linear",method="Camera",wall=true,team=false,fov=120,show=true,fill=false,anim="None",silent=false,sMode="Silent",part="Head",ignoreFov=false,sFov=120,sShow=true,sFill=false,sGrad=false,wallbang=false,hitChance=100,wHead=70,wTorso=30,legit=false,act="Always On",aReact=0,aSwitch=0,lReact=0,lSwitch=0};
		local M = {};
		local hasDraw = (type(Drawing) == "table") and (type(Drawing.new) == "function");
		local params = RaycastParams.new();
		params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude;
		local S = {vy=0,vp=0,target=nil,since=0,flash=0,vel=setmetatable({}, {__mode="k"}),noise=0,rand=setmetatable({}, {__mode="k"}),depth=0,hook=nil,hookAt=0,lostAt=0,pend=nil,pendAt=0,pendDelay=0,wrapSet=setmetatable({}, {__mode="k"}),depthAt=0};
		local aa = addSub(mCombat, "Aim Assist", "Camera aim assist. Moves the camera and viewmodel together.");
		aa.Section("Aim Assist");
		aa.Toggle("Aim Assist", false, function(v)
			A.on = v;
		end);
		aa.Dropdown("Activation", {"Always On","Button (Hold)","Button (Toggle)"}, "Always On", function(v)
			A.act = v;
			local b = FX.MB.aim;
			if b then
				b.kind = ((v == "Button (Toggle)") and "toggle") or "hold";
				b.on = false;
				b.down = false;
				b.refresh();
			end
		end);
		aa.Hint("Always On aims whenever a target is in the FOV. Button modes add a movable on-screen AIM button.");
		aa.Dropdown("Aim Mode", {"Linear","Lock","Spring","AI"}, "Linear", function(v)
			A.mode = v;
			S.vy, S.vp = 0, 0;
		end);
		aa.Slider("Smoothing", 0, 1, 0, function(v)
			A.smooth = v;
		end, 0.01);
		aa.Hint("0 = snap onto the target, 1 = soft follow.");
		aa.Section("Targeting");
		aa.Slider("FOV Radius", 20, 600, 120, function(v)
			A.fov = v;
		end, 5);
		aa.Toggle("Visible Check (Walls)", true, function(v)
			A.wall = v;
