		aa.Toggle("Target Teammates", false, function(v)
			A.team = v;
		end, true);
		aa.Section("Timing");
		aa.Slider("Aim Reaction (ms)", 0, 500, 0, function(v)
			A.aReact = v;
		end, 5);
		aa.Slider("Switch Target (ms)", 0, 1000, 0, function(v)
			A.aSwitch = v;
		end, 10);
		aa.Section("FOV Circle");
		aa.Toggle("Show FOV Circle", true, function(v)
			A.show = v;
		end, true);
		aa.Toggle("FOV Fill", false, function(v)
			A.fill = v;
		end, true);
		aa.Dropdown("Fill Animation", {"None","Pulse","Rainbow","Lock Flash"}, "None", function(v)
			A.anim = v;
		end);
		aa.ColorWheel("FOV Color", "#F03E3E");
		local sa = addSub(mCombat, "Silent Aim", "Redirects your shots without moving your view.");
		local W = {};
		sa.Section("Silent Aim");
		sa.Toggle("Silent Aim", false, function(v)
			A.silent = v;
			if M.syncHook then
				M.syncHook();
			end
		end);
		sa.Dropdown("Mode", {"Silent","Camera"}, "Silent", function(v)
			A.sMode = v;
			if M.syncHook then
				M.syncHook();
			end
		end);
		sa.Toggle("Ignore FOV", false, function(v)
			A.ignoreFov = v;
		end, true);
		sa.Hint("Silent = shots only. Camera = Legit-style camera aim.");
		sa.Section("Hit Settings");
		sa.Slider("Hit Chance (%)", 0, 100, 100, function(v)
			A.hitChance = v;
		end, 1);
		sa.Dropdown("Hit Part", {"Head","Torso","Random","Weighted"}, "Head", function(v)
			A.part = v;
			if M.syncWeighted then
				M.syncWeighted();
			end
		end);
		W.head = sa.Slider("Head Hit Chance", 0, 100, 70, function(v)
			A.wHead = v;
		end, 1);
		W.torso = sa.Slider("Torso Hit Chance", 0, 100, 30, function(v)
			A.wTorso = v;
		end, 1);
		sa.Toggle("Wallbang", false, function(v)
			A.wallbang = v;
		end, true);
		sa.Hint("Wallbang: targets behind walls count as visible.");
		sa.Section("FOV Circle");
		sa.Toggle("FOV Circle", true, function(v)
			A.sShow = v;
		end, true);
		sa.Slider("Circle Radius", 20, 600, 120, function(v)
			A.sFov = v;
		end, 5);
		sa.ColorWheel("Circle Color", "#F03E3E");
		sa.Toggle("FOV Fill", false, function(v)
			A.sFill = v;
		end, true);
		sa.Toggle("Gradient Fill", false, function(v)
			A.sGrad = v;
		end, true);
		sa.ColorWheel("Gradient Color", "#3E7BF0");
		sa.Section("Legit");
		sa.Toggle("Legit Aim Assist", false, function(v)
			A.legit = v;
		end);
		sa.Slider("Aim Reaction (ms)", 0, 500, 0, function(v)
			A.lReact = v;
		end, 5);
		sa.Slider("Switch Target (ms)", 0, 1000, 0, function(v)
			A.lSwitch = v;
		end, 10);
		sa.Hint("Uses this tab's FOV circle, radius and Hit Part.");
		M.syncWeighted = function()
			local on = A.part == "Weighted";
			for _, h in pairs(W) do
				if (h and h.Frame) then
					h.Frame:SetAttribute("Hid", not on);
					h.Frame.Visible = on;
				end
			end
		end;
		M.syncWeighted();
		local aimBtn = FX.MB.new("aim", "AIM", 0.88, 0.5);
		FX.MB.aim = aimBtn;
		aimBtn.kind = ((A.act == "Button (Toggle)") and "toggle") or "hold";
		aimBtn.want = function()
			return (A.on or A.legit or (A.silent and (A.sMode == "Camera"))) and ((A.act == "Button (Hold)") or (A.act == "Button (Toggle)"));
		end;
		local gGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui");
		gGui.Name = "1MagFov";
		gGui.ResetOnSpawn = false;
		gGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true;
		gGui.DisplayOrder = 1;
		pcall(function()
			gGui.Parent = gui.Parent;
		end);
		local function mkCircle(filled)
			local f = Instance.new("Frame");
			f.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5);
			f.BorderSizePixel = 0;
			f.Visible = false;
			f.Active = false;
			f.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1);
			f.BackgroundTransparency = (filled and 0.8) or 1;
			f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 10);
			f.Parent = gGui;
			Instance.new("UICorner", f).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0);
			local st;
			if not filled then
				st = Instance.new("UIStroke");
				st.Thickness = 1.5;
				st.Parent = f;
			end
			return f, st;
		end
		local ring, ringSt = mkCircle(false);
		local fillC = mkCircle(true);
		local ring2, ring2St = mkCircle(false);
		local fill2 = mkCircle(true);
		local gFill = mkCircle(true);
		local gGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient");
		gGrad.Parent = gFill;
		local function wrap(a)
			return ((a + math.pi) % (2 * math.pi)) - math.pi;
		end
		M.partFor = function(ch, mode)
			mode = mode or A.part;
			local head = ch:FindFirstChild("Head");
			local torso = ch:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or ch:FindFirstChild("Torso");
			local root = ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart");
			if (mode == "Head") then
				return head or root;
			end
			if (mode == "Torso") then
				return torso or root;
			end
			local r = S.rand[ch];
			if (r and r.Parent) then
				return r;
			end
			if (mode == "Weighted") then
				local total = A.wHead + A.wTorso;
				if (total <= 0) then
					r = head or root;
				elseif ((math.random() * total) <= A.wHead) then
					r = head or root;
				else
					r = torso or root;
				end
			else
				local list = {};
				for _, x in ipairs({head,torso}) do
					if x then
						list[#list + 1] = x;
					end
				end
				r = list[math.random(math.max(#list, 1))] or root;
			end
			S.rand[ch] = r;
			return r;
		end;
		M.valid = function(plr, c, partMode)
			if (plr == player) then
				return nil;
			end
			if not (A.team or (plr.Team == nil) or (plr.Team ~= player.Team)) then
				return nil;
			end
			local ch = plr.Character;
			local hum = ch and ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid");
			local part = ch and M.partFor(ch, partMode);
			if not (hum and part and (hum.Health > 0)) then
				return nil;
			end
			if ((part.Transparency > 0.95) and (part.LocalTransparencyModifier > 0.95)) then
				return nil;
			end
			return ch, part;
		end;
		M.visible = function(c, ch, part, wall)
			if not wall then
				return true;
			end
			local hit = workspace:Raycast(c.CFrame.Position, part.Position - c.CFrame.Position, params);
			return (hit == nil) or hit.Instance:IsDescendantOf(ch);
		end;
		M.inFov = function(c, part, center, fov, ignore)
			local p = c:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position);
			if (p.Z <= 0) then
				if ignore then
					return 100000 + (part.Position - c.CFrame.Position).Magnitude;
				end
				return nil;
			end
			local d = (Vector2.new(p.X, p.Y) - center).Magnitude;
			if (ignore or (d <= (fov or A.fov))) then
				return d;
			end
		end;
		M.pick = function(c, center, prefer, o)
			params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {(player.Character or c),c};
			local cur = prefer;
			if (prefer == nil) then
				cur = S.target;
			end
			if (cur and cur.Parent) then
				local plr = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(cur.Parent);
				if plr then
					local ch, part = M.valid(plr, c, o.part);
					if (ch and (part == cur) and M.inFov(c, part, center, o.fov, o.ignore) and M.visible(c, ch, part, o.wall)) then
						return part;
					end
				end
			end
			local best, bestD;
			for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				local ch, part = M.valid(plr, c, o.part);
				if ch then
					local d = M.inFov(c, part, center, o.fov, o.ignore);
					if (d and (not bestD or (d < bestD)) and M.visible(c, ch, part, o.wall)) then
						best, bestD = part, d;
					end
				end
			end
			return best;
		end;
		M.step = function(ey, ep, dt, now)
			local s = A.smooth;
			local err = math.sqrt((ey * ey) + (ep * ep));
			if ((s <= 0.001) or (err < 0.00001)) then
				return ey, ep;
			end
			local mode = A.mode;
			local f;
			if (mode == "Linear") then
				local speed = math.rad(1500 + ((90 - 1500) * s));
				f = math.min(1, (speed * dt) / err);
			elseif (mode == "Lock") then
				local alpha = 1 + ((0.1 - 1) * s);
				f = 1 - ((1 - alpha) ^ (dt * 60));
			elseif (mode == "Spring") then
				local d = math.min(dt, 1 / 45);
				local k = 2500 + ((60 - 2500) * (s ^ 0.7));
				local c = 2 * math.sqrt(k) * 0.85;
				S.vy += (((ey * k) - (S.vy * c)) * d)
				S.vp += (((ep * k) - (S.vp * c)) * d)
				return S.vy * d, S.vp * d;
			else
				if ((now - S.since) < (0.04 + (0.12 * s))) then
					return 0, 0;
				end
				local t = math.clamp(math.deg(err) / 20, 0, 1);
				local ease = 0.35 + (0.65 * t * t * (3 - (2 * t)));
				f = math.min(1, (0.9 + ((0.08 - 0.9) * s)) * ease * dt * 60);
				local w = math.rad(0.12) * s;
				return (ey * f) + (math.sin(now * 7.3) * w * dt * 60), (ep * f) + (math.cos(now * 5.1) * w * dt * 60);
			end
			return ey * f, ep * f;
		end;
		M.syncViewmodels = function(c, delta)
			local function pivot(m)
				if (m:IsA("Model") and not m:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")) then
					pcall(function()
						m:PivotTo(delta * m:GetPivot());
					end);
				end
			end
			local function scan(root)
				for _, ch in ipairs(root:GetChildren()) do
					if ch:IsA("Folder") then
						for _, m in ipairs(ch:GetChildren()) do
							pivot(m);
						end
					else
						pivot(ch);
					end
				end
			end
			scan(c);
			local vm = workspace:FindFirstChild("ViewModels") or workspace:FindFirstChild("Viewmodels");
			if vm then
				scan(vm);
			end
		end;
		local function hexC(k, d)
			local ok, c = pcall(Color3.fromHex, CONFIG_VALUES[k] or d);
			if ok then
				return c;
			end
			return Color3.fromHex(d);
		end
		local function place(f, center, r)
			f.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(center.X, center.Y);
			f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(r * 2, r * 2);
		end
		local function updateCircle(center, now)
			local col = hexC("Combat.Aim Assist.FOV Circle.FOV Color", "#F03E3E");
			local op = 0.18;
			if (A.anim == "Rainbow") then
				col = Color3.fromHSV((now * 0.3) % 1, 0.85, 1);
			elseif (A.anim == "Pulse") then
				op = 0.18 + (0.14 * math.sin(now * 3.2));
			elseif (A.anim == "Lock Flash") then
				op = 0.1 + (0.4 * S.flash);
			end
			op = math.clamp(op, 0.03, 0.9);
			local aimShow = A.show and A.on;
			ring.Visible = aimShow;
			fillC.Visible = aimShow and A.fill;
			if aimShow then
				place(ring, center, A.fov);
				ringSt.Color = col;
				place(fillC, center, A.fov);
				fillC.BackgroundColor3 = col;
				fillC.BackgroundTransparency = 1 - op;
			end
			local silShow = A.sShow and (A.silent or A.legit) and not A.ignoreFov;
			local c1 = hexC("Combat.Silent Aim.FOV Circle.Circle Color", "#F03E3E");
			local c2 = hexC("Combat.Silent Aim.FOV Circle.Gradient Color", "#3E7BF0");
			ring2.Visible = silShow;
			if silShow then
				place(ring2, center, A.sFov);
				ring2St.Color = c1;
			end
			local grad = silShow and A.sGrad;
			fill2.Visible = silShow and A.sFill and not grad;
			if fill2.Visible then
				place(fill2, center, A.sFov);
				fill2.BackgroundColor3 = c1;
				fill2.BackgroundTransparency = 0.8;
			end
			gFill.Visible = (grad and true) or false;
			if grad then
				place(gFill, center, A.sFov);
				gFill.BackgroundTransparency = 0.7;
				gGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new(c1, c2);
				gGrad.Rotation = (now * 60) % 360;
			end
		end
		local HARD_BLOCK = {Warper=true,Medkit=true,["Subspace Tripmine"]=true,Warpstone=true,Flashbang=true,Grenade=true,["Jump Pad"]=true,Molotov=true,Satchel=true,["Smoke Grenade"]=true,["War Horn"]=true,Grappler=true,Flamethrower=true};
		M.fighterCtrl = function()
			if not S.fc then
				pcall(function()
					S.fc = require(player.PlayerScripts.Controllers.FighterController);
				end);
			end
			return S.fc;
		end;
		M.localFighter = function()
			local fc = M.fighterCtrl();
			if not fc then
				return nil;
			end
			local f = fc.LocalFighter;
			if (not f and (type(fc.GetFighter) == "function")) then
				local ok, r = pcall(fc.GetFighter, fc, player);
				f = (ok and r) or nil;
			end
			return f;
		end;
		M.localItem = function()
			local f = M.localFighter();
			return (f and f.EquippedItem) or nil;
		end;
		M.ready = function()
			local c = workspace.CurrentCamera;
			if not c then
				return false;
			end
			local hum = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid");
			if not (hum and (hum.Health > 0)) then
				return false;
			end
			local f = M.localFighter();
			local entity = f and f.Entity;
			if ((type(entity) == "table") and (rawget(entity, "_is_in_world") ~= true)) then
				return false;
			end
			return true;
		end;
		M.shielded = function(plr)
			local fc = M.fighterCtrl();
			if not (plr and fc and (type(fc.GetFighter) == "function")) then
				return false;
			end
			local ok, f = pcall(fc.GetFighter, fc, plr);
			local entity = ok and f and f.Entity;
			local data = entity and entity.Data;
			return (type(data) == "table") and (data.IsInvincible == true);
		end;
		M.hookable = function(item)
			if ((type(item) ~= "table") or HARD_BLOCK[item.Name] or (type(item.StartShooting) ~= "function")) then
				return false;
			end
			local info = item.Info;
			if not info then
				return (item.Name == "Daggers") or (item.Name == "Bow") or (item.Name == "Slingshot");
			end
			local timed = (type(info.ShootCooldown) == "number") or (type(info.ShootBurstCooldown) == "number") or (type(info.ShootBurst) == "number") or (type(info.QuickShotCooldown) == "number") or (type(info.ChargeReleaseCooldown) == "number") or (type(info.BladeCooldown) == "number");
			local melee = (type(info.AttackCooldown) == "number") and ((info.Class == "Melee") or (info.Type == "Melee"));
			return timed or melee or (item.Name == "Daggers") or (item.Name == "Bow") or (item.Name == "Slingshot");
		end;
		M.cacheWindow = function(item)
			local info, w = item and item.Info, math.huge;
			for _, v in ipairs({(info and info.ShootBurstCooldown),(info and info.QuickShotCooldown),(info and info.ShootCooldown),(info and info.ChargeReleaseCooldown)}) do
				if ((type(v) == "number") and (v > 0)) then
					w = math.min(w, v);
				end
			end
			if (w == math.huge) then
				return 1 / 30;
			end
			return math.clamp(w, 1 / 60, 0.08);
		end;
		M.resolveHookTarget = function(item, c)
			local center = c.ViewportSize / 2;
			params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {(player.Character or c),c};
			local cur, now = S.hook, os.clock();
			if (cur and cur.Parent and ((now - S.hookAt) <= M.cacheWindow(item))) then
				local plr = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(cur.Parent);
				local ch, part = nil, nil;
				if plr then
					ch, part = M.valid(plr, c, A.part);
				end
				if (ch and M.inFov(c, part, center, A.sFov, A.ignoreFov) and M.visible(c, ch, part, not A.wallbang)) then
					S.hook = part;
					return part;
				end
			end
			S.rand = setmetatable({}, {__mode="k"});
			local part = M.pick(c, center, false, {fov=A.sFov,ignore=A.ignoreFov,part=A.part,wall=not A.wallbang});
			S.hook, S.hookAt = part, (part and now) or 0;
			return part;
		end;
		M.prime = function(part, c)
			if not M.ready() then
				return false;
			end
			if not (part and part.Parent and c) then
				return false;
			end
			c.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(c.CFrame.Position, part.Position);
			return true;
		end;
		M.BeginSilentShot = function(item)
			if ((S.depth > 0) and ((os.clock() - S.depthAt) > 1)) then
				S.depth = 0;
			end
			if (S.depth > 0) then
				return false, nil, nil;
			end
			if not (A.silent and (A.sMode == "Silent") and M.ready()) then
				return false, nil, nil;
			end
			if ((A.hitChance < 100) and ((math.random() * 100) > A.hitChance)) then
				return false, nil, nil;
			end
			S.rand = setmetatable({}, {__mode="k"});
			local c = workspace.CurrentCamera;
			local part = c and M.resolveHookTarget(item, c);
			if not part then
				return false, nil, nil;
			end
			local plr = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(part.Parent);
			if (plr and M.shielded(plr)) then
				return false, nil, nil;
			end
			local original = c.CFrame;
			if not M.prime(part, c) then
				return false, nil, nil;
			end
			S.depth += 1
			S.depthAt = os.clock();
			return true, c, original;
		end;
		M.FinishSilentShot = function(didPrime, c, original)
			if not didPrime then
				return;
			end
			S.depth = math.max(S.depth - 1, 0);
			if (c and original and (workspace.CurrentCamera == c) and M.ready()) then
				c.CFrame = original;
			end
		end;
		M.unhook = function()
			local item = S.hooked;
			if not item then
				return;
			end
			if (rawget(item, "StartShooting") == S.hookWrap) then
				pcall(function()
					if S.hookHad then
						item.StartShooting = S.hookOrig;
					else
						rawset(item, "StartShooting", nil);
					end
				end);
			end
			S.hooked, S.hookOrig, S.hookWrap = nil, nil, nil;
		end;
		M.ensureHook = function(item)
			if ((S.hooked == item) or not M.hookable(item)) then
				return;
			end
			M.unhook();
			local orig = item.StartShooting;
			if S.wrapSet[orig] then
				S.hooked = item;
				return;
			end
			local function wrapped(self, ...)
				local primed, c, original = M.BeginSilentShot(self);
				local results = table.pack(pcall(orig, self, ...));
				M.FinishSilentShot(primed, c, original);
				if not results[1] then
					warn("[1Mag] silent_start_shooting: " .. tostring(results[2]));
					return nil;
				end
				return table.unpack(results, 2, results.n);
			end
			S.wrapSet[wrapped] = true;
			S.hookHad = rawget(item, "StartShooting") ~= nil;
			local ok = pcall(function()
				item.StartShooting = wrapped;
			end);
			if ok then
				S.hooked, S.hookOrig, S.hookWrap = item, orig, wrapped;
			end
		end;
		M.syncHook = function()
			if not (A.silent and (A.sMode == "Silent")) then
				M.unhook();
				S.hook = nil;
			end
		end;
		local function aimStep(dt)
			local c = workspace.CurrentCamera;
			if not c then
				ring.Visible = false;
				fillC.Visible = false;
				ring2.Visible = false;
				fill2.Visible = false;
				gFill.Visible = false;
				return;
			end
			local center = c.ViewportSize / 2;
			local now = os.clock();
			local held;
			if (A.act == "Always On") then
				held = true;
			elseif (A.act == "Button (Hold)") then
				held = aimBtn.down;
			elseif (A.act == "Button (Toggle)") then
				held = aimBtn.on;
			else
				held = true;
			end
			if (A.silent and (A.sMode == "Silent")) then
				local item = M.localItem();
				if item then
					M.ensureHook(item);
				end
			end
			local o;
			if A.on then
				o = {fov=A.fov,ignore=false,part="Head",wall=A.wall,react=A.aReact,switch=A.aSwitch};
			elseif (A.legit or (A.silent and (A.sMode == "Camera"))) then
				o = {fov=A.sFov,ignore=A.ignoreFov,part=A.part,wall=not A.wallbang,react=A.lReact,switch=A.lSwitch};
			end
			local cand = (o and held and M.pick(c, center, nil, o)) or nil;
			local target = S.target;
			if (cand ~= target) then
				if not cand then
					if target then
						S.lostAt = now;
					end
					target, S.pend = nil, nil;
				else
					if (S.pend ~= cand) then
						S.pend, S.pendAt = cand, now;
						S.pendDelay = ((target or ((now - S.lostAt) < 0.35)) and o.switch) or o.react;
					end
					if ((now - S.pendAt) >= (S.pendDelay / 1000)) then
						target, S.pend = cand, nil;
					else
						if target then
							S.lostAt = now;
						end
						target = nil;
					end
				end
			end
			if (target ~= S.target) then
				S.target = target;
				S.since = now;
				S.vy, S.vp = 0, 0;
				S.rand = setmetatable({}, {__mode="k"});
			end
			S.flash += ((((target and 1) or 0) - S.flash) * math.min(1, dt * 10))
			pcall(updateCircle, center, now);
			if not target then
				return;
			end
			local pos = target.Position;
			if (A.mode == "AI") then
				local last = S.vel[target];
				local v = (last and ((pos - last.p) / math.max(now - last.t, 0.0010000000474974513))) or Vector3.zero;
				if last then
					v = last.v:Lerp(v, 0.3);
				end
				S.vel[target] = {p=pos,t=now,v=v};
				pos = pos + (v * 0.06);
			end
			local cf = c.CFrame;
			local px, py, pz = cf:ToOrientation();
			local d = pos - cf.Position;
			if (d.Magnitude < 0.0010000000474974513) then
				return;
			end
			d = d.Unit;
			local ey = wrap(math.atan2(-d.X, -d.Z) - py);
			local ep = math.asin(math.clamp(d.Y, -1, 1)) - px;
			local dy, dp = M.step(ey, ep, dt, now);
			local newCF = CFrame.new(cf.Position) * CFrame.fromOrientation(math.clamp(px + dp, -1.5, 1.5), py + dy, pz);
			c.CFrame = newCF;
			M.syncViewmodels(c, newCF * cf:Inverse());
		end
		pcall(RunService.UnbindFromRenderStep, RunService, "1MagAim");
		RunService:BindToRenderStep("1MagAim", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 1, function(dt)
			local ok, err = pcall(aimStep, dt);
			if not ok then
				S.errs = (S.errs or 0) + 1;
				if (S.errs <= 3) then
					warn("[1Mag] aim step error: " .. tostring(err));
				end
			end
		end);
		gui.Destroying:Connect(function()
			pcall(RunService.UnbindFromRenderStep, RunService, "1MagAim");
			M.unhook();
			pcall(function()
				gGui:Destroy();
			end);
		end);
		do
			local fireOk, fireErr = pcall(function()
				local TB = {on=false,act="Hold",react=0,release=0,part="Head + Body",team=false,since=nil,keep=0,armed=false};
				local FB = {on=false,shoot=false,delay=0,cool=250,dur=110,curve=12,human=30,fov=150,part="Head",wall=true,state="Idle",t=0,timer=0,target=nil,y0=0,p0=0,side=1,ph1=0,ph2=0};
				local TKEY, FKEY = "Combat.Triggerbot.Trigger.Trigger Key", "Combat.Flickbot.Flick.Flick Key";
				local function keyName(k, d)
					local v = CONFIG_VALUES[k];
					return ((type(v) == "string") and (v ~= "") and (v ~= "None") and v) or d;
				end
				local function keyDown(name)
					local ok, kc = pcall(function()
						return Enum.KeyCode[name];
					end);
					return (ok and kc and UIS:IsKeyDown(kc)) or false;
				end
				local ta = addSub(mCombat, "Triggerbot", "Fires automatically while your crosshair is on an enemy.");
				ta.Section("Trigger");
				ta.Toggle("Triggerbot", false, function(v)
					TB.on = v;
					TB.since = nil;
					TB.keep = 0;
				end);
				ta.Dropdown("Activation", {"Always","Hold","Toggle"}, "Hold", function(v)
					TB.act = v;
					TB.armed = false;
				end);
				ta.Keybind("Trigger Key", "T");
				ta.Hint("Always fires whenever you aim at an enemy. Hold / Toggle use the key above.");
				ta.Section("Targeting");
				ta.Dropdown("Hit Parts", {"Head","Body","Head + Body","Any"}, "Head + Body", function(v)
					TB.part = v;
				end);
				ta.Toggle("Target Teammates", false, function(v)
					TB.team = v;
				end, true);
				ta.Section("Timing");
				ta.Slider("Reaction Delay (s)", 0, 2, 0, function(v)
					TB.react = v;
				end, 0.01);
				ta.Slider("Release Delay (s)", 0, 2, 0, function(v)
					TB.release = v;
				end, 0.01);
				ta.Hint("Reaction = how long the crosshair must stay on target before firing. Release = how long it keeps firing after leaving.");
				local fa = addSub(mCombat, "Flickbot", "Press a key to snap onto the best target with a fast, curved camera flick.");
				fa.Section("Flick");
				fa.Toggle("Flickbot", false, function(v)
					FB.on = v;
				end);
				fa.Keybind("Flick Key", "Q");
				fa.Slider("Flick FOV", 20, 600, 150, function(v)
					FB.fov = v;
				end, 5);
				fa.Dropdown("Hit Part", {"Head","Torso"}, "Head", function(v)
					FB.part = v;
				end);
				fa.Toggle("Visible Check (Walls)", true, function(v)
					FB.wall = v;
				end, true);
				fa.Section("Shooting");
				fa.Toggle("Shoot", false, function(v)
					FB.shoot = v;
				end);
				fa.Slider("Shot Delay (ms)", 0, 250, 0, function(v)
					FB.delay = v;
				end, 1);
				fa.Slider("Cooldown (ms)", 0, 2000, 250, function(v)
					FB.cool = v;
				end, 10);
				fa.Section("Motion");
				fa.Slider("Flick Duration (ms)", 30, 400, 110, function(v)
					FB.dur = v;
				end, 5);
				fa.Slider("Curvature", 0, 50, 12, function(v)
					FB.curve = v;
				end, 1);
				fa.Slider("Humanness", 0, 100, 30, function(v)
					FB.human = v;
				end, 1);
				fa.Hint("Curvature bends the path, Humanness adds natural wobble and uneven speed. One flick per key press.");
				local rp = RaycastParams.new();
				rp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude;
				local function partOk(name)
					local m = TB.part;
					if (m == "Any") then
						return true;
					end
					local head = name == "Head";
					if (m == "Head") then
						return head;
					end
					if (m == "Body") then
						return not head;
					end
					return true;
				end
				local function crosshairHit(c)
					local ctr = c.ViewportSize / 2;
					local ray = c:ViewportPointToRay(ctr.X, ctr.Y);
					rp.FilterDescendantsInstances = {(player.Character or c),c};
					local hit = workspace:Raycast(ray.Origin, ray.Direction * 2000, rp);
					if not hit then
						return false;
					end
					local ch = hit.Instance:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model");
					local plr = ch and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(ch);
					if (not plr or (plr == player)) then
						return false;
					end
					if not (TB.team or (plr.Team == nil) or (plr.Team ~= player.Team)) then
						return false;
					end
					local hum = ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid");
					if (not hum or (hum.Health <= 0)) then
						return false;
					end
					if M.shielded(plr) then
						return false;
					end
					return partOk(hit.Instance.Name);
				end
				local function fire()
					local item = M.localItem();
					if (item and M.hookable(item)) then
						pcall(item.StartShooting, item);
					end
				end
				local function triggerStep(c, dt)
					if not (TB.on and M.ready()) then
						TB.since = nil;
						TB.keep = 0;
						return;
					end
					local active = (TB.act == "Always") or ((TB.act == "Hold") and keyDown(keyName(TKEY, "T"))) or ((TB.act == "Toggle") and TB.armed);
					local item = M.localItem();
					if not (active and item and M.hookable(item)) then
						TB.since = nil;
						TB.keep = 0;
						return;
					end
					if crosshairHit(c) then
						TB.since = (TB.since or 0) + ((TB.since and dt) or 0);
						if (TB.since >= TB.react) then
							TB.keep = TB.release;
							fire();
							return;
						end
					else
						TB.since = nil;
					end
					TB.keep = math.max(0, TB.keep - dt);
					if (TB.keep > 0) then
						fire();
					end
				end
				local function wrapA(a)
					return ((a + math.pi) % (2 * math.pi)) - math.pi;
				end
				local function angTo(c, pos)
					local d = pos - c.CFrame.Position;
					if (d.Magnitude < 0.0010000000474974513) then
						return nil;
					end
					d = d.Unit;
					return math.atan2(-d.X, -d.Z), math.asin(math.clamp(d.Y, -1, 1));
				end
				local function setAim(c, yaw, pitch)
					local cf = c.CFrame;
					local _, _, pz = cf:ToOrientation();
					local newCF = CFrame.new(cf.Position) * CFrame.fromOrientation(math.clamp(pitch, -1.5, 1.5), yaw, pz);
					c.CFrame = newCF;
					M.syncViewmodels(c, newCF * cf:Inverse());
				end
				local function finish()
					FB.target = nil;
					if (FB.cool > 0) then
						FB.state = "Cooldown";
						FB.timer = FB.cool / 1000;
					else
						FB.state = "Idle";
					end
				end
				local function startFlick()
					if not (FB.on and (FB.state == "Idle") and M.ready()) then
						return;
					end
					local c = workspace.CurrentCamera;
					if not c then
						return;
					end
					local part = M.pick(c, c.ViewportSize / 2, false, {fov=FB.fov,ignore=false,part=FB.part,wall=FB.wall});
					if not part then
						return;
					end
					local px, py = c.CFrame:ToOrientation();
					FB.p0, FB.y0 = px, py;
					FB.target, FB.t, FB.state = part, 0, "Flicking";
					FB.side = ((math.random() < 0.5) and -1) or 1;
					FB.ph1, FB.ph2 = math.random() * 6.28, math.random() * 6.28;
				end
				local function flickStep(c, dt)
					if (FB.state == "Idle") then
						return;
					end
					if (FB.state == "Cooldown") then
						FB.timer -= dt
						if (FB.timer <= 0) then
							FB.state = "Idle";
						end
						return;
					end
					if (FB.state == "PostShot") then
						FB.timer -= dt
						if (FB.timer <= 0) then
							fire();
							finish();
						end
						return;
					end
					local part = FB.target;
					if not (part and part.Parent and M.ready()) then
						finish();
						return;
					end
					local ty, tp = angTo(c, part.Position);
					if not ty then
						finish();
						return;
					end
					FB.t += (dt * 1000)
					local u = math.clamp(FB.t / math.max(FB.dur, 1), 0, 1);
					local h = FB.human / 100;
					local pw = 1 + (h * 0.6);
					local e = u ^ pw;
					e = e * e * e * ((e * ((e * 6) - 15)) + 10);
					local dy, dp = wrapA(ty - FB.y0), tp - FB.p0;
					local dist = math.sqrt((dy * dy) + (dp * dp));
					local arc = math.sin(math.pi * u);
					local curve = math.rad(FB.curve) * math.min(1, dist / math.rad(40)) * arc * FB.side;
					local wob = math.rad(1.2) * h * math.min(1, dist / math.rad(30)) * arc;
					local yaw = FB.y0 + (dy * e) + ((-dp / math.max(dist, 0.0001)) * curve) + (math.sin((u * 9) + FB.ph1) * wob);
					local pit = FB.p0 + (dp * e) + ((dy / math.max(dist, 0.0001)) * curve) + (math.sin((u * 7) + FB.ph2) * wob);
					if (u >= 1) then
						yaw, pit = ty, tp;
					end
					setAim(c, yaw, pit);
					if (u >= 1) then
						if FB.shoot then
							FB.state = "PostShot";
							FB.timer = FB.delay / 1000;
						else
							finish();
						end
					end
				end
				local conns = {};
				conns[#conns + 1] = UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(i, gp)
					if (gp or (i.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard)) then
						return;
					end
					if (TB.on and (TB.act == "Toggle") and (i.KeyCode.Name == keyName(TKEY, "T"))) then
						TB.armed = not TB.armed;
					end
					if (i.KeyCode.Name == keyName(FKEY, "Q")) then
						pcall(startFlick);
					end
				end);
				pcall(RunService.UnbindFromRenderStep, RunService, "1MagFire");
				RunService:BindToRenderStep("1MagFire", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 2, function(dt)
					local c = workspace.CurrentCamera;
					if not c then
						return;
					end
					local ok, err = pcall(function()
						flickStep(c, dt);
						triggerStep(c, dt);
					end);
					if not ok then
						FB.errs = (FB.errs or 0) + 1;
						if (FB.errs <= 3) then
							warn("[1Mag] fire assist error: " .. tostring(err));
						end
					end
				end);
				gui.Destroying:Connect(function()
					pcall(RunService.UnbindFromRenderStep, RunService, "1MagFire");
					for _, cn in ipairs(conns) do
						pcall(function()
							cn:Disconnect();
						end);
					end
				end);
			end);
			if not fireOk then
				warn("[1Mag] Fire Assist failed to load: " .. tostring(fireErr));
			end
		end
		do
			local wh2Ok, wh2Err = pcall(function()
				local WH = {fire={on=false,pct=25},recoil={on=false,pct=75},aim={on=false,pct=300},melee={on=false,pct=25},dash={on=false,pct=25},auto=false,dj=false,motion=false};
				local ORIG, TOUCHED, dirty, lastItem = setmetatable({}, {__mode="k"}), setmetatable({}, {__mode="k"}), true, nil;
				local SPRINGS = {_sliding_spring=0,_sprinting_spring=0,_bobbing_speed_spring=0,_bobbing_value_spring=Vector2.zero,_landing_spring=0,_jump_spring=0,_tilt_spring=Vector2.zero,_impulse_position_spring=Vector3.zero,_recoil_spring=Vector3.zero,_unrecoil_spring=Vector3.zero};
				local function mark()
					dirty = true;
				end
				local wa = addSub(mCombat, "Weapon Handling", "Cooldowns, recoil, aim speed, and movement tweaks for the weapon you hold.");
				wa.Section("Firing");
				wa.Toggle("Fire Cooldown", false, function(v)
					WH.fire.on = v;
					mark();
				end);
				wa.Slider("Fire Cooldown %", 5, 100, 25, function(v)
					WH.fire.pct = v;
					mark();
				end, 1);
				wa.Toggle("Recoil Reduction", false, function(v)
					WH.recoil.on = v;
					mark();
				end);
				wa.Slider("Recoil Reduced By %", 0, 100, 75, function(v)
					WH.recoil.pct = v;
					mark();
				end, 1);
				wa.Toggle("Automatic Fire", false, function(v)
					WH.auto = v;
					mark();
				end);
				wa.Section("Aiming");
				wa.Toggle("Aim Speed", false, function(v)
					WH.aim.on = v;
					mark();
				end);
				wa.Slider("Aim Speed %", 100, 1000, 300, function(v)
					WH.aim.pct = v;
					mark();
				end, 10);
				wa.Toggle("Remove Weapon Motion", false, function(v)
					WH.motion = v;
					mark();
				end);
				wa.Hint("Removes viewmodel sway, bob, tilt and recoil motion.");
				wa.Section("Melee and Movement");
				wa.Toggle("Melee Cooldown", false, function(v)
					WH.melee.on = v;
					mark();
				end);
				wa.Slider("Melee Cooldown %", 5, 100, 25, function(v)
					WH.melee.pct = v;
					mark();
				end, 1);
				wa.Toggle("Dash Cooldown", false, function(v)
					WH.dash.on = v;
					mark();
				end);
				wa.Slider("Dash Cooldown %", 5, 100, 25, function(v)
					WH.dash.pct = v;
					mark();
				end, 1);
				wa.Toggle("Infinite Double Jumps", false, function(v)
					WH.dj = v;
					mark();
				end);
				wa.Hint("Percent sliders: 25% fire cooldown means the weapon fires 4x as often. Changes apply to the weapon you are holding and swap back when turned off.");
				local function rget(t, k)
					local ok, v = pcall(rawget, t, k);
					if ok then
						return v;
					end
				end
				local function setField(t, k, v)
					if (type(setreadonly) == "function") then
						pcall(setreadonly, t, false);
					end
					if not pcall(rawset, t, k, v) then
						pcall(function()
							t[k] = v;
						end);
					end
				end
				local function applyItem(item)
					if (type(item) ~= "table") then
						return;
					end
					local info = rget(item, "Info") or item.Info;
					if (type(info) ~= "table") then
						return;
					end
					local o = ORIG[item];
					if not o then
						o = {};
						for _, k in ipairs({"ShootCooldown","AimSpeed","AttackCooldown","DashCooldown","ShootRecoil","MaxDoubleJumps"}) do
							o[k] = rget(info, k);
						end
						local isp = rget(info, "InputSpammingEnabled");
						if (type(isp) == "table") then
							o.InputSpammingEnabled = table.clone(isp);
						end
						ORIG[item] = o;
					end
					TOUCHED[item] = true;
					local function mul(k, on, f)
						local base = o[k];
						if (base == nil) then
							return;
						end
						if (on and (type(base) == "number")) then
							setField(info, k, base * f);
						else
							setField(info, k, base);
						end
					end
					mul("ShootCooldown", WH.fire.on, WH.fire.pct / 100);
					mul("AimSpeed", WH.aim.on, WH.aim.pct / 100);
					mul("AttackCooldown", WH.melee.on, WH.melee.pct / 100);
					mul("DashCooldown", WH.dash.on, WH.dash.pct / 100);
					if (o.ShootRecoil ~= nil) then
						if WH.recoil.on then
							local f = 1 - (WH.recoil.pct / 100);
							local b = o.ShootRecoil;
							local ok, r = pcall(function()
								return b * f;
							end);
							if ok then
								setField(info, "ShootRecoil", r);
							end
						else
							setField(info, "ShootRecoil", o.ShootRecoil);
						end
					end
					if (o.MaxDoubleJumps ~= nil) then
						setField(info, "MaxDoubleJumps", (WH.dj and 676767) or o.MaxDoubleJumps);
					end
					if o.InputSpammingEnabled then
						if WH.auto then
							local c = table.clone(o.InputSpammingEnabled);
							c.StartShooting = 0;
							c.StartReloading = 0;
							setField(info, "InputSpammingEnabled", c);
						else
							setField(info, "InputSpammingEnabled", o.InputSpammingEnabled);
						end
					end
					local vm = rget(item, "ViewModel");
					if (type(vm) == "table") then
						for k, val in pairs(SPRINGS) do
							local sp = rget(vm, k);
							if (type(sp) == "table") then
								setField(sp, "Value", (WH.motion and val) or nil);
							end
						end
					end
				end
				local function allItems()
					local list = {};
					local f = M.localFighter();
					if f then
						local ok, items = pcall(function()
							if (type(f.GetItems) == "function") then
								return f:GetItems();
							end
							return f.Items;
						end);
						if (ok and (type(items) == "table")) then
							for _, it in pairs(items) do
								if (type(it) == "table") then
									list[#list + 1] = it;
								end
							end
						end
						if f.EquippedItem then
							list[#list + 1] = f.EquippedItem;
						end
					end
					return list;
				end
				local acc = 0;
				local whConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
					acc += dt
					if (acc < 0.2) then
						return;
					end
					acc = 0;
					local cur = M.localItem();
					if (cur ~= lastItem) then
						lastItem = cur;
						dirty = true;
					end
					if dirty then
						dirty = false;
						for _, it in ipairs(allItems()) do
							pcall(applyItem, it);
						end
						for it in pairs(TOUCHED) do
							pcall(applyItem, it);
						end
					end
				end);
				local HF = {on=false,snd=false,head="Ping",body="Bass",headId="",bodyId="",hv=1,hp=1,bv=1,bp=1,note=false,text="Hit %DNAME% (%NAME%) for %DMG% in %PART%",chams=false,cdur=0.8,flash=false,fdur=0.6};
				local PRESETS = {Ping="rbxasset://sounds/electronicpingshort.wav",Bass="rbxasset://sounds/bass.wav",Snap="rbxasset://sounds/snap.mp3",Switch="rbxasset://sounds/switch.mp3",Button="rbxasset://sounds/button.wav",Uuhhh="rbxasset://sounds/uuhhh.mp3"};
				local NAMES = {"Ping","Bass","Snap","Switch","Button","Uuhhh","Custom ID"};
				local ha = addSub(mCombat, "Hit Feedback", "Sound, flash, highlight and text when you damage an enemy.");
				ha.Section("Hit Feedback");
				ha.Toggle("Enable Hit Feedback", false, function(v)
					HF.on = v;
				end);
				ha.Hint("Hits are detected from enemy health dropping right after you shoot while aiming near them.");
				ha.Section("Sound");
				ha.Toggle("Play Hit Sound", false, function(v)
					HF.snd = v;
				end);
				ha.Dropdown("Headshot Sound", NAMES, "Ping", function(v)
					HF.head = v;
				end);
				ha.TextBox("Headshot Custom ID", "rbxassetid://...", function(v)
					HF.headId = v;
				end);
				ha.Slider("Headshot Volume", 0.1, 10, 1, function(v)
					HF.hv = v;
				end, 0.1);
				ha.Slider("Headshot Pitch", 0.1, 3, 1, function(v)
					HF.hp = v;
				end, 0.1);
				ha.Dropdown("Body Hit Sound", NAMES, "Bass", function(v)
					HF.body = v;
				end);
				ha.TextBox("Body Custom ID", "rbxassetid://...", function(v)
					HF.bodyId = v;
				end);
				ha.Slider("Body Hit Volume", 0.1, 10, 1, function(v)
					HF.bv = v;
				end, 0.1);
				ha.Slider("Body Hit Pitch", 0.1, 3, 1, function(v)
					HF.bp = v;
				end, 0.1);
				ha.Section("Notification");
				ha.Toggle("Hit Notification", false, function(v)
					HF.note = v;
				end);
				ha.TextBox("Notification Text", "Hit %DNAME% for %DMG%", function(v)
					if (v ~= "") then
						HF.text = v;
					end
				end);
				ha.Hint("Placeholders: %NAME% %DNAME% %DMG% %PART%");
				ha.Section("Chams");
				ha.Toggle("Enable Hit Chams", false, function(v)
					HF.chams = v;
				end);
				ha.ColorWheel("Hit Chams Color", "#FF0000");
				ha.Slider("Hit Chams Duration", 0.1, 3, 0.8, function(v)
					HF.cdur = v;
				end, 0.1);
				ha.Section("Flash");
				ha.Toggle("Enable Hit Flash", false, function(v)
					HF.flash = v;
				end);
				ha.ColorWheel("Hit Flash Color", "#5AAAFF");
				ha.Slider("Hit Flash Duration", 0.1, 3, 0.6, function(v)
					HF.fdur = v;
				end, 0.1);
				local function hexOf(sec, text, def)
					local ok, c = pcall(Color3.fromHex, CONFIG_VALUES["Combat.Hit Feedback." .. sec .. "." .. text] or def);
					return (ok and c) or Color3.fromHex(def);
				end
				local function playHit(head)
					local id;
					if head then
						id = ((HF.head == "Custom ID") and HF.headId) or PRESETS[HF.head];
					else
						id = ((HF.body == "Custom ID") and HF.bodyId) or PRESETS[HF.body];
					end
					if (not id or (id == "")) then
						return;
					end
					if not id:find("rbx") then
						id = "rbxassetid://" .. id;
					end
					local s = Instance.new("Sound");
					s.SoundId = id;
					s.Volume = (head and HF.hv) or HF.bv;
					s.PlaybackSpeed = (head and HF.hp) or HF.bp;
					s.Parent = game:GetService("SoundService");
					s.Ended:Once(function()
						s:Destroy();
					end);
					s:Play();
					task.delay(5, function()
						if s.Parent then
							s:Destroy();
						end
					end);
				end
				local flashF = Instance.new("Frame");
				flashF.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1);
				flashF.BorderSizePixel = 0;
				flashF.BackgroundTransparency = 1;
				flashF.Active = false;
				flashF.ZIndex = 50;
				flashF.Parent = gGui;
				local hrp = RaycastParams.new();
				hrp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Include;
				local lastShot = -10;
				local function onHit(plr, ch, dmg)
					local c = workspace.CurrentCamera;
					local part = "Body";
					if c then
						hrp.FilterDescendantsInstances = {ch};
						local ctr = c.ViewportSize / 2;
						local ray = c:ViewportPointToRay(ctr.X, ctr.Y);
						local h = workspace:Raycast(ray.Origin, ray.Direction * 2000, hrp);
						if (h and (h.Instance.Name == "Head")) then
							part = "Head";
						end
					end
					if HF.snd then
						pcall(playHit, part == "Head");
					end
					if HF.note then
						local txt = HF.text:gsub("%%(%w+)%%", {NAME=plr.Name,DNAME=plr.DisplayName,PART=part,DMG=tostring(math.floor(dmg + 0.5))});
						pcall(notify, "Hit", txt, 1.5, "ok");
					end
					if HF.chams then
						local hl = Instance.new("Highlight");
						hl.FillColor = hexOf("Chams", "Hit Chams Color", "#FF0000");
						hl.OutlineTransparency = 1;
						hl.FillTransparency = 0.25;
						hl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop;
						hl.Parent = ch;
						tween(hl, HF.cdur, {FillTransparency=1});
						task.delay(HF.cdur + 0.1, function()
							hl:Destroy();
						end);
					end
					if HF.flash then
						flashF.BackgroundColor3 = hexOf("Flash", "Hit Flash Color", "#5AAAFF");
						flashF.BackgroundTransparency = 0.7;
						tween(flashF, HF.fdur, {BackgroundTransparency=1});
					end
				end
				local conns = {whConn};
				local function watch(plr)
					if (plr == player) then
						return;
					end
					local function hook(ch)
						local hum = ch:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5);
						if not hum then
							return;
						end
						local last = hum.Health;
						conns[#conns + 1] = hum.HealthChanged:Connect(function(h)
							local d = last - h;
							last = h;
							if ((d <= 0) or not HF.on) then
								return;
							end
							if ((os.clock() - lastShot) > 0.8) then
								return;
							end
							local c = workspace.CurrentCamera;
							local root = ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or ch:FindFirstChild("Head");
							if not (c and root) then
								return;
							end
							local toT = root.Position - c.CFrame.Position;
							if ((toT.Magnitude < 0.0010000000474974513) or (math.deg(math.acos(math.clamp(c.CFrame.LookVector:Dot(toT.Unit), -1, 1))) > 20)) then
								return;
							end
							pcall(onHit, plr, ch, d);
						end);
					end
					if plr.Character then
						task.spawn(hook, plr.Character);
					end
					conns[#conns + 1] = plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(hook);
				end
				for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
					watch(p);
				end
				conns[#conns + 1] = Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(watch);
				conns[#conns + 1] = UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
					local t = i.UserInputType;
					if ((t == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1) or (t == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) or (i.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonR2)) then
						lastShot = os.clock();
					end
				end);
				local trigConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
					if UIS:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1) then
						lastShot = os.clock();
					end
				end);
				conns[#conns + 1] = trigConn;
				gui.Destroying:Connect(function()
					for _, cn in ipairs(conns) do
						pcall(function()
							cn:Disconnect();
						end);
					end
					for it in pairs(TOUCHED) do
						WH.fire.on, WH.recoil.on, WH.aim.on, WH.melee.on, WH.dash.on, WH.auto, WH.dj, WH.motion = false, false, false, false, false, false, false, false;
						pcall(applyItem, it);
					end
					pcall(function()
						flashF:Destroy();
					end);
				end);
			end);
			if not wh2Ok then
				warn("[1Mag] Weapon Handling / Hit Feedback failed to load: " .. tostring(wh2Err));
			end
		end
	end
	do
		local V = {};
		local function hex3(h)
			return Color3.fromHex(h);
		end
		local plrs = game:GetService("Players");
		local cam = function()
			return workspace.CurrentCamera;
		end;
		local hasDraw = (type(Drawing) == "table") and (type(Drawing.new) == "function");
		do
			local esp = addSub(mVis, "ESP", "Player ESP.");
			esp.Section("ESP Options");
			esp.Toggle("ESP Enabled", false, function(v)
				V.esp = v;
			end);
			esp.Toggle("Chams (Highlight)", false, function(v)
				V.hl = v;
			end);
			esp.Toggle("Box", false, function(v)
				V.box = v;
			end);
			esp.Dropdown("Box Style", {"Corner","Full"}, "Corner", function(v)
				V.style = v;
			end);
			esp.Toggle("Box Fill", false, function(v)
				V.bfill = v;
			end);
			esp.Toggle("Names", false, function(v)
				V.names = v;
			end);
			esp.Toggle("Health Bar", false, function(v)
				V.health = v;
			end);
			esp.Toggle("Distance", false, function(v)
				V.dist = v;
			end);
			esp.Toggle("Tracers", false, function(v)
				V.tracer = v;
			end);
			esp.Dropdown("Tracer Origin", {"Character (HRP)","Bottom","Center","Mouse"}, "Character (HRP)", function(v)
				V.tOrigin = v;
			end);
			esp.Toggle("Look Direction", false, function(v)
				V.look = v;
			end);
			esp.Toggle("Offscreen Arrows", false, function(v)
				V.arrows = v;
			end);
			esp.Toggle("Show Teammates", false, function(v)
				V.team = v;
			end);
			esp.Toggle("Only Draw In Match", true, function(v)
				V.matchOnly = v;
			end);
			esp.Slider("Chams Fill Transparency", 0, 100, 75, function(v)
				V.fill = v / 100;
			end);
			esp.Slider("Chams Outline Transparency", 0, 100, 0, function(v)
				V.outl = v / 100;
			end);
			esp.Slider("Max Distance", 100, 3000, 1500, function(v)
				V.maxd = v;
			end, 50);
			esp.Slider("Screen Y Offset", -150, 150, 0, function(v)
				V.yoff = v;
			end);
			esp.Section("Colours");
			esp.Color("Visible Color", "#FF2A3D");
			esp.Color("Hidden Color", "#FFFFFF");
			esp.Color("Look Direction Color", "#FFFF00");
			V.fill = V.fill or 0.75;
			V.outl = V.outl or 0;
			V.maxd = V.maxd or 1500;
			V.style = V.style or "Corner";
			local items = {};
			local function colorOf(k, d)
				return hex3(CONFIG_VALUES["Visual.ESP.Colours." .. k] or d);
			end
			local function dl(kind, props)
				local d = Drawing.new(kind);
				for k, v in pairs(props) do
					pcall(function()
						d[k] = v;
					end);
				end
				d.Visible = false;
				return d;
			end
			local function mk(plr)
				local o = {};
				o.hl = Instance.new("Highlight");
				o.hl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop;
				o.hl.Enabled = false;
				if hasDraw then
					o.fillSq = dl("Square", {Filled=true,Transparency=0.12});
					o.edge, o.edgeO = {}, {};
					for i = 1, 8 do
						o.edgeO[i] = dl("Line", {Thickness=3,Color=Color3.new(0, 0, 0)});
						o.edge[i] = dl("Line", {Thickness=1.5});
					end
					o.hpBg = dl("Square", {Filled=true,Color=Color3.new(0, 0, 0),Transparency=0.6});
					o.hp = dl("Square", {Filled=true});
					o.name = dl("Text", {Center=true,Outline=true,Size=14,Font=2,Color=Color3.new(1, 1, 1)});
					o.info = dl("Text", {Center=true,Outline=true,Size=12,Font=2,Color=Color3.fromRGB(210, 210, 210)});
					o.line = dl("Line", {Thickness=1.5});
					o.look = dl("Line", {Thickness=1.5});
					o.arrow = dl("Triangle", {Filled=true});
				end
				items[plr] = o;
			end
			local function hideAll(o)
				o.hl.Enabled = false;
				if not o.name then
					return;
				end
				o.fillSq.Visible = false;
				o.hpBg.Visible = false;
				o.hp.Visible = false;
				o.name.Visible = false;
				o.info.Visible = false;
				o.line.Visible = false;
				o.look.Visible = false;
				o.arrow.Visible = false;
				for i = 1, 8 do
					o.edge[i].Visible = false;
					o.edgeO[i].Visible = false;
				end
			end
			local function rm(plr)
				local o = items[plr];
				if not o then
					return;
				end
				pcall(function()
					o.hl:Destroy();
				end);
				if o.name then
					for _, k in ipairs({"fillSq","hpBg","hp","name","info","line","look","arrow"}) do
						pcall(function()
							o[k]:Remove();
						end);
					end
					for i = 1, 8 do
						pcall(function()
							o.edge[i]:Remove();
							o.edgeO[i]:Remove();
						end);
					end
				end
				items[plr] = nil;
			end
			local espInit = false;
			local c1 = plrs.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(p)
				if (espInit and (p ~= player)) then
					mk(p);
				end
			end);
			local c2 = plrs.PlayerRemoving:Connect(rm);
			local function setBox(o, x, y, w, h, col, corner)
				local L = (corner and 0.28) or 0.5;
				local segs = {{x,y,(x + (w * L)),y},{(x + (w * (1 - L))),y,(x + w),y},{x,(y + h),(x + (w * L)),(y + h)},{(x + (w * (1 - L))),(y + h),(x + w),(y + h)},{x,y,x,(y + (h * L))},{x,(y + (h * (1 - L))),x,(y + h)},{(x + w),y,(x + w),(y + (h * L))},{(x + w),(y + (h * (1 - L))),(x + w),(y + h)}};
				for i, sg in ipairs(segs) do
					for _, ln in ipairs({o.edgeO[i],o.edge[i]}) do
						ln.From = Vector2.new(sg[1], sg[2]);
						ln.To = Vector2.new(sg[3], sg[4]);
						ln.Visible = true;
					end
					o.edge[i].Color = col;
				end
			end
			local params = RaycastParams.new();
			params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude;
			local camNow;
			local function P(v3)
				local p, on = camNow:WorldToViewportPoint(v3);
				return Vector3.new(p.X, p.Y + (V.yoff or 0), p.Z), on;
			end
			local function fin(n)
				return (n == n) and (n > -100000) and (n < 100000);
			end
			local function hideAllItems()
				for _, o in pairs(items) do
					hideAll(o);
				end
			end
			local function setHL(o, ch, col)
				if ((o.hlChar ~= ch) or not o.hl or (o.hl.Parent == nil)) then
					pcall(function()
						if o.hl then
							o.hl:Destroy();
						end
					end);
					local hl = Instance.new("Highlight");
					hl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop;
					local okp = pcall(function()
						hl.Parent = ch;
					end);
					if not okp then
						hl.Parent = gui;
					end
					o.hl = hl;
					o.hlChar = ch;
				end
				local hl = o.hl;
				hl.Adornee = ch;
				hl.Enabled = (V.hl and true) or false;
				hl.FillColor, hl.OutlineColor = col, col;
				hl.FillTransparency, hl.OutlineTransparency = V.fill, V.outl;
			end
			local function updatePlayer(plr, o, c, vp, myChar, myRoot, inMatch)
				local ch = plr.Character;
				local hum = ch and ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid");
				local rootp = ch and (ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or ch:FindFirstChild("Head"));
				local ok = V.esp and inMatch and rootp and hum and (hum.Health > 0) and (V.team or (plr.Team ~= player.Team) or (plr.Team == nil));
				local dist = (ok and myRoot and (rootp.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude) or 0;
				if (ok and (dist > V.maxd)) then
					ok = false;
				end
				local hd = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("Head");
				if (ok and hd and (hd.Transparency > 0.95) and (hd.LocalTransparencyModifier > 0.95)) then
					ok = false;
				end
				if not ok then
					hideAll(o);
					return;
				end
				local head = ch:FindFirstChild("Head") or rootp;
				local hit = workspace:Raycast(c.CFrame.Position, head.Position - c.CFrame.Position, params);
				local visible = (hit == nil) or hit.Instance:IsDescendantOf(ch);
				local col = (visible and colorOf("Visible Color", "#FF2A3D")) or colorOf("Hidden Color", "#FFFFFF");
				setHL(o, ch, col);
				if not o.name then
					return;
				end
				local pos, on = P(rootp.Position);
				local top = P(rootp.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2.9, 0));
				local bot = P(rootp.Position - Vector3.new(0, 3.1, 0));
				local h = math.abs(bot.Y - top.Y);
				local valid = on and (pos.Z > 0) and (top.Z > 0) and (bot.Z > 0) and fin(pos.X) and fin(pos.Y) and fin(h) and (h >= 1) and (h <= (vp.Y * 3));
				if valid then
					local w = h * 0.55;
					local x, y = math.floor(pos.X - (w / 2)), math.floor(top.Y);
					h = math.floor(h);
					w = math.floor(w);
					if V.box then
						setBox(o, x, y, w, h, col, V.style == "Corner");
					else
						for i = 1, 8 do
							o.edge[i].Visible = false;
							o.edgeO[i].Visible = false;
						end
					end
					o.fillSq.Visible = (V.box and V.bfill and true) or false;
					o.fillSq.Position = Vector2.new(x, y);
					o.fillSq.Size = Vector2.new(w, h);
					o.fillSq.Color = col;
					o.name.Visible = (V.names and true) or false;
					o.name.Text = plr.DisplayName;
					o.name.Position = Vector2.new(pos.X, y - 16);
					local pct = math.clamp(hum.Health / math.max(hum.MaxHealth, 1), 0, 1);
					o.hpBg.Visible = (V.health and true) or false;
					o.hpBg.Position = Vector2.new(x - 6, y - 1);
					o.hpBg.Size = Vector2.new(4, h + 2);
					o.hp.Visible = (V.health and true) or false;
					o.hp.Position = Vector2.new(x - 5, y + (h * (1 - pct)));
					o.hp.Size = Vector2.new(2, h * pct);
					o.hp.Color = Color3.fromHSV(pct * 0.33, 0.9, 1);
					local info = {};
					if V.dist then
						info[#info + 1] = math.floor(dist) .. "m";
					end
					if V.health then
						info[#info + 1] = math.floor(hum.Health) .. "hp";
					end
					o.info.Visible = #info > 0;
					o.info.Text = table.concat(info, " · ");
					o.info.Position = Vector2.new(pos.X, y + h + 3);
					if V.tracer then
						local from = Vector2.new(vp.X / 2, vp.Y);
						local mode = V.tOrigin or "Character (HRP)";
						if (mode == "Center") then
							from = vp / 2;
						elseif (mode == "Mouse") then
							local ml = UIS:GetMouseLocation();
							from = Vector2.new(ml.X, ml.Y);
						elseif ((mode == "Character (HRP)") and myRoot) then
							local mp = P(myRoot.Position);
							local _, mon = camNow:WorldToViewportPoint(myRoot.Position);
							if (mon and (mp.Z > 0) and fin(mp.X) and fin(mp.Y)) then
								from = Vector2.new(mp.X, mp.Y);
							end
						end
						o.line.Visible = true;
						o.line.From = from;
						o.line.To = Vector2.new(pos.X, pos.Y);
						o.line.Color = col;
						o.line.Transparency = 0.6;
					else
						o.line.Visible = false;
					end
					if V.look then
						local a2 = P(head.Position);
						local b2 = P(head.Position + (head.CFrame.LookVector * 6));
						if ((a2.Z > 0) and (b2.Z > 0) and fin(a2.X) and fin(a2.Y) and fin(b2.X) and fin(b2.Y)) then
							o.look.Visible = true;
							o.look.From = Vector2.new(a2.X, a2.Y);
							o.look.To = Vector2.new(b2.X, b2.Y);
							o.look.Color = colorOf("Look Direction Color", "#FFFF00");
						else
							o.look.Visible = false;
						end
					else
						o.look.Visible = false;
					end
					o.arrow.Visible = false;
				else
					o.fillSq.Visible = false;
					o.hpBg.Visible = false;
					o.hp.Visible = false;
					o.name.Visible = false;
					o.info.Visible = false;
					o.line.Visible = false;
					o.look.Visible = false;
					for i = 1, 8 do
						o.edge[i].Visible = false;
						o.edgeO[i].Visible = false;
					end
					if V.arrows then
						local rel = c.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(rootp.Position);
						local ang = math.atan2(rel.X, -rel.Z);
						local d = Vector2.new(math.sin(ang), -math.cos(ang));
						local n = Vector2.new(-d.Y, d.X);
						local p0 = (vp / 2) + (d * math.min(vp.X, vp.Y) * 0.4);
						o.arrow.PointA = p0 + (d * 12);
						o.arrow.PointB = (p0 - (d * 8)) + (n * 9);
						o.arrow.PointC = (p0 - (d * 8)) - (n * 9);
						o.arrow.Color = col;
						o.arrow.Visible = true;
					else
						o.arrow.Visible = false;
					end
				end
			end
			RunService:BindToRenderStep("1MagESP", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 1, function()
				local c = cam();
				if (not c or not V.esp) then
					hideAllItems();
					return;
				end
				if not espInit then
					espInit = true;
					for _, p in ipairs(plrs:GetPlayers()) do
						if ((p ~= player) and not items[p]) then
							pcall(mk, p);
						end
					end
				end
				camNow = c;
				local myChar = player.Character;
				local myRoot = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart");
				local inMatch = not V.matchOnly or ((myRoot ~= nil) and ((c.CFrame.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude < 40));
				params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {(myChar or c),c};
				local vp = c.ViewportSize;
				for plr, o in pairs(items) do
					local okk = pcall(updatePlayer, plr, o, c, vp, myChar, myRoot, inMatch);
					if not okk then
						hideAll(o);
					end
				end
			end);
			gui.Destroying:Connect(function()
				pcall(RunService.UnbindFromRenderStep, RunService, "1MagESP");
				c1:Disconnect();
				c2:Disconnect();
				for p in pairs(items) do
					rm(p);
				end
			end);
			if not hasDraw then
				esp.Label("Drawing API missing on this executor - only Highlight works.");
			end
		end
		do
			local fx = addSub(mVis, "Client Effects", "Camera and screen effects.");
			fx.Section("Camera View");
			local origFov;
			local fovOn, fovVal = false, 90;
			fx.Toggle("Custom Camera FOV", false, function(v)
				fovOn = v;
				local c = cam();
				if (c and not origFov) then
					origFov = c.FieldOfView;
				end
				if (not v and c and origFov) then
					c.FieldOfView = origFov;
				end
			end);
			fx.Slider("Camera FOV", 30, 120, 90, function(v)
				fovVal = v;
			end);
			local fc = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
				local c = cam();
				if (fovOn and c) then
					c.FieldOfView = fovVal;
				end
			end);
			gui.Destroying:Connect(function()
				fc:Disconnect();
			end);
			fx.Section("Overlay & Effect Removals");
			local rem = {};
			fx.Toggle("No Flashbang", false, function(v)
				rem.flash = v;
			end);
			fx.Toggle("No Burn Effect", false, function(v)
				rem.burn = v;
			end);
			fx.Toggle("No ADS Vignette", false, function(v)
				rem.vig = v;
			end);
			local kw = {flash={"flash"},burn={"burn","fire"},vig={"vignette"}};
			local function hideIn(root)
				for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
					if d:IsA("GuiObject") then
						local n = d.Name:lower();
						for k, words in pairs(kw) do
							if rem[k] then
								for _, w in ipairs(words) do
									if n:find(w, 1, true) then
										d.Visible = false;
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
			local rcAcc = 0;
			local rc = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
				rcAcc += dt
				if (rcAcc < 0.4) then
					return;
				end
				rcAcc = 0;
				if (rem.flash or rem.burn or rem.vig) then
					pcall(hideIn, PG);
				end
				if rem.flash then
					for _, e in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
						if (e:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") and e.Name:lower():find("flash")) then
							e.Enabled = false;
						end
					end
				end
			end);
			gui.Destroying:Connect(function()
				rc:Disconnect();
			end);
		end
		do
			local ch = addSub(mVis, "Crosshair", "Custom crosshair.");
			ch.Section("Custom Crosshair");
			local C = {on=false,len=12,th=2,gap=6,rot=false,spd=90,ang=0,shimmer=false};
			ch.Toggle("Custom Crosshair", false, function(v)
				C.on = v;
			end);
			ch.Slider("Length", 1, 40, 12, function(v)
				C.len = v;
			end);
			ch.Slider("Thickness", 1, 8, 2, function(v)
				C.th = v;
			end);
			ch.Slider("Gap", 0, 40, 6, function(v)
				C.gap = v;
			end);
			ch.Color("Crosshair Color", "#FF2A3D");
			ch.Toggle("Animated Rotation", false, function(v)
				C.rot = v;
			end);
			ch.Slider("Rotation Speed", 0, 360, 90, function(v)
				C.spd = v;
			end);
			ch.Toggle("Colour Shimmer", false, function(v)
				C.shimmer = v;
			end);
			ch.Toggle("Outline", true, function(v)
				C.outline = v;
			end);
			ch.Toggle("Centre Dot", true, function(v)
				C.dot = v;
			end);
			if hasDraw then
				local L, LO = {}, {};
				for i = 1, 4 do
					LO[i] = Drawing.new("Line");
					LO[i].Visible = false;
					LO[i].Color = Color3.new(0, 0, 0);
					L[i] = Drawing.new("Line");
					L[i].Visible = false;
				end
				local dotO = Drawing.new("Circle");
				dotO.Filled = true;
				dotO.Color = Color3.new(0, 0, 0);
				dotO.Radius = 3;
				dotO.NumSides = 20;
				dotO.Visible = false;
				local dotI = Drawing.new("Circle");
				dotI.Filled = true;
				dotI.Radius = 2;
				dotI.NumSides = 20;
				dotI.Visible = false;
				local t0 = tick();
				local cc = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
					local c = cam();
					if not c then
						return;
					end
					if not C.on then
						for i = 1, 4 do
							L[i].Visible = false;
							LO[i].Visible = false;
						end
						dotO.Visible = false;
						dotI.Visible = false;
						return;
					end
					if C.rot then
						C.ang = (C.ang + (C.spd * dt)) % 360;
					end
					local ctr = c.ViewportSize / 2;
					local col = hex3(CONFIG_VALUES["Visual.Crosshair.Custom Crosshair.Crosshair Color"] or "#FF2A3D");
					if C.shimmer then
						col = Color3.fromHSV(((tick() - t0) * 0.4) % 1, 0.9, 1);
					end
					for i = 1, 4 do
						local a = math.rad(C.ang + ((i - 1) * 90));
						local d = Vector2.new(math.sin(a), -math.cos(a));
						L[i].From = ctr + (d * C.gap);
						L[i].To = ctr + (d * (C.gap + C.len));
						L[i].Thickness = C.th;
						L[i].Color = col;
						L[i].Visible = true;
						LO[i].From = L[i].From;
						LO[i].To = L[i].To;
						LO[i].Thickness = C.th + 2;
						LO[i].Visible = (C.outline and true) or false;
					end
					dotI.Position = ctr;
					dotI.Color = col;
					dotI.Visible = (C.dot and true) or false;
					dotO.Position = ctr;
					dotO.Visible = (C.dot and C.outline and true) or false;
				end);
				gui.Destroying:Connect(function()
					cc:Disconnect();
					for i = 1, 4 do
						pcall(function()
							L[i]:Remove();
							LO[i]:Remove();
						end);
					end
					pcall(function()
						dotO:Remove();
						dotI:Remove();
					end);
				end);
			else
				ch.Label("Drawing API missing on this executor.");
			end
		end
		do
			local mv = addSub(mChar, "Movement", "Speed, jump, flight and more.");
			local M = {ws=50,jp=100,fly=100,ls=60,lu=20,ss=50,ljMode="Camera Look",jumpT=0,nc={},slideSpd=80,slideDur=0.5,jas=0.25,saj=0.5,onlyMove=true,autoJump=true,flyBtns=(UIS.TouchEnabled and not UIS.KeyboardEnabled),slMode=((UIS.TouchEnabled and not UIS.KeyboardEnabled and "Button (Hold)") or "Keys (Ctrl+Shift)"),ljBtn=(UIS.TouchEnabled and not UIS.KeyboardEnabled)};
			local function hum()
				local c = player.Character;
				return c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid");
			end
			local function root()
				local c = player.Character;
				return c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart");
			end
			local function grounded(h)
				return h.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air;
			end
			mv.Section("Speed & Jump");
			mv.Toggle("WalkSpeed", false, function(v)
				M.wsOn = v;
				local h = hum();
				if v then
					if (h and not M.baseWs) then
						M.baseWs = h.WalkSpeed;
					end
				elseif h then
					h.WalkSpeed = M.baseWs or 16;
					M.baseWs = nil;
				end
			end);
			mv.Slider("WalkSpeed Value", 16, 250, 50, function(v)
				M.ws = v;
			end);
			mv.Toggle("Jump Power", false, function(v)
				M.jpOn = v;
				local h = hum();
				if v then
					if (h and not M.baseUse) then
						M.baseUse = h.UseJumpPower;
						M.baseJp = h.JumpPower;
						M.baseJh = h.JumpHeight;
					end
				elseif (h and (M.baseUse ~= nil)) then
					pcall(function()
						h.UseJumpPower = M.baseUse;
						h.JumpPower = M.baseJp;
						h.JumpHeight = M.baseJh;
					end);
					M.baseUse = nil;
				end
			end);
			mv.Slider("Jump Power Value", 30, 350, 100, function(v)
				M.jp = v;
			end);
			mv.Toggle("Infinite Double Jumps", false, function(v)
				M.inf = v;
			end);
			mv.Section("Slide");
			mv.Toggle("Slide Speed", false, function(v)
				M.slideOn = v;
			end);
			mv.Slider("Slide Speed Value", 10, 300, 80, function(v)
				M.slideSpd = v;
			end);
			mv.Label("While sliding (Ctrl + Shift), any move key thrusts you forward.");
			mv.Dropdown("Slide Activation", {"Keys (Ctrl+Shift)","Button (Hold)","Button (Toggle)","Always On"}, (UIS.TouchEnabled and not UIS.KeyboardEnabled and "Button (Hold)") or "Keys (Ctrl+Shift)", function(v)
				M.slMode = v;
				local b = FX.MB.slide;
				if b then
					b.kind = ((v == "Button (Toggle)") and "toggle") or "hold";
					b.on = false;
					b.down = false;
					b.refresh();
				end
			end);
			mv.Toggle("Auto Slide", false, function(v)
				M.autoSlide = v;
			end);
			mv.Toggle("Only While Moving", true, function(v)
				M.onlyMove = v;
			end);
			mv.Toggle("Jump After Slide", true, function(v)
				M.autoJump = v;
			end);
			mv.Slider("Slide Duration (s)", 0.1, 2, 0.5, function(v)
				M.slideDur = v;
			end, 0.05);
			mv.Slider("Jump After Slide Cooldown (s)", 0, 2, 0.25, function(v)
				M.jas = v;
			end, 0.05);
			mv.Slider("Slide After Jump Cooldown (s)", 0, 2, 0.5, function(v)
				M.saj = v;
			end, 0.05);
			mv.Section("Flight");
			mv.Toggle("Flight", false, function(v)
				M.flyOn = v;
			end);
			mv.Slider("Flight Speed", 25, 500, 100, function(v)
				M.fly = v;
			end);
			mv.Dropdown("Flight Controls", {"Keys","Buttons"}, (UIS.TouchEnabled and not UIS.KeyboardEnabled and "Buttons") or "Keys", function(v)
				M.flyBtns = v == "Buttons";
			end);
			mv.Label("Fly where you look. Keys: W/A/S/D, Space up, Ctrl down. Buttons: on-screen UP / DOWN.");
			mv.Section("Other");
			mv.Toggle("Noclip", false, function(v)
				M.noclip = v;
				if not v then
					for p in pairs(M.nc) do
						pcall(function()
							p.CanCollide = true;
						end);
					end
					M.nc = {};
				end
			end);
			mv.Toggle("Auto Strafe", false, function(v)
				M.strafe = v;
			end);
			mv.Slider("Auto Strafe Speed", 1, 200, 50, function(v)
				M.ss = v;
			end);
			mv.Section("Long Jump");
			mv.Toggle("Long Jump", false, function(v)
				M.ljOn = v;
			end);
			mv.Keybind("Long Jump Key", "E");
			mv.Dropdown("Long Jump Trigger", {"Key","Button"}, (UIS.TouchEnabled and not UIS.KeyboardEnabled and "Button") or "Key", function(v)
				M.ljBtn = v == "Button";
			end);
			mv.Dropdown("Direction", {"Camera Look","Move Direction"}, "Camera Look", function(v)
				M.ljMode = v;
			end);
			mv.Slider("Force", 0, 300, 60, function(v)
				M.ls = v;
			end);
			mv.Slider("Upward Velocity", 0, 200, 20, function(v)
				M.lu = v;
			end);
			local flyUp = FX.MB.new("flyup", "UP", 0.88, 0.4);
			local flyDn = FX.MB.new("flydown", "DOWN", 0.88, 0.6);
			flyUp.want = function()
				return M.flyOn and M.flyBtns;
			end;
			flyDn.want = flyUp.want;
			local slBtn = FX.MB.new("slide", "SLIDE", 0.76, 0.5);
			FX.MB.slide = slBtn;
			slBtn.kind = ((M.slMode == "Button (Toggle)") and "toggle") or "hold";
			slBtn.want = function()
				return M.slideOn and ((M.slMode == "Button (Hold)") or (M.slMode == "Button (Toggle)"));
			end;
			local function enforce()
				local h, r, c = hum(), root(), cam();
				if not (h and r) then
					return;
				end
				if M.wsOn then
					h.WalkSpeed = M.ws;
				end
				if M.jpOn then
					pcall(function()
						h.UseJumpPower = true;
						h.JumpPower = M.jp;
					end);
				end
				if (M.flyOn and c) then
					local cf = c.CFrame;
					local look, right = cf.LookVector, cf.RightVector;
					local f, s, u = 0, 0, 0;
					if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then
						f += 1
					end
					if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then
						f -= 1
					end
					if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then
						s += 1
					end
					if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then
						s -= 1
					end
					if ((f == 0) and (s == 0)) then
						local md = h.MoveDirection;
						if (md.Magnitude > 0.05) then
							local fl, fr = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z), Vector3.new(right.X, 0, right.Z);
							if (fl.Magnitude > 0.01) then
								f = md:Dot(fl.Unit);
							end
							if (fr.Magnitude > 0.01) then
								s = md:Dot(fr.Unit);
							end
						end
					end
					if (UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) or flyUp.down) then
						u += 1
					end
					if (UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) or UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Q) or flyDn.down) then
						u -= 1
					end
					local d = (look * f) + (right * s) + Vector3.new(0, u, 0);
					r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = (((d.Magnitude > 1) and d.Unit) or d) * M.fly;
					return;
				end
				local slideActive = M.sliding or (UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) and UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift)) or (M.slMode == "Always On") or ((M.slMode == "Button (Hold)") and slBtn.down) or ((M.slMode == "Button (Toggle)") and slBtn.on);
				if (M.slideOn and slideActive and c) then
					local hasInput = (h.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0.05) or UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) or UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) or UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) or UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D);
					if hasInput then
						local lv = c.CFrame.LookVector;
						local flat = Vector3.new(lv.X, 0, lv.Z);
						if (flat.Magnitude > 0.01) then
							local t = flat.Unit * M.slideSpd;
							local v = r.AssemblyLinearVelocity;
							r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(t.X, v.Y, t.Z);
						end
					end
				elseif (M.wsOn and grounded(h)) then
					local md = h.MoveDirection;
					if (md.Magnitude > 0.1) then
						local v = r.AssemblyLinearVelocity;
						if (Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z).Magnitude < M.ws) then
							local t = md.Unit * M.ws;
							r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(t.X, v.Y, t.Z);
						end
					end
				end
				if (M.noclip and player.Character) then
					for _, p in ipairs(player.Character:GetDescendants()) do
						if (p:IsA("BasePart") and p.CanCollide) then
							p.CanCollide = false;
							M.nc[p] = true;
						end
					end
				end
			end
			local c1 = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
				local h, r = hum(), root();
				if not (h and r) then
					return;
				end
				enforce();
				local g = grounded(h);
				if (M.jpOn and M.wasG and not g and not M.flyOn) then
					local v = r.AssemblyLinearVelocity;
					if ((v.Y > 10) and (((tick() - M.jumpT) < 0.3) or UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space)) and (math.abs(v.Y - M.jp) > 2)) then
						r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(v.X, M.jp, v.Z);
					end
				end
				M.wasG = g;
			end);
			local c2 = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
				local h, r = hum(), root();
				if not (h and r) then
					return;
				end
				enforce();
				if (M.strafe and not M.flyOn and not grounded(h)) then
					local md = h.MoveDirection;
					if (md.Magnitude > 0.05) then
						local v = r.AssemblyLinearVelocity;
						local hv = Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z);
						local sp = hv.Magnitude;
						local cur = ((sp > 1) and hv.Unit) or md.Unit;
						local dir = (cur + (md.Unit * math.clamp(dt * 6, 0, 1))).Unit;
						local ns = math.max(sp, math.min(M.ss, sp + (M.ss * dt * 1.5)));
						r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(dir.X * ns, v.Y, dir.Z * ns);
					end
				end
			end);
			local function onJump()
				M.jumpT = tick();
				local h, r = hum(), root();
				if (not (h and r) or M.flyOn) then
					return;
				end
				if not grounded(h) then
					if M.inf then
						local jp = (M.jpOn and M.jp) or (h.UseJumpPower and h.JumpPower) or math.sqrt(2 * workspace.Gravity * h.JumpHeight);
						local v = r.AssemblyLinearVelocity;
						r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(v.X, jp, v.Z);
						pcall(function()
							h:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping);
						end);
					end
				elseif M.jpOn then
					task.delay(0.05, function()
						local h2, r2 = hum(), root();
						if (h2 and r2 and M.jpOn and grounded(h2)) then
							local v = r2.AssemblyLinearVelocity;
							r2.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(v.X, M.jp, v.Z);
						end
					end);
				end
			end
			local function longJump()
				local h, r, c = hum(), root(), cam();
				if not (h and r and c) then
					return;
				end
				local dir;
				if ((M.ljMode == "Move Direction") and (h.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0.05)) then
					dir = h.MoveDirection;
				else
					dir = c.CFrame.LookVector * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1);
				end
				if (dir.Magnitude < 0.05) then
					dir = r.CFrame.LookVector * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1);
				end
				r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = (dir.Unit * M.ls) + Vector3.new(0, M.lu, 0);
			end
			local ljB = FX.MB.new("longjump", "LONG\nJUMP", 0.76, 0.65);
			ljB.kind = "tap";
			ljB.onTap = function()
				if M.ljOn then
					longJump();
				end
			end;
			ljB.want = function()
				return M.ljOn and M.ljBtn;
			end;
			local VIM;
			pcall(function()
				VIM = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager");
			end);
			local VK = {[Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl]=162,[Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift]=160,[Enum.KeyCode.Space]=32};
			local function key(code, down)
				local ok = false;
				if VIM then
					ok = pcall(function()
						VIM:SendKeyEvent(down, code, false, game);
					end);
				end
				if (not ok and VK[code]) then
					local f = (down and keypress) or keyrelease;
					if f then
						pcall(f, VK[code]);
					end
				end
			end
			local function simJump()
				local h = hum();
				if not h then
					return;
				end
				M.jumpT = tick();
				key(Enum.KeyCode.Space, true);
				task.delay(0.06, function()
					key(Enum.KeyCode.Space, false);
				end);
				task.delay(0.07, function()
					local h2, r2 = hum(), root();
					if (h2 and r2 and grounded(h2)) then
						local jp = (M.jpOn and M.jp) or (h2.UseJumpPower and h2.JumpPower) or math.sqrt(2 * workspace.Gravity * h2.JumpHeight);
						local v = r2.AssemblyLinearVelocity;
						r2.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(v.X, jp, v.Z);
						pcall(function()
							h2:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping);
						end);
					end
				end);
			end
			local alive = true;
			local function waitFor(t)
				local t0 = os.clock();
				while (os.clock() - t0) < t do
					if not (alive and M.autoSlide) then
						return false;
					end
					RunService.Heartbeat:Wait();
				end
				return alive and M.autoSlide;
			end
			local function wantsMove(h)
				return (h.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0.05) or UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) or UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) or UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) or UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D);
			end
			task.spawn(function()
				while alive do
					RunService.Heartbeat:Wait();
					local h, r = hum(), root();
					if (M.autoSlide and h and r and (h.Health > 0) and not M.flyOn and grounded(h) and (not M.onlyMove or wantsMove(h))) then
						M.sliding = true;
						key(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl, true);
						key(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift, true);
						local still = waitFor(M.slideDur);
						key(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl, false);
						key(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift, false);
						M.sliding = false;
						if (still and M.autoJump) then
							if waitFor(M.jas) then
								simJump();
								waitFor(M.saj);
							end
						elseif still then
							waitFor(M.saj);
						end
					end
				end
			end);
			local c3 = UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(i, gp)
				if gp then
					return;
				end
				if ((i.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Space) or (i.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonA)) then
					onJump();
				end
				if (M.ljOn and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard) and (i.KeyCode.Name == (CONFIG_VALUES["Character.Movement.Long Jump.Long Jump Key"] or "E"))) then
					longJump();
				end
			end);
			local c4 = UIS.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
				if (UIS.TouchEnabled and not UIS.KeyboardEnabled) then
					onJump();
				end
			end);
			gui.Destroying:Connect(function()
				alive = false;
				M.sliding = false;
				key(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl, false);
				key(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift, false);
				c1:Disconnect();
				c2:Disconnect();
				c3:Disconnect();
				c4:Disconnect();
				local h = hum();
				if h then
					if M.wsOn then
						h.WalkSpeed = M.baseWs or 16;
					end
					if (M.baseUse ~= nil) then
						pcall(function()
							h.UseJumpPower = M.baseUse;
							h.JumpPower = M.baseJp;
							h.JumpHeight = M.baseJh;
						end);
					end
				end
				for p in pairs(M.nc) do
					pcall(function()
						p.CanCollide = true;
					end);
				end
			end);
		end
	end
	do
		local function hex3(h)
			return Color3.fromHex(h);
		end

		local custom = CONFIG_VALUES["World.CustomSkies"];
		if (type(custom) ~= "table") then
			custom = {};
			CONFIG_VALUES["World.CustomSkies"] = custom;
		end
		local W = {};
		local function newFx(class)
			local e = Instance.new(class);
			e.Name = "1Mag" .. class;
			e.Enabled = false;
			e.Parent = Lighting;
			return e;
		end
		local bloom, cc, dof = newFx("BloomEffect"), newFx("ColorCorrectionEffect"), newFx("DepthOfFieldEffect");
		local rays = newFx("SunRaysEffect");
		local fxList = {bloom,cc,dof,rays};
		local orig = {Ambient=Lighting.Ambient,OutdoorAmbient=Lighting.OutdoorAmbient,ClockTime=Lighting.ClockTime,Brightness=Lighting.Brightness,FogStart=Lighting.FogStart,FogEnd=Lighting.FogEnd,GlobalShadows=Lighting.GlobalShadows,FogColor=Lighting.FogColor,ExposureCompensation=Lighting.ExposureCompensation};
		for _, prop in ipairs({"ColorShift_Top","ColorShift_Bottom","EnvironmentDiffuseScale","EnvironmentSpecularScale","GeographicLatitude","ShadowSoftness","LightingStyle","TonemapperPreset","PrioritizeLightingQuality","ShadowColor"}) do
			local ok, v = pcall(function()
				return Lighting[prop];
			end);
			if (ok and (v ~= nil)) then
				orig[prop] = v;
			end
		end
		local mySky = Instance.new("Sky");
		mySky.Name = "1MagSky";
		local stash, skyOn, curSky = {}, false, nil;
		local function aid(x)
			x = tostring(x);
			return (x:match("^%d+$") and ("rbxassetid://" .. x)) or x;
		end
		local function applySky(p)
			if not p then
				return;
			end
			curSky = p;
			mySky.SkyboxBk = aid(p.Bk);
			mySky.SkyboxDn = aid(p.Dn);
			mySky.SkyboxFt = aid(p.Ft);
			mySky.SkyboxLf = aid(p.Lf);
			mySky.SkyboxRt = aid(p.Rt);
			mySky.SkyboxUp = aid(p.Up);
			mySky.StarCount = W.stars or tonumber(p.Stars) or 3000;
			mySky.SunAngularSize = W.sunSize or tonumber(p.Sun) or 21;
			mySky.MoonAngularSize = tonumber(p.Moon) or 11;
			mySky.SunTextureId = (p.SunTex and aid(p.SunTex)) or "rbxasset://sky/sun.jpg";
			mySky.MoonTextureId = (p.MoonTex and aid(p.MoonTex)) or "rbxasset://sky/moon.jpg";
		end
		local function stashOthers()
			for _, c in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
				if (c:IsA("Sky") and (c ~= mySky)) then
					stash[#stash + 1] = c;
					c.Parent = nil;
				end
			end
		end
		local function skyEnable(v)
			skyOn = v;
			if v then
				stashOthers();
				mySky.Parent = Lighting;
			else
				mySky.Parent = nil;
				for _, s in ipairs(stash) do
					pcall(function()
						s.Parent = Lighting;
					end);
				end
				stash = {};
			end
		end
		local function findSky(name)
			return custom[name] or COMMUNITY[name] or PRESETS[name];
		end
		local function skyNames()
			local out = {};
			for n in pairs(COMMUNITY) do
				out[#out + 1] = n;
			end
			table.sort(out);
			local rest = {};
			for n in pairs(custom) do
				rest[#rest + 1] = "[Mine] " .. n;
			end
			for n in pairs(PRESETS) do
				rest[#rest + 1] = n;
			end
			table.sort(rest);
			for _, n in ipairs(rest) do
				out[#out + 1] = n;
			end
			return out;
		end
		local function lookup(name)
			if ((type(name) == "string") and (name:sub(1, 7) == "[Mine] ")) then
				return custom[name:sub(8)];
			end
			return findSky(name);
		end

		do
			local box = make("TextBox", {BackgroundColor3=T.PanelHi,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(1, 0),Position=UDim2.new(1, -134, 0, 5),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(150, 24),Text="",PlaceholderText="search skybox...",PlaceholderColor3=T.Dim,FontFace=F_BOLD,TextSize=17,TextColor3=T.Text,ClearTextOnFocus=false,ZIndex=6}, dd.Frame);
			corner(box, 6);
			stroke(box, T.Border, 1, 0.4);
			box:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
				local q, res = string.lower(box.Text), {};
				for _, n in ipairs(skyNames()) do
					if ((q == "") or string.find(string.lower(n), q, 1, true)) then
						res[#res + 1] = n;
					end
				end
				dd:SetOptions(res);
				if ((q ~= "") and (#res > 0)) then
					dd.Open(true);
				end
			end);
		end
		sk.Button("Random Skybox", function()
			local all = skyNames();
			local n = all[math.random(#all)];
			dd:Set(n);
			if not skyOn then
				W.pendingEnable = true;
			end
			notify("Skybox", n, 2);
		end);
