		local cs = addSub(mWorld, "Custom Sky", "Build your own skybox from six image IDs.");
		cs.Section("Face IDs (number or rbxassetid://...)");
		local faces = {};
		for _, f in ipairs({{"Up","Up"},{"Down","Dn"},{"Front","Ft"},{"Back","Bk"},{"Left","Lf"},{"Right","Rt"}}) do
			faces[f[2]] = cs.TextBox(f[1] .. " Face", "image id");
		end
		local function readFaces()
			local p = {};
			for k, tb in pairs(faces) do
				local v = (tb.Get() or ""):gsub("%s", "");
				if (v == "") then
					return nil, k;
				end
				p[k] = v;
			end
			return p;
		end
		cs.Section("Actions");
		cs.Button("Apply Custom Skybox", function()
			local p, missing = readFaces();
			if not p then
				notify("Custom Sky", "Missing a face: " .. tostring(missing), 2.5);
				return;
			end
			applySky(p);
			skyEnable(true);
			notify("Custom Sky", "Applied", 2);
		end);
		local saveName = cs.TextBox("Save As Name", "my sky");
		cs.Button("Save To My Skyboxes", function()
			local p = readFaces();
			local n = (saveName.Get() or ""):gsub("[^%w _%-]", "");
			if (not p or (n == "")) then
				notify("Custom Sky", "Fill all 6 faces and a name", 2.5);
				return;
			end
			custom[n] = p;
			dd:SetOptions(skyNames());
			notify("Custom Sky", "Saved as [Mine] " .. n, 2.5);
		end);
		cs.Button("Remove Saved (by name)", function()
			local n = (saveName.Get() or ""):gsub("[^%w _%-]", "");
			if custom[n] then
				custom[n] = nil;
				dd:SetOptions(skyNames());
				notify("Custom Sky", "Removed " .. n, 2);
			else
				notify("Custom Sky", "No saved sky with that name", 2);
			end
		end);

		ambSnd.Looped = true;
		ambSnd.Parent = SoundService;
		RunService:BindToRenderStep("1MagStretch", Enum.RenderPriority.Last.Value, function()
			if not W.strOn then
				return;
			end
			local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera;
			if cam then
				cam.CFrame = cam.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 0, W.strX or 1, 0, 0, 0, W.strY or 1, 0, 0, 0, 1);
			end
		end);
		local function ensureAtmo()
			if (atmo and atmo.Parent) then
				return atmo;
			end
			atmo = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Atmosphere");
			if atmo then
				atmoOrig = {Density=atmo.Density,Haze=atmo.Haze,Glare=atmo.Glare,Color=atmo.Color,Decay=atmo.Decay,Offset=atmo.Offset};
				atmoMade = false;
			else
				atmo = Instance.new("Atmosphere");
				atmo.Name = "1MagAtmosphere";
				atmo.Parent = Lighting;
				atmoMade = true;
			end
			return atmo;
		end
		local lastAtmo = false;
		local loop = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
			for _, fx in ipairs(fxList) do
				if (fx.Parent ~= Lighting) then
					fx.Parent = Lighting;
				end
			end
			if W.pendingEnable then
				W.pendingEnable = nil;
				skyEnable(true);
				if curSky then
					applySky(curSky);
				end
			end
			if skyOn then
				if (mySky.Parent ~= Lighting) then
					mySky.Parent = Lighting;
				end
				for _, c in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
					if (c:IsA("Sky") and (c ~= mySky)) then
						stash[#stash + 1] = c;
						c.Parent = nil;
					end
				end
			end
			if W.amb then
				Lighting.Ambient = hex3(CONFIG_VALUES["World.Lighting.Lighting.Indoor Ambient"] or "#3BCBFF");
				Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = hex3(CONFIG_VALUES["World.Lighting.Lighting.Outdoor Ambient"] or "#01668B");
			end
			if W.cs then
				Lighting.ColorShift_Top = hex3(CONFIG_VALUES["World.Lighting.Lighting.Color Shift Top"] or "#64A0FF");
				Lighting.ColorShift_Bottom = hex3(CONFIG_VALUES["World.Lighting.Lighting.Color Shift Bottom"] or "#14285A");
			end
			if (W.dif and W.difv) then
				Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = W.difv;
			end
			if (W.spec and W.specv) then
				Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = W.specv;
			end
			if (W.lat and W.latv) then
				Lighting.GeographicLatitude = W.latv;
			end
			if (W.soft and W.softv) then
				Lighting.ShadowSoftness = W.softv;
			end
			if (W.sty and W.styv) then
				pcall(function()
					Lighting.LightingStyle = Enum.LightingStyle[W.styv];
				end);
			end
			if W.shc then
				pcall(function()
					Lighting.ShadowColor = hex3(CONFIG_VALUES["World.Lighting.Fog & Shadows.Shadow Color Value"] or "#B3B3B8");
				end);
			end
			if W.plq then
				Lighting.PrioritizeLightingQuality = true;
			end
			if (W.tone and W.tonev) then
				pcall(function()
					Lighting.TonemapperPreset = Enum.TonemapperPreset[W.tonev];
				end);
			end
			if W.ambOn then
				local id = tostring(CONFIG_VALUES["World.Effects.Ambience.Custom Sound (ID or URL)"] or "");
				if (id ~= "") then
					id = aid(id);
					if (ambSnd.SoundId ~= id) then
						ambSnd.SoundId = id;
					end
					ambSnd.Volume = W.ambVol or 1;
					if not ambSnd.IsPlaying then
						ambSnd:Play();
					end
				elseif ambSnd.IsPlaying then
					ambSnd:Stop();
				end
			elseif ambSnd.IsPlaying then
				ambSnd:Stop();
			end
			if (W.sunLock and W.sunTime) then
				Lighting.ClockTime = W.sunTime;
			elseif (W.clock and W.time) then
				Lighting.ClockTime = W.time;
			end
			if (W.bri and W.briv) then
				Lighting.Brightness = W.briv;
			end
			if (W.exp and W.expv) then
				Lighting.ExposureCompensation = W.expv;
			end
			if W.fog then
				Lighting.FogStart = W.fs or 0;
				Lighting.FogEnd = W.fe or 3000;
				Lighting.FogColor = hex3(CONFIG_VALUES["World.Lighting.Fog & Shadows.Fog Color"] or "#B070FF");
			end
			if W.sh then
				Lighting.GlobalShadows = false;
			end
			if cc.Enabled then
				cc.TintColor = hex3(CONFIG_VALUES["World.Effects.Colour Grading.Tint"] or "#FFFFFF");
			end
			if W.atmoOn then
				local a = ensureAtmo();
				a.Density = W.aDen or 0.35;
				a.Haze = W.aHaze or 1.5;
				a.Glare = W.aGlare or 4;
				a.Offset = W.aOff or 0.25;
				a.Color = hex3(CONFIG_VALUES["World.Effects.Atmosphere (haze & glare).Atmosphere Color"] or "#C8AA6C");
				a.Decay = hex3(CONFIG_VALUES["World.Effects.Atmosphere (haze & glare).Atmosphere Decay"] or "#5C3C0E");
				lastAtmo = true;
			elseif lastAtmo then
				lastAtmo = false;
				if (atmo and atmo.Parent) then
					if atmoMade then
						atmo:Destroy();
						atmo = nil;
					elseif atmoOrig then
						atmo.Density, atmo.Haze, atmo.Glare = atmoOrig.Density, atmoOrig.Haze, atmoOrig.Glare;
						atmo.Color, atmo.Decay, atmo.Offset = atmoOrig.Color, atmoOrig.Decay, atmoOrig.Offset;
					end
				end
			end
		end);
		gui.Destroying:Connect(function()
			loop:Disconnect();
			pcall(RunService.UnbindFromRenderStep, RunService, "1MagStretch");
			pcall(function()
				ambSnd:Destroy();
			end);
			for _, fx in ipairs(fxList) do
				pcall(function()
					fx:Destroy();
				end);
			end
			pcall(function()
				mySky:Destroy();
			end);
			for _, s in ipairs(stash) do
				pcall(function()
					s.Parent = Lighting;
				end);
			end
			if (atmo and atmoMade) then
				pcall(function()
					atmo:Destroy();
				end);
			end
			for k, v in pairs(orig) do
				pcall(function()
					Lighting[k] = v;
				end);
			end
		end);
	end
	do
		local TEX_DIR = ROOT .. "/Textures";
		local CACHE_DIR = ROOT .. "/TextureCache";
		ensureFolder(TEX_DIR);
		ensureFolder(CACHE_DIR);
		local ti = addSub(mTex, "Texture Injector", "Paste an id/url config, save it, then load it.");
		local function parseConfig(text)
			text = tostring(text or "");
			if not text:find("%S") then
				return nil, "Paste a config first";
			end
			local rules = {};
			local rest = text:gsub('{%s*ids%s*=%s*{([%d%s,]*)}%s*,?%s*url%s*=%s*\"([^\"]-)\"%s*,?%s*}', function(idStr, url)
				local ids = {};
				for n in idStr:gmatch("%d+") do
					ids[#ids + 1] = n;
				end
				if (#ids == 0) then
					return nil;
				end
				if not (url:find("^https://") or url:find("^rbxassetid://")) then
					return nil;
				end
				rules[#rules + 1] = {ids=ids,url=url};
				return "";
			end);
			if ((rest:gsub("[%s,{}]", "") ~= "") or (#rules == 0)) then
				return nil, 'Only {ids = {...}, url = \"...\"}, entries are allowed';
			end
			return rules;
		end
		local function serialize(rules)
			local out = {};
			for _, r in ipairs(rules) do
				out[#out + 1] = "{ids = {" .. table.concat(r.ids, ", ") .. '}, url = \"' .. r.url .. '\"},';
			end
			return table.concat(out, "\n");
		end
		local function hashStr(s)
			local h1, h2 = 5381, 52711;
			for i = 1, #s do
				local b = s:byte(i);
				h1 = ((h1 * 33) + b) % 4294967296;
				h2 = ((h2 * 31) + b) % 4294967291;
			end
			return string.format("%08x%08x", h1, h2);
		end
		local assetCache = {};
		local function getAsset(url)
			if assetCache[url] then
				return assetCache[url];
			end
			if url:find("^rbxassetid://") then
				return url;
			end
			local ok, result = pcall(function()
				if (writefile and isfile and getcustomasset) then
					local fileName = CACHE_DIR .. "/tex_" .. hashStr(url) .. ".asset";
					if not isfile(fileName) then
						local data = game:HttpGet(url);
						if (data and (#data > 80)) then
							writefile(fileName, data);
						end
					end
					if isfile(fileName) then
						local a = getcustomasset(fileName);
						if (a and (a ~= "")) then
							return a;
						end
					end
				end
				return url;
			end);
			result = (ok and result) or url;
			if (result ~= url) then
				assetCache[url] = result;
			end
			return result;
		end
		local active = {conn=nil,orig=setmetatable({}, {__mode="k"})};
		local gen = 0;
		local PROPS = {Sound="SoundId",ImageLabel="Image",ImageButton="Image",Decal="Texture",Texture="Texture",MeshPart="TextureID"};
		local function applyObj(obj, map)
			local prop = PROPS[obj.ClassName];
			if not prop then
				return;
			end
			local value = obj[prop];
			if (type(value) ~= "string") then
				return;
			end
			local id = value:match("%d+");
			local new = id and map[id];
			if (new and (new ~= value)) then
				if not active.orig[obj] then
					active.orig[obj] = {prop,value};
				end
				obj[prop] = new;
			end
		end
		local function unloadTextures()
			gen = gen + 1;
			if active.conn then
				active.conn:Disconnect();
				active.conn = nil;
			end
			for obj, d in pairs(active.orig) do
				pcall(function()
					obj[d[1]] = d[2];
				end);
			end
			active.orig = setmetatable({}, {__mode="k"});
		end
		local function loadRules(rules, label)
			unloadTextures();
			local my = gen;
			task.spawn(function()
				notify("Texture Injector", "Loading textures...", 2);
				local map, pending = {}, #rules;
				for _, r in ipairs(rules) do
					task.spawn(function()
						local asset = getAsset(r.url);
						for _, id in ipairs(r.ids) do
							map[id] = asset;
						end
						pending -= 1
					end);
				end
				local t0 = os.clock();
				while (pending > 0) and ((os.clock() - t0) < 30) do
					task.wait();
				end
				if (gen ~= my) then
					return;
				end
				local list = game:GetDescendants();
				for i = 1, #list do
					local o = list[i];
					if PROPS[o.ClassName] then
						pcall(applyObj, o, map);
					end
					if ((i % 6000) == 0) then
						task.wait();
						if (gen ~= my) then
							return;
						end
					end
				end
				active.conn = game.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(o)
					if PROPS[o.ClassName] then
						task.defer(pcall, applyObj, o, map);
					end
				end);
				notify("Texture Injector", "Loaded: " .. tostring(label) .. " (" .. #rules .. " entries)", 3);
			end);
		end
		local function listTexConfigs()
			local out = {};
			if (type(listfiles) == "function") then
				pcall(function()
					for _, f in ipairs(listfiles(TEX_DIR)) do
						local n = tostring(f):match("([^/\\]+)%.txt$");
						if n then
							table.insert(out, n);
						end
					end
				end);
			end
			if (#out == 0) then
				out = {"None"};
			end
			table.sort(out);
			return out;
		end
		ti.Section("Add Config");
		local pasteBox;
		pasteBox = ti.TextBox("Paste Config", "paste {ids = ...}", function(t)
			for k in pairs(CONFIG_VALUES) do
				if ((type(k) == "string") and k:find("%.Paste Config$")) then
					CONFIG_VALUES[k] = nil;
				end
			end
			if not (t or ""):find("%S") then
				return;
			end
			local rules, err = parseConfig(t);
			if rules then
				notify("Texture Injector", #rules .. " entries ready - name it and save", 2.5);
			else
				pasteBox:Set("");
				notify("Texture Injector", err, 3.5);
			end
		end);
		local nameBox = ti.TextBox("Name Config", "texture config name");
		local pick;
		ti.Button("Save Config", function()
			local rules, err = parseConfig(pasteBox.Get());
			if not rules then
				notify("Texture Injector", err, 3);
				return;
			end
			local n = (nameBox.Get() or ""):gsub("[^%w _%-]", "");
			n = n:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$");
			if (n == "") then
				notify("Texture Injector", "Type a config name first", 2);
				return;
			end
			if (type(writefile) ~= "function") then
				notify("Texture Injector", "Executor has no writefile", 2);
				return;
			end
			local ok = pcall(writefile, TEX_DIR .. "/" .. n .. ".txt", serialize(rules));
			if not ok then
				notify("Texture Injector", "Save failed", 2);
				return;
			end
			pick:SetOptions(listTexConfigs());
			pick:Set(n);
			pasteBox:Set("");
			notify("Texture Injector", "Saved: " .. n .. " (" .. #rules .. " entries)", 2.5);
		end);
		ti.Section("Saved Configs");
		pick = ti.Dropdown("Saved Configs", listTexConfigs(), "None");
		ti.Button("Load", function()
			local n = pick.Get();
			local path = TEX_DIR .. "/" .. tostring(n) .. ".txt";
			if ((type(isfile) == "function") and (type(readfile) == "function") and isfile(path)) then
				local rules, err = parseConfig(readfile(path));
				if rules then
					loadRules(rules, n);
					return;
				end
				notify("Texture Injector", err or "Config is invalid", 3);
				return;
			end
			notify("Texture Injector", "Pick a saved config first", 2);
		end);
		ti.Section("Manage");
		ti.Button("Unload Textures (restore originals)", function()
			unloadTextures();
			notify("Texture Injector", "Textures restored", 2);
		end);
		ti.Button("Delete Selected Config", function()
			local n = pick.Get();
			local path = TEX_DIR .. "/" .. tostring(n) .. ".txt";
			if ((type(delfile) == "function") and (type(isfile) == "function") and isfile(path)) then
				pcall(delfile, path);
				pick:SetOptions(listTexConfigs());
				pick:Set(listTexConfigs()[1]);
				notify("Texture Injector", "Deleted: " .. n, 2);
			else
				notify("Texture Injector", "Nothing to delete", 2);
			end
		end);
		ti.Button("Refresh List", function()
			pick:SetOptions(listTexConfigs());
		end);
	end
	do
		local COLS, GAP, CELL_H = 4, 8, 126;
		local KINDS = {{kind="Skin",tab="Skins",desc="Pick a weapon, then click a skin to equip it (click again to unequip)."},{kind="Wrap",tab="Wraps",desc="Pick a weapon, then click a wrap to apply it (click again to remove)."},{kind="Charm",tab="Charms",desc="Pick a weapon, then click a charm to attach it (click again to remove)."},{kind="Finisher",tab="Finishers",desc="Pick a weapon, then click a finisher (plays when you finish with that weapon)."}};
		local saved = CONFIG_VALUES["Cosmetics.Equipped"];
		if (type(saved) ~= "table") then
			saved = {};
		end
		CONFIG_VALUES["Cosmetics.Equipped"] = saved;
		local invertAll = CONFIG_VALUES["Cosmetics.Wraps.Options.Invert Patterns"] == true;
		local allWeapons = (Cosmetic.Ready and Cosmetic.Weapons()) or {};
		local function viewportOf(holderF, model)
			local vp = make("ViewportFrame", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Size=UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),ZIndex=5,Ambient=Color3.fromRGB(190, 190, 200),LightColor=Color3.new(1, 1, 1)}, holderF);
			local ok = pcall(function()
				if model:IsA("BasePart") then
					model.Anchored = true;
				end
				for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
					if d:IsA("BasePart") then
						d.Anchored = true;
					end
				end
				model.Parent = vp;
				local cf, size;
				if model:IsA("Model") then
					cf, size = model:GetBoundingBox();
				else
					cf, size = model.CFrame, model.Size;
				end
				local cam = Instance.new("Camera");
				cam.FieldOfView = 35;
				local dist = math.max(size.Magnitude, 0.5) * 1.25;
				local dir = ((cf.RightVector * 1) + (cf.UpVector * 0.25) + (cf.LookVector * 0.35)).Unit;
				cam.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(cf.Position + (dir * dist), cf.Position);
				cam.Parent = vp;
				vp.CurrentCamera = cam;
			end);
			if not ok then
				vp:Destroy();
				pcall(function()
					model:Destroy();
				end);
			end
			return ok;
		end
		local function cloneModel(src)
			local model;
			pcall(function()
				local arch = {};
				local function unlock(i)
					if not i.Archivable then
						arch[#arch + 1] = i;
						i.Archivable = true;
					end
				end
				unlock(src);
				for _, d in ipairs(src:GetDescendants()) do
					unlock(d);
				end
				if src:IsA("Folder") then
					model = Instance.new("Model");
					for _, ch in ipairs(src:GetChildren()) do
						local cl = ch:Clone();
						if cl then
							cl.Parent = model;
						end
					end
				else
					model = src:Clone();
				end
				for _, i in ipairs(arch) do
					i.Archivable = false;
				end
			end);
			return model;
		end
		local function drawIcon(btn, kind, name, weapon)
			local holderF = make("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Position=UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8),Size=UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -40),ZIndex=4}, btn);
			local id = Cosmetic.IconId(name);
			if id then
				make("ImageLabel", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Size=UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),Image=id,ScaleType=Enum.ScaleType.Fit,ZIndex=5}, holderF);
				return;
			end
			local src, wrapProps;
			if (kind == "Skin") then
				src = Cosmetic.PreviewSource(name);
			elseif (kind == "Charm") then
				src = Cosmetic.CharmModel(name);
			elseif (kind == "Wrap") then
				src = Cosmetic.WeaponModel(weapon);
			end
			local model = src and cloneModel(src);
			if (model and (kind == "Wrap")) then
				local ok = pcall(function()
					local ctrl = Cosmetic.WrapController();
					local props = ctrl:RecordOriginalWrapProperties(model);
					ctrl:ApplyWrap(props, {Name=name});
				end);
				if not ok then
					pcall(function()
						model:Destroy();
					end);
					model = nil;
				end
			end
			if (model and viewportOf(holderF, model)) then
				return;
			end
			make("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Size=UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),Text=string.upper(string.sub(name, 1, 2)),FontFace=F_HEAVY,TextSize=34,TextColor3=T.Dim,ZIndex=5}, holderF);
		end
		local function weaponPicker(sub, onPick)
			local dd = sub.Dropdown("Weapon", allWeapons, allWeapons[1], onPick);
			local f = dd.Frame;
			local box = make("TextBox", {BackgroundColor3=T.PanelHi,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(1, 0),Position=UDim2.new(1, -134, 0, 5),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(150, 24),Text="",PlaceholderText="search weapon...",PlaceholderColor3=T.Dim,FontFace=F_BOLD,TextSize=17,TextColor3=T.Text,ClearTextOnFocus=false,ZIndex=6}, f);
			corner(box, 6);
			stroke(box, T.Border, 1, 0.4);
			box:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
				local q = string.lower(box.Text);
				local res = {};
				for _, w in ipairs(allWeapons) do
					if ((q == "") or string.find(string.lower(w), q, 1, true)) then
						res[#res + 1] = w;
					end
				end
				dd:SetOptions(res);
				if ((q ~= "") and (#res > 0)) then
					dd.Open(true);
				end
			end);
			return dd;
		end
		local repaint = {};
		local function randomizeKind(kind)
			if not Cosmetic.Ready then
				return 0;
			end
			saved[kind] = ((type(saved[kind]) == "table") and saved[kind]) or {};
			local store, n = saved[kind], 0;
			local global = ((kind ~= "Skin") and Cosmetic.NamesAllowed(kind)) or nil;
			for _, w in ipairs(allWeapons) do
				local pool = global or Cosmetic.SkinsFor(w);
				if (#pool > 0) then
					local pick = pool[math.random(#pool)];
					if Cosmetic.Set(w, kind, pick, ((kind == "Wrap") and invertAll) or nil) then
						store[w] = pick;
						n += 1
					end
				end
			end
			if repaint[kind] then
				pcall(repaint[kind]);
			end
			return n;
		end
		local function randomizeAll()
			local total = 0;
			for _, K in ipairs(KINDS) do
				total += randomizeKind(K.kind)
			end
			notify("Cosmetics", "Randomised " .. total .. " cosmetics across all weapons", 3);
		end
		local function clearAll()
			for _, K in ipairs(KINDS) do
				for w in pairs(saved[K.kind] or {}) do
					Cosmetic.Set(w, K.kind, nil);
				end
				saved[K.kind] = {};
				if repaint[K.kind] then
					pcall(repaint[K.kind]);
				end
			end
			notify("Cosmetics", "All cosmetics reset to default", 2);
		end
		for _, K in ipairs(KINDS) do
			local kind = K.kind;
			local sub = addSub(mCosm, K.tab, K.desc);
			sub.Section("Randomiser");
			if (K.kind == "Skin") then
				sub.Button("Randomise ALL Cosmetics", randomizeAll);
				sub.Button("Reset ALL Cosmetics", clearAll);
			end
			sub.Button("Randomise All " .. K.tab, function()
				notify(K.tab, "Randomised " .. randomizeKind(kind) .. " weapons", 2);
			end);
			sub.Section("Weapons");
			saved[kind] = ((type(saved[kind]) == "table") and saved[kind]) or {};
			local store = saved[kind];
			local grid = make("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Size=UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,LayoutOrder=100000,ZIndex=3}, sub.Page);
			make("UIGridLayout", {CellSize=UDim2.new(1 / COLS, -((GAP * (COLS - 1)) / COLS), 0, CELL_H),CellPadding=UDim2.fromOffset(GAP, GAP),SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,HorizontalAlignment=Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left}, grid);
			make("UIPadding", {PaddingTop=UDim.new(0, 4)}, grid);
			local cards, buildToken, currentWeapon = {}, 0, nil;
			local function paintCard(c)
				local on = store[currentWeapon] == c.name;
				tween(c.stroke, 0.15, {Color=((on and T.Accent) or T.Border),Transparency=((on and 0) or 0.4),Thickness=((on and 2) or 1)});
				tween(c.btn, 0.15, {BackgroundColor3=((on and T.PanelHi) or T.Element)});
				c.tag.Visible = on;
			end
			local function paintAll()
				for _, c in ipairs(cards) do
					paintCard(c);
				end
			end
			local showWeaponNow;
			local function drawQueue(list, my, weapon)
				task.spawn(function()
					local n = 0;
					for _, card in ipairs(list) do
						if (buildToken ~= my) then
							return;
						end
						if card.btn.Parent then
							pcall(drawIcon, card.btn, kind, card.name, weapon);
						end
						n += 1
						if ((n % 6) == 0) then
							task.wait();
						end
					end
				end);
			end
			function showWeaponNow(weapon)
				buildToken += 1
				local my = buildToken;
				currentWeapon = weapon;
				for _, c in ipairs(cards) do
					c.btn:Destroy();
				end
				cards = {};
				if not Cosmetic.Ready then
					return;
				end
				local list = ((kind == "Skin") and Cosmetic.SkinsFor(weapon)) or Cosmetic.NamesAllowed(kind);
				for i, name in ipairs(list) do
					local btn = make("TextButton", {BackgroundColor3=T.Element,Text="",AutoButtonColor=false,LayoutOrder=i,ZIndex=3,ClipsDescendants=true}, grid);
					corner(btn, 18);
					local st = stroke(btn, T.Border, 1, 0.4);
					make("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency=1,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0, 1),Position=UDim2.new(0, 6, 1, -4),Size=UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 30),Text=name,FontFace=F_HEAVY,TextSize=17,TextColor3=T.Text,TextWrapped=true,TextTruncate=Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,ZIndex=6}, btn);
					local tag = make("Frame", {BackgroundColor3=T.Accent,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(1, 0),Position=UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 8),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(10, 10),BorderSizePixel=0,Visible=false,ZIndex=7}, btn);
					corner(tag, 5);
					onAccent(function()
						tag.BackgroundColor3 = T.Accent;
					end);
					local card = {btn=btn,stroke=st,tag=tag,name=name};
					cards[#cards + 1] = card;
					btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
						hoverSfx();
						if (store[currentWeapon] ~= name) then
							tween(btn, 0.15, {BackgroundColor3=T.ElementHi});
						end
					end);
					btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
						paintCard(card);
					end);
					btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
						clickSfx();
						local w = currentWeapon;
						local inv = ((kind == "Wrap") and invertAll) or nil;
						if (store[w] == name) then
							store[w] = nil;
							Cosmetic.Set(w, kind, nil);
							notify(K.tab, w .. ": default", 2);
						elseif Cosmetic.Set(w, kind, name, inv) then
							store[w] = name;
							notify(K.tab, w .. ": " .. name, 2);
						else
							notify(K.tab, "Could not equip " .. name, 2);
						end
						paintAll();
					end);
					local on = store[currentWeapon] == name;
					st.Color = (on and T.Accent) or T.Border;
					st.Transparency = (on and 0) or 0.4;
					st.Thickness = (on and 2) or 1;
					btn.BackgroundColor3 = (on and T.PanelHi) or T.Element;
					tag.Visible = on;
				end
				if (#list == 0) then
					local lbl = make("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Size=UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30),Text=("Nothing found for " .. weapon),FontFace=F_BOLD,TextSize=18,TextColor3=T.Sub,ZIndex=4}, grid);
					cards[#cards + 1] = {btn=lbl,stroke=Instance.new("UIStroke"),tag=Instance.new("Frame"),name=""};
				else
					drawQueue(cards, my, weapon);
				end
			end
			local pendingWeapon;
			local function showWeapon(weapon)
				if sub.Page.Visible then
					pendingWeapon = nil;
					showWeaponNow(weapon);
				else
					pendingWeapon = weapon;
				end
			end
			sub.Page:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function()
				if (sub.Page.Visible and pendingWeapon) then
					local w = pendingWeapon;
					pendingWeapon = nil;
					task.defer(showWeaponNow, w);
				end
			end);
			repaint[kind] = function()
				paintAll();
			end;
			if Cosmetic.Ready then
				for w, n in pairs(store) do
					if not Cosmetic.Set(w, kind, n, ((kind == "Wrap") and invertAll) or nil) then
						store[w] = nil;
					end
				end
			end
			if (#allWeapons == 0) then
				sub.Label("Cosmetic library not found - open the menu in a match lobby and reload.");
			else
				weaponPicker(sub, function(w)
					if w then
						showWeapon(w);
					end
				end);
			end
			if (kind == "Wrap") then
				sub.Section("Options");
				sub.Toggle("Invert Patterns", invertAll, function(on)
					invertAll = on;
					for w in pairs(store) do
						Cosmetic.SetInverted(w, on);
					end
				end);
			end
		end
	end
	local userScale = 1;
	local fit;
	local function listProfiles()
		local out = {};
		if (type(listfiles) == "function") then
			pcall(function()
				for _, f in ipairs(listfiles(PROFILES)) do
					local n = tostring(f):match("([^/\\]+)%.json$");
					if n then
						table.insert(out, n);
					end
				end
			end);
		end
		if (#out == 0) then
			out = {"None"};
		end
		table.sort(out);
		return out;
	end
	do
		local cp = addSub(mCfg, "Config Profiles", "Save, load and manage your settings.");
		cp.Section("Profiles");
		local nameBox = cp.TextBox("Profile Name", "my profile");
		local pick = cp.Dropdown("Profile", listProfiles(), "None");
		local function refresh()
			pick:SetOptions(listProfiles());
		end
		cp.Section("Actions");
		cp.Button("Save Profile", function()
			local n = (nameBox.Get() or ""):gsub("[^%w _%-]", "");
			if (n == "") then
				notify("Profiles", "Type a profile name first", 2, "danger");
				return;
			end
			if (type(writefile) == "function") then
				pcall(function()
					writefile(PROFILES .. "/" .. n .. ".json", encode());
				end);
				refresh();
				pick:Set(n);
				notify("Profiles", "Saved profile: " .. n, 2, "ok");
			else
				notify("Profiles", "Executor has no writefile", 2, "danger");
			end
		end);
		cp.Button("Load Profile", function()
			local n = pick.Get();
			local path = PROFILES .. "/" .. tostring(n) .. ".json";
			if ((type(isfile) == "function") and isfile(path)) then
				local ok, d = pcall(function()
					return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(path));
				end);
				if (ok and (type(d) == "table")) then
					applyTable(d);
					notify("Profiles", "Loaded: " .. n, 2, "ok");
					return;
				end
			end
			notify("Profiles", "Could not load profile", 2, "danger");
		end);
		cp.Button("Delete Profile", function()
			local n = pick.Get();
			local path = PROFILES .. "/" .. tostring(n) .. ".json";
			if ((type(delfile) == "function") and (type(isfile) == "function") and isfile(path)) then
				pcall(delfile, path);
				refresh();
				notify("Profiles", "Deleted: " .. n, 2);
			else
				notify("Profiles", "Nothing to delete", 2, "danger");
			end
		end);
		cp.Button("Refresh List", refresh);
		cp.Section("Auto Load");
		cp.Toggle("Auto Load On Startup", true);
		cp.Button("Set Selected Profile As Autoload", function()
			local n = pick.Get();
			if (not n or (n == "None")) then
				notify("Autoload", "Pick a saved profile first", 2, "danger");
				return;
			end
			if (type(writefile) == "function") then
				pcall(function()
					writefile(ROOT .. "/autoload.json", HttpService:JSONEncode({profile=n}));
				end);
				notify("Autoload", n .. " will load on startup", 2.5, "ok");
			else
				notify("Autoload", "Executor has no writefile", 2, "danger");
			end
		end);
		cp.Button("Clear Autoload Profile", function()
			if ((type(delfile) == "function") and (type(isfile) == "function") and isfile(ROOT .. "/autoload.json")) then
				pcall(delfile, ROOT .. "/autoload.json");
				notify("Autoload", "Cleared (main config still loads)", 2.5);
			else
				notify("Autoload", "No autoload set", 2, "danger");
			end
		end);
		cp.Section("Main Config");
		cp.Hint("Your live settings. Delete only removes the saved file - what you see now stays until you reload.");
		cp.Button("Save Main Config", function()
			local ok = saveConfig();
			notify("Config", (ok and "Saved") or "Save failed", 2, (ok and "ok") or "danger");
		end);
		cp.Button("Load Main Config", function()
			local ok = loadConfig();
			notify("Config", (ok and "Loaded and applied") or "No config found", 2, (ok and "ok") or "danger");
		end);
		cp.Button("Delete Main Config", function()
			if ((type(delfile) == "function") and (type(isfile) == "function") and isfile(CONFIG)) then
				pcall(delfile, CONFIG);
				notify("Config", "Main config file deleted", 2.5);
			else
				notify("Config", "No main config to delete", 2, "danger");
			end
		end);
		cp.Toggle("Auto Save (30s)", true);
		cp.Section("Danger Zone");
		cp.Hint("Turn Everything Off disables every active feature (interface settings stay). Reset puts every feature back to its default. Settings, profiles and cosmetics are kept.");
		cp.Danger("Turn Everything Off", function()
			local count = 0;
			for key in pairs(toggleKeys) do
				local isUI = key:find("^Settings%.") or key:find("^Configs%.");
				if (not isUI and (CONFIG_VALUES[key] == true) and registry[key]) then
					pcall(registry[key], false);
					count += 1
				end
			end
			markDirty();
			notify("Config", ((count > 0) and ("Turned off " .. count .. " features")) or "Everything is already off", 3, "danger");
		end);
		cp.Danger("Reset Config", function()
			local keep = function(k)
				return (type(k) == "string") and (k:find("^Settings%.") or k:find("^Configs%.") or k:find("^Cosmetic"));
			end;
			local count = 0;
			for key, fn in pairs(resetters) do
				if not keep(key) then
					pcall(fn);
					count += 1
				end
			end
			for key in pairs(CONFIG_VALUES) do
				if (not keep(key) and not resetters[key]) then
					CONFIG_VALUES[key] = nil;
				end
			end
			saveConfig();
			notify("Config", "Reset " .. count .. " settings to default", 3, "danger");
		end);
		gui.Destroying:Connect(function()
			saveConfig();
		end);
		task.spawn(function()
			while gui.Parent do
				task.wait(30);
				if flag("Configs.Config Profiles.Main Config.Auto Save (30s)") then
					saveConfig();
				end
			end
		end);
	end
	do
		local ge = addSub(mSet, "General", "Interface, binds, startup, and notifications.");
		build(ge, {{"sec","Interface"},{"t","Sound FX",true},{"t","Background Blur",true,function(v)
			blurOn = v;
			refreshBlur();
		end},{"s","UI Scale %",60,110,100,1,function(v)
			userScale = v / 100;
			if fit then
				fit();
			end
		end},{"sec","Binds"},{"k","Toggle Hub","RightShift"},{"sec","Startup"},{"t","Show Welcome Notification",true},{"t","Open Menu On Load",true},{"sec","Notifications"},{"t","Enable Notifications",true},{"sec","Danger Zone"},{"dg","Unload 1Mag.ez",function()
			gui:Destroy();
		end}});
		local W = Color3.new(1, 1, 1);
		local function mk(acc, bg, txt, hi)
			return {Accent=acc,AccentHi=(hi or acc:Lerp(W, 0.28)),Bg=bg,Panel=bg:Lerp(W, 0.03):Lerp(acc, 0.03),PanelHi=bg:Lerp(W, 0.06):Lerp(acc, 0.04),Element=bg:Lerp(W, 0.08):Lerp(acc, 0.05),ElementHi=bg:Lerp(W, 0.14):Lerp(acc, 0.08),Border=bg:Lerp(W, 0.12):Lerp(acc, 0.22),Text=txt,Sub=txt:Lerp(bg, 0.35),Dim=txt:Lerp(bg, 0.62)};
		end
		local C = Color3.fromRGB;
		local TXT = C(244, 244, 248);
		local THEMES = {Crimson={C(196, 26, 30),C(10, 10, 13),C(255, 110, 90),TXT},Synthwave={C(255, 46, 151),C(14, 8, 28),C(0, 229, 255),C(250, 236, 255)},Midnight={C(88, 101, 242),C(8, 10, 22),C(140, 200, 255),C(232, 236, 255)},Sakura={C(255, 143, 177),C(20, 12, 16),C(255, 214, 230),C(255, 240, 245)},Mocha={C(203, 166, 247),C(17, 17, 27),C(245, 194, 231),C(205, 214, 244)},Dracula={C(189, 147, 249),C(24, 25, 33),C(255, 121, 198),C(248, 248, 242)},["Tokyo Night"]={C(122, 162, 247),C(20, 21, 30),C(187, 154, 247),C(192, 202, 245)},["Rose Pine"]={C(235, 111, 146),C(22, 20, 33),C(246, 193, 119),C(224, 222, 244)},Nord={C(136, 192, 208),C(28, 32, 41),C(163, 190, 140),C(229, 233, 240)},Gruvbox={C(254, 128, 25),C(27, 29, 30),C(250, 189, 47),C(235, 219, 178)},Aurora={C(0, 255, 170),C(6, 14, 18),C(120, 120, 255),C(230, 255, 248)},Inferno={C(255, 69, 0),C(14, 7, 6),C(255, 200, 0),C(255, 240, 225)},Cyberpunk={C(252, 238, 10),C(10, 10, 14),C(255, 0, 60),C(250, 250, 232)},Ocean={C(30, 140, 255),C(7, 12, 20),C(0, 235, 220),TXT},Matrix={C(0, 220, 90),C(5, 12, 8),C(190, 255, 120),C(225, 255, 235)},Monochrome={C(210, 210, 218),C(9, 9, 10),C(255, 255, 255),TXT}};
		local ORDER = {"Crimson","Synthwave","Midnight","Sakura","Mocha","Dracula","Tokyo Night","Rose Pine","Nord","Gruvbox","Aurora","Inferno","Cyberpunk","Ocean","Matrix","Monochrome","Custom"};
		local TKEYS = {"Accent","AccentHi","Bg","Panel","PanelHi","Element","ElementHi","Border","Text","Sub","Dim"};
		local function hx(c)
			return (math.floor((c.R * 255) + 0.5) * 65536) + (math.floor((c.G * 255) + 0.5) * 256) + math.floor((c.B * 255) + 0.5);
		end
		local function applyTheme(new)
			local same = true;
			for _, k in ipairs(TKEYS) do
				if (T[k] ~= new[k]) then
					same = false;
					break;
				end
			end
			if same then
				return;
			end
			local map = {};
			for _, k in ipairs(TKEYS) do
				local h = hx(T[k]);
				if (map[h] == nil) then
					map[h] = new[k];
				end
			end
			for _, k in ipairs(TKEYS) do
				T[k] = new[k];
			end
			local function m(c)
				local n = map[hx(c)];
				return n;
			end
			for _, o in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
				pcall(function()
					if o:IsA("GuiObject") then
						local n = m(o.BackgroundColor3);
						if n then
							o.BackgroundColor3 = n;
						end
						if (o:IsA("TextLabel") or o:IsA("TextButton") or o:IsA("TextBox")) then
							n = m(o.TextColor3);
							if n then
								o.TextColor3 = n;
							end
							if o:IsA("TextBox") then
								n = m(o.PlaceholderColor3);
								if n then
									o.PlaceholderColor3 = n;
								end
							end
						elseif (o:IsA("ImageLabel") or o:IsA("ImageButton")) then
							n = m(o.ImageColor3);
							if n then
								o.ImageColor3 = n;
							end
						elseif o:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
							n = m(o.ScrollBarImageColor3);
							if n then
								o.ScrollBarImageColor3 = n;
							end
						end
					elseif o:IsA("UIStroke") then
						local n = m(o.Color);
						if n then
							o.Color = n;
						end
					elseif (o:IsA("UIGradient") and (o.Name ~= "Shimmer") and (o.Name ~= "NoTheme")) then
						local ks, ch = {}, false;
						for i, kp in ipairs(o.Color.Keypoints) do
							local n = m(kp.Value);
							if n then
								ch = true;
							end
							ks[i] = ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(kp.Time, n or kp.Value);
						end
						if ch then
							o.Color = ColorSequence.new(ks);
						end
					end
				end);
			end
			for _, f in ipairs(accentHooks) do
				pcall(f);
			end
		end
		local curPreset = "Crimson";
		local cust = {AR=196,AG=26,AB=30,BR=10,BG=10,BB=13,TR=244,TG=244,TB=248};
		local function compute()
			if (curPreset == "Custom") then
				return mk(C(cust.AR, cust.AG, cust.AB), C(cust.BR, cust.BG, cust.BB), C(cust.TR, cust.TG, cust.TB), cust.hi);
			end
			local p = THEMES[curPreset] or THEMES.Crimson;
			return mk(p[1], p[2], p[4] or TXT, p[3]);
		end
		local pending = false;
		local function queue()
			if pending then
				return;
			end
			pending = true;
			task.delay(0.05, function()
				pending = false;
				applyTheme(compute());
			end);
		end
		local function wheelTo(kr, kg, kb, vMin, vMax)
			return function(hexv)
				local ok, c = pcall(Color3.fromHex, hexv);
				if not ok then
					return;
				end
				local hh, ss, vv = c:ToHSV();
				c = Color3.fromHSV(hh, ss, math.clamp(vv, vMin, vMax));
				if (kr == "AR") then
					cust.hi = nil;
				end
				cust[kr], cust[kg], cust[kb] = math.floor((c.R * 255) + 0.5), math.floor((c.G * 255) + 0.5), math.floor((c.B * 255) + 0.5);
				if (curPreset == "Custom") then
					queue();
				end
			end;
		end
		local th = addSub(mSet, "Theme", "Pick a preset or build your own.");
		build(th, {{"sec","Presets"},{"d","Preset",ORDER,"Crimson",function(v)
			curPreset = v;
			queue();
		end},{"b","Random Theme",function()
			local h = math.random();
			cust.AR, cust.AG, cust.AB = math.floor(Color3.fromHSV(h, 0.8, 1).R * 255), math.floor(Color3.fromHSV(h, 0.8, 1).G * 255), math.floor(Color3.fromHSV(h, 0.8, 1).B * 255);
			cust.hi = Color3.fromHSV((h + 0.12) % 1, 0.65, 1);
			local b = Color3.fromHSV(h, 0.55, 0.09);
			cust.BR, cust.BG, cust.BB = math.floor(b.R * 255), math.floor(b.G * 255), math.floor(b.B * 255);
			curPreset = "Custom";
			queue();
		end},{"sec","Custom Accent"},{"cw","Accent Colour","#C41A1E",wheelTo("AR", "AG", "AB", 0, 1)},{"sec","Custom Background"},{"cw","Background Colour","#0A0A0D",wheelTo("BR", "BG", "BB", 0, 0.27)},{"sec","Custom Text"},{"cw","Text Colour","#F4F4F8",wheelTo("TR", "TG", "TB", 0.47, 1)},{"b","Apply Custom Theme",function()
			curPreset = "Custom";
			queue();
		end},{"sec","Hub Flame"},{"t","Hub Flame",true,function(v)
			FX.Flame.on = v;
		end},{"s","Flame Intensity",8,100,FX.Flame.n,2,function(v)
			FX.Flame.n = v;
		end}});
		th.Section("Live Preview");
		th.Slider("Window Transparency", 0, 60, 0, function(v)
			win.BackgroundTransparency = v / 100;
		end, 1);
		local ms = addSub(mSet, "Mobile", "On-screen buttons for touch devices.");
		build(ms, {{"sec","On-Screen Buttons"},{"t","Show Buttons",true,function(v)
			FX.MB.show = v;
		end},{"t","Lock Button Positions",false,function(v)
			FX.MB.locked = v;
		end},{"s","Button Size",44,110,64,2,function(v)
			FX.MB.size = v;
		end},{"b","Reset Button Positions",function()
			FX.MB.reset();
		end},{"l","Choose a mode in each feature's tab. Drag a button to move it."},{"l","The hub button (logo) can be dragged too."}});
		local hs = addSub(mSet, "HUD", "Watermark and array list overlay.");
		hs.Section("Overlay");
		hs.Toggle("Show HUD (above all UIs)", true, function(v)
			HUD.on = v;
			HUD.apply();
		end);
		hs.Section("Watermark");
		hs.Toggle("Show Watermark", true, function(v)
			HUD.wm = v;
			HUD.apply();
		end);
		hs.Dropdown("Watermark Position", {"Top Left","Top Right","Bottom Left","Bottom Right"}, "Top Left", function(v)
			HUD.wmPos = v;
			HUD.apply();
		end);
		hs.Toggle("Show Name", true, function(v)
			HUD.name = v;
			HUD.apply();
		end);
		hs.Toggle("Show FPS", true, function(v)
			HUD.fps = v;
			HUD.apply();
		end);
		hs.Toggle("Show Ping", true, function(v)
			HUD.ping = v;
			HUD.apply();
		end);
		hs.Section("Array List");
		hs.Toggle("Show Array List", true, function(v)
			HUD.arr = v;
			HUD.apply();
		end);
		hs.Dropdown("Array Side", {"Right","Left"}, "Right", function(v)
			HUD.side = v;
			HUD.apply();
		end);
		hs.Toggle("Rainbow Colours", false, function(v)
			HUD.rainbow = v;
		end);
	end
	function fit()
		local vp = gui.AbsoluteSize;
		local baseW = math.max(1, holder.Size.X.Offset);
		local baseH = math.max(1, holder.Size.Y.Offset);
		local s = math.min((vp.X - (2 * (SQ + 26))) / baseW, (vp.Y - 30) / baseH, 1);
		holderScale.Scale = math.max(0.35, s) * userScale;
	end
	track(gui:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(fit));
	fit();
	do
		local dragging, startMouse, startPos;
		top.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
			if isPress(i) then
				dragging = true;
				startMouse, startPos = i.Position, holder.Position;
				i.Changed:Connect(function()
					if (i.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End) then
						dragging = false;
						saveWindowState();
					end
				end);
			end
		end);
		track(UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
			if (dragging and isMove(i)) then
				local d = i.Position - startMouse;
				holder.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y);
			end
		end));
	end
	local resizeGrip = make("TextButton", {Name="ResizeGrip",BackgroundColor3=T.Accent,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(1, 1),Position=UDim2.new(1, -3, 1, -3),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18),Text="◢",FontFace=F_HEAVY,TextSize=14,TextColor3=T.Text,AutoButtonColor=false,ZIndex=30}, win);
	corner(resizeGrip, 3);
	onAccent(function()
		resizeGrip.BackgroundColor3 = T.Accent;
	end);
	do
		local resizing, startMouse, startSize = false, nil, nil;
		resizeGrip.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
			if isPress(i) then
				resizing = true;
				startMouse = i.Position;
				startSize = holder.Size;
				i.Changed:Connect(function()
					if (i.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End) then
						resizing = false;
						saveWindowState();
						fit();
					end
				end);
			end
		end);
		track(UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
			if (resizing and isMove(i)) then
				local d = i.Position - startMouse;
				holder.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.clamp(startSize.X.Offset + d.X, 620, 1100), math.clamp(startSize.Y.Offset + d.Y, 380, 800));
				glow.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(holder.Size.X.Offset + 14, holder.Size.Y.Offset + 14);
				shadow.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(holder.Size.X.Offset + 60, holder.Size.Y.Offset + 60);
			end
		end));
	end
	local toggle = make("TextButton", {Name="Squircle",BackgroundColor3=T.Panel,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0, 0.5),Position=UDim2.new(0, 14, 0.5, 0),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(SQ, SQ),Text="",AutoButtonColor=false,ZIndex=600}, gui);
	make("UICorner", {CornerRadius=UDim.new(0, 12)}, toggle);
	local tStroke = stroke(toggle, T.Accent, 1.5, 0);
	local tRing = make("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency=1,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),Position=UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(SQ, SQ),BorderSizePixel=0,ZIndex=599}, toggle);
	make("UICorner", {CornerRadius=UDim.new(0, 12)}, tRing);
	local ringStroke = stroke(tRing, T.Accent, 2, 0.4);
	onAccent(function()
		tStroke.Color = T.Accent;
		ringStroke.Color = T.Accent;
	end);
	if LOGO_ID then
		logoImg({BackgroundTransparency=1,Size=UDim2.fromScale(0.74, 0.74),AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),Position=UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),ScaleType=Enum.ScaleType.Fit,ZIndex=601}, toggle);
	else
		make("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency=1,Size=UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),Text="1M",FontFace=F_HEAVY,TextSize=17,TextColor3=T.AccentHi,ZIndex=601}, toggle);
	end
	task.spawn(function()
		while toggle.Parent do
			tRing.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(SQ, SQ);
			ringStroke.Transparency = 0.4;
			local tw = tween(tRing, 1.6, {Size=UDim2.fromOffset(SQ + 16, SQ + 16)}, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine);
			tween(ringStroke, 1.6, {Transparency=1}, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine);
			tw.Completed:Wait();
			task.wait(0.1);
		end
	end);
	toggle.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		hoverSfx();
		tween(toggle, 0.2, {BackgroundColor3=T.ElementHi});
	end);
	toggle.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		tween(toggle, 0.2, {BackgroundColor3=T.Panel});
	end);
	FX.sq = {id="hub",px=(14 / math.max(gui.AbsoluteSize.X, 1)),py=0.5};
	FX.MB.attach(toggle, FX.sq);
	local isOpen, token = false, 0;
	function refreshBlur()
		local on = isOpen and blurOn;
		tween(blurFx, 0.35, {Size=((on and 18) or 0)});
		tween(FX.dim, 0.35, {BackgroundTransparency=((on and 0.6) or 1)});
	end
	local function setOpen(v)
		if (isOpen == v) then
			return;
		end
		isOpen = v;
		token += 1
		refreshBlur();
		local my = token;
		if v then
			win.Visible = true;
			shadow.Visible = true;
			sweep.Visible = true;
			sweep.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-120, 0);
			tween(sweep, 0.75, {Position=UDim2.fromOffset(holder.Size.X.Offset + 20, 0)}, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad).Completed:Connect(function()
				sweep.Visible = false;
			end);
			tween(winScale, 0.4, {Scale=1}, Enum.EasingStyle.Back);
			tween(win, 0.3, {GroupTransparency=0});
			tween(shadow, 0.4, {ImageTransparency=0.55});
		else
			tween(winScale, 0.25, {Scale=0.88}, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In);
			tween(shadow, 0.25, {ImageTransparency=1});
			tween(win, 0.25, {GroupTransparency=1}).Completed:Connect(function()
				if ((token == my) and not isOpen) then
					win.Visible = false;
					shadow.Visible = false;
				end
			end);
		end
	end
	toggle.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		if FX.sq.wasDragged then
			return;
		end
		clickSfx();
		setOpen(not isOpen);
	end);
	closeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		setOpen(false);
	end);
	track(UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(i, gp)
		if gp then
			return;
		end
		if ((i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard) and (i.KeyCode.Name == (flag("Settings.General.Binds.Toggle Hub") or "RightShift"))) then
			setOpen(not isOpen);
		end
	end));
	gui.Destroying:Connect(function()
		pcall(saveWindowState);
		for _, c in ipairs(conns) do
			pcall(function()
				c:Disconnect();
			end);
		end
		pcall(function()
			blurFx:Destroy();
		end);
		pcall(Cosmetic.Unload);
		local ld = PG:FindFirstChild("1MagLoading");
		if ld then
			ld:Destroy();
		end
	end);
	selectMain(mHome);
	badgeRefresh();
	if gui.Parent then
		setOpen(true);
		notify("1Mag.ez", "Loaded - welcome " .. player.DisplayName, 1);
	end
	do
		if not gui.Parent then
			return;
		end
		LOADING = true;
		pcall(function()
			if (type(delfile) == "function") then
				if isfile(ROOT .. "/applying.txt") then
					delfile(ROOT .. "/applying.txt");
				end
				if isfile(ROOT .. "/badkeys.json") then
					delfile(ROOT .. "/badkeys.json");
				end
			end
		end);
		local LAST = {["Combat.Aim Assist.Aim Assist.Aim Assist"]=true,["Combat.Silent Aim.Silent Aim.Silent Aim"]=true,["Combat.Silent Aim.Legit.Legit Aim Assist"]=true};
		local late, n = {}, 0;
		for k, v in pairs(SAVED) do
			if not gui.Parent then
				break;
			end
			if LAST[k] then
				late[#late + 1] = {k,v};
			elseif (CONFIG_VALUES[k] ~= v) then
				CONFIG_VALUES[k] = v;
				if registry[k] then
					pcall(registry[k], v);
				end
				n += 1
			end
		end
		for _, kv in ipairs(late) do
			if not gui.Parent then
				break;
			end
			if (CONFIG_VALUES[kv[1]] ~= kv[2]) then
				CONFIG_VALUES[kv[1]] = kv[2];
				if registry[kv[1]] then
					pcall(registry[kv[1]], kv[2]);
				end
			end
		end
		LOADING = false;
		if arrayRefresh then
			pcall(arrayRefresh);
		end
		SAVED = {};
		CFG_READY = true;
		pcall(clearDirty);
		pcall(badgeRefresh);
		if (gui.Parent and (flag("Settings.General.Startup.Open Menu On Load") == false)) then
			setOpen(false);
		end
	end
end
loadMain();
